- [zHeap: PostgreSQL with UNDO](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/zheap-postgresql-with-undo-db0a8db4c38f) - 2019-04-06 | Medium | Oracle Database, PostgreSQL | Internals & Storage, Cloud & Infrastructure, Operations & Observability; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2019-04-06_zHeap--PostgreSQL-with-UNDO-db0a8db4c38f.html](2018-medium/posts/2019-04-06_zHeap--PostgreSQL-with-UNDO-db0a8db4c38f.html)
  Evidence note: Compiles EnterpriseDB's experimental zHeap storage engine, which adds in-place updates powered by an UNDO log, and monitors it with the pg_active_session_history extension.
- [Adding JDBC driver property in SQL Developer connecting to MySQL](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/adding-jdbc-driver-property-in-sql-developer-connecting-to-mysql-387886a18086) - 2019-04-04 | Medium | Oracle Database, MySQL | Operations & Observability, Development & Drivers; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2019-04-04_Adding-JDBC-driver-property-in-SQL-Developer-connecting-to-MySQL-387886a18086.html](2018-medium/posts/2019-04-04_Adding-JDBC-driver-property-in-SQL-Developer-connecting-to-MySQL-387886a18086.html)
  Evidence note: Injects a serverTimezone parameter after the port field in SQL Developer's MySQL connection dialog, working around its lack of a dedicated JDBC property input field.
- [19c EZCONNECT and Wallet (Easy Connect and External Password File)](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/19c-ezconnect-and-wallet-easy-connect-and-external-password-file-8e326bb8c9f5) - 2019-04-04 | Medium | Oracle Database | General Database; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2019-04-04_19c-EZCONNECT-and-Wallet--Easy-Connect-and-External-Password-File--8e326bb8c9f5.html](2018-medium/posts/2019-04-04_19c-EZCONNECT-and-Wallet--Easy-Connect-and-External-Password-File--8e326bb8c9f5.html)
  Evidence note: Creates a wallet credential tied to a specific EZCONNECT service name so multiple users on the same service can each authenticate passwordless, which plain EZCONNECT could not do before.
- [High CPU usage in docker-proxy with chatty database application? Disable userland-proxy!](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/high-cpu-usage-in-docker-proxy-with-chatty-database-application-disable-userland-proxy-415ffa064955) - 2019-03-18 | Medium | PostgreSQL | Performance & Benchmarking, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2019-03-18_High-CPU-usage-in-docker-proxy-with-chatty-database-application--Disable-userland-proxy--415ffa064955.html](2018-medium/posts/2019-03-18_High-CPU-usage-in-docker-proxy-with-chatty-database-application--Disable-userland-proxy--415ffa064955.html)
  Evidence note: Traces high docker-proxy CPU usage in a chatty PostgreSQL and pgbench container setup back to Docker's default userland-proxy, resolved by disabling it or co-locating the containers.
- [SQL, PL/SQL and JavaScript running in the Database Server (Oracle MLE)](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/sql-pl-sql-and-javascript-running-in-the-database-server-oracle-mle-a7a102e1e5ce) - 2019-03-17 | Medium | Oracle Database | Transactions & Consistency, Data Modeling & SQL, Development & Drivers; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2019-03-17_SQL--PL-SQL-and-JavaScript-running-in-the-Database-Server--Oracle-MLE--a7a102e1e5ce.html](2018-medium/posts/2019-03-17_SQL--PL-SQL-and-JavaScript-running-in-the-Database-Server--Oracle-MLE--a7a102e1e5ce.html)
  Evidence note: Repeats a 400,000-row account-transfer benchmark from an earlier CPU-usage test using the newer Oracle Multilingual Engine 0.3.0 beta to run JavaScript stored procedures alongside SQL.
- [Oracle Multi-Lingual Engine](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/oracle-multi-lingual-engine-7ce6414aa6c4) - 2019-03-15 | Medium | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Cloud & Infrastructure, Development & Drivers; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2019-03-15_Oracle-Multi-Lingual-Engine-7ce6414aa6c4.html](2018-medium/posts/2019-03-15_Oracle-Multi-Lingual-Engine-7ce6414aa6c4.html)
  Evidence note: Downloads the Oracle 12.2 Multilingual Engine beta Docker image and SQLcl client on an Oracle Cloud VM to run a simple JavaScript stored procedure demo end to end.
- [Oracle stored procedure compilation errors displayed for humans](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/oracle-stored-procedure-compilation-errors-displayed-for-humans-ef798bcd096c) - 2019-03-14 | Medium | Oracle Database | Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2019-03-14_Oracle-stored-procedure-compilation-errors-displayed-for-humans-ef798bcd096c.html](2018-medium/posts/2019-03-14_Oracle-stored-procedure-compilation-errors-displayed-for-humans-ef798bcd096c.html)
  Evidence note: Provides a query joining DBA_ERRORS with DBA_DEPENDENCIES and DBA_SOURCE to filter out cascading invalid-object errors after a Data Pump import, isolating the true root cause.
- [SQL prevents database corruption and injection, except in the ridiculous movie’s hacker scenes.](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/sql-prevents-database-corruption-and-injection-except-in-the-ridiculous-movies-hacker-scenes-8b89479468b4) - 2019-03-10 | Medium | Microsoft SQL Server | Security, JSON & Documents; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2019-03-10_SQL-prevents-database-corruption-and-injection--except-in-the-ridiculous-movie-s-hacker-scenes--8b89479468b4.html](2018-medium/posts/2019-03-10_SQL-prevents-database-corruption-and-injection--except-in-the-ridiculous-movie-s-hacker-scenes--8b89479468b4.html)
  Evidence note: Argues that Hollywood's SQL-injection hacking scenes misrepresent SQL, which under Codd's rule five actually provides DDL, DML, and DCL controls to encapsulate and protect data access.
- [Oracle 19c Data Guard sandbox created by DBCA -createDuplicateDB](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/oracle-19c-data-guard-sandbox-created-by-dbca-createduplicatedb-88aab20ea0ab) - 2019-03-06 | Medium | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Internals & Storage, Cloud & Infrastructure, Operations & Observability; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2019-03-06_Oracle-19c-Data-Guard-sandbox-created-by-DBCA--createDuplicateDB-88aab20ea0ab.html](2018-medium/posts/2019-03-06_Oracle-19c-Data-Guard-sandbox-created-by-DBCA--createDuplicateDB-88aab20ea0ab.html)
  Evidence note: Provisions an Oracle Linux 7.6 Compute Instance with Huge Pages and an attached 8TB block device, then uses 19c DBCA's createDuplicateDB feature to build a Data Guard sandbox.
- [19c Observe-Only Data Guard FSFO: no split-brain risk in manual failover](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/19c-observe-only-data-guard-fsfo-no-split-brain-risk-in-manual-failover-60ebdaecec3e) - 2019-03-06 | Medium | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, High Availability & Replication, Backup & Recovery; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2019-03-06_19c-Observe-Only-Data-Guard-FSFO--no-split-brain-risk-in-manual-failover-60ebdaecec3e.html](2018-medium/posts/2019-03-06_19c-Observe-Only-Data-Guard-FSFO--no-split-brain-risk-in-manual-failover-60ebdaecec3e.html)
  Evidence note: Explains how Fast-Start Failover's observer process automatically triggers failover once the primary is unreachable, and how the ObserverOverride property changes that decision.
- [My next Conferences in 2019](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/my-next-conferences-in-2019-dd66d9558cdc) - 2019-03-05 | Medium | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Performance & Benchmarking, Internals & Storage, Operations & Observability, Development & Drivers, Events & Community; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2019-03-05_My-next-Conferences-in-2019-dd66d9558cdc.html](2018-medium/posts/2019-03-05_My-next-Conferences-in-2019-dd66d9558cdc.html)
  Evidence note: Lists 2019 talks on data and code co-location with the Oracle MLE engine, optimizer statistics best practices, and join-method internals traced through internal rowsource functions.
- [/proc/meminfo formatted for humans](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/proc-meminfo-formatted-for-humans-350c6bebc380) - 2019-02-27 | Medium | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure, Operations & Observability; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2019-02-27_-proc-meminfo-formatted-for-humans-350c6bebc380.html](2018-medium/posts/2019-02-27_-proc-meminfo-formatted-for-humans-350c6bebc380.html)
  Evidence note: Shares an awk one-liner that converts proc-meminfo's kilobyte values into GB, correctly handling HugePages_Total and HugePages_Free by multiplying with the reported Hugepagesize.
- [19c Auto Index: the dictionary views](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/19c-auto-index-the-dictionary-views-ea2927a9786) - 2019-02-25 | Medium | Oracle Database | Indexing, Operations & Observability, AI & Search; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2019-02-25_19c-Auto-Index--the-dictionary-views-ea2927a9786.html](2018-medium/posts/2019-02-25_19c-Auto-Index--the-dictionary-views-ea2927a9786.html)
  Evidence note: Surveys the DBA_AUTO_INDEX_CONFIG and related cataivw.sql views, backed by the SMB dollar CONFIG SQL Management Base table, exposing 19c Automatic Indexing configuration and activity.
- [19c Easy Connect](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/19c-easy-connect-e0c3b77968d7) - 2019-02-23 | Medium | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Operations & Observability, Development & Drivers; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2019-02-23_19c-Easy-Connect-e0c3b77968d7.html](2018-medium/posts/2019-02-23_19c-Easy-Connect-e0c3b77968d7.html)
  Evidence note: Uses tnsping to show how 19c's extended EZCONNECT syntax resolves multi-host, failover, and parameterized strings into a full DESCRIPTION, reducing but not removing tnsnames.ora.
- [19c DG Broker export/import configuration](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/19c-dg-broker-export-import-configuration-f4ce8a37bfe3) - 2019-02-20 | Medium | Oracle Database | Operations & Observability; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2019-02-20_19c-DG-Broker-export-import-configuration-f4ce8a37bfe3.html](2018-medium/posts/2019-02-20_19c-DG-Broker-export-import-configuration-f4ce8a37bfe3.html)
  Evidence note: Reads the previously binary-only broker configuration dat files with strings to show they already store XML internally, which 19c can now export and import as a portable file.
- [Oracle 19c Hint Usage reporting](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/oracle-19c-hint-usage-reporting-345563a461f0) - 2019-02-18 | Medium | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Indexing, Internals & Storage; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2019-02-18_Oracle-19c-Hint-Usage-reporting-345563a461f0.html](2018-medium/posts/2019-02-18_Oracle-19c-Hint-Usage-reporting-345563a461f0.html)
  Evidence note: Uses dbms_xplan's hint-report format to distinguish syntax-error hints from semantically unresolved ones, showing exactly which query block and alias each hint failed to apply to.
- [Network troubleshooting with tcpdump and strace](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/network-troubleshooting-with-tcpdump-and-strace-73e74dc727ee) - 2019-02-14 | Medium | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Backup & Recovery; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2019-02-14_Network-troubleshooting-with-tcpdump-and-strace-73e74dc727ee.html](2018-medium/posts/2019-02-14_Network-troubleshooting-with-tcpdump-and-strace-73e74dc727ee.html)
  Evidence note: Correlates client and server-side strace read() calls with tcpdump packet captures on both ends to diagnose why an RMAN show-all command hung on an established but stalled connection.
- [Where to check Data Guard gap?](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/where-to-check-data-guard-gap-e1ccadc8f41) - 2019-01-27 | Medium | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Backup & Recovery, Migration & Compatibility, Operations & Observability; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2019-01-27_Where-to-check-Data-Guard-gap--e1ccadc8f41.html](2018-medium/posts/2019-01-27_Where-to-check-Data-Guard-gap--e1ccadc8f41.html)
  Evidence note: Compares v$database's SCN-to-timestamp conversion, gv$recovery_progress, and other views for detecting Data Guard apply gaps, noting v$database works without Active Data Guard licensing.
- [Oracle numbers in K/M/G/T/P/E](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/oracle-numbers-in-k-m-g-t-p-e-81e3175eae7e) - 2019-01-24 | Medium | Oracle Database | Operations & Observability; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2019-01-24_Oracle-numbers-in-K-M-G-T-P-E-81e3175eae7e.html](2018-medium/posts/2019-01-24_Oracle-numbers-in-K-M-G-T-P-E-81e3175eae7e.html)
  Evidence note: Reuses DBMS_XPLAN's internal FORMAT_SIZE, FORMAT_NUMBER, and FORMAT_TIME functions to convert raw V$SYSSTAT byte and time counters into human-readable units without a custom function.
- [Oracle — Table lock modes](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/oracle-table-lock-modes-83346ccf6a41) - 2019-01-18 | Medium | Oracle Database | Indexing, Transactions & Consistency, Migration & Compatibility, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2019-01-18_Oracle---Table-lock-modes-83346ccf6a41.html](2018-medium/posts/2019-01-18_Oracle---Table-lock-modes-83346ccf6a41.html)
  Evidence note: Points to a table-lock demo covering the RS/RX/Share/SSX/X compatibility matrix, TX and TM lock detection, and the event 10704 trace replaced by UTS tracing since 12cR2.
- [Ok, good to know.](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/ok-good-to-know-9458e8521995) - 2019-01-18 | Medium | Database agnostic | General Database; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2019-01-18_Ok--good-to-know--9458e8521995.html](2018-medium/posts/2019-01-18_Ok--good-to-know--9458e8521995.html)
  Evidence note: Acknowledges that Oracle development began investigating an issue after the original write-up appeared, recommending the reader open a Service Request to have it formally tracked.
- [SYS.STATS_TARGET$](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/sys-stats-target-f5aa6700e17e) - 2019-01-16 | Medium | Oracle Database | Operations & Observability; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2019-01-16_SYS-STATS-TARGET--f5aa6700e17e.html](2018-medium/posts/2019-01-16_SYS-STATS-TARGET--f5aa6700e17e.html)
  Evidence note: Documents the undocumented SYS.STATS_TARGET dollar table, including its STATUS lifecycle from PENDING to COMPLETED or TIMED OUT, used internally by the Auto Stats maintenance job.
- [You don’t need the PLAN_TABLE table](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/you-dont-need-the-plan-table-table-7761bc034704) - 2019-01-10 | Medium | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2019-01-10_You-don-t-need-the-PLAN-TABLE-table-7761bc034704.html](2018-medium/posts/2019-01-10_You-don-t-need-the-PLAN-TABLE-table-7761bc034704.html)
  Evidence note: Traces a cannot-fetch-last-explain-plan error to a stale application-owned PLAN_TABLE shadowing the SYS global temporary table since Oracle 8i, fixed by dropping or renaming it.
- [Oracle global vs. partition level statistics CBO usage](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/oracle-global-vs-partition-level-statistics-cbo-usage-1c2aa2aa3f32) - 2019-01-08 | Medium | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Operations & Observability; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2019-01-08_Oracle-global-vs--partition-level-statistics-CBO-usage-1c2aa2aa3f32.html](2018-medium/posts/2019-01-08_Oracle-global-vs--partition-level-statistics-CBO-usage-1c2aa2aa3f32.html)
  Evidence note: Tests on 18c to confirm that the optimizer uses partition-level statistics only when pruning resolves to a single partition known at parse time, otherwise falling back to global stats.
- [Oracle literal vs bind-variable in partition pruning and Top-N queries](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/oracle-literal-vs-bind-variable-in-partition-pruning-and-top-n-queries-9c4145831389) - 2019-01-07 | Medium | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Indexing; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2019-01-07_Oracle-literal-vs-bind-variable-in-partition-pruning-and-Top-N-queries-9c4145831389.html](2018-medium/posts/2019-01-07_Oracle-literal-vs-bind-variable-in-partition-pruning-and-Top-N-queries-9c4145831389.html)
  Evidence note: Traces event 10128 and the KKPAP_PRUNING table to show that literal values let a Top-N query stop after two partitions via descending range scan, while bind variables read every partition.
- [Data Guard gap history](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/data-guard-gap-history-efc982f6aecd) - 2019-01-06 | Medium | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Backup & Recovery, Operations & Observability; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2019-01-06_Data-Guard-gap-history-efc982f6aecd.html](2018-medium/posts/2019-01-06_Data-Guard-gap-history-efc982f6aecd.html)
  Evidence note: Parses the standby alert.log with awk to join Media Recovery Log timestamps against V$ARCHIVED_LOG.COMPLETION_TIME, surfacing historical apply gaps a single live view would miss.
- [Oracle listener static service hi-jacking](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/oracle-listener-static-service-hi-jacking-b9dff0fbd5e9) - 2019-01-05 | Medium | Oracle Database | Security; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2019-01-05_Oracle-listener-static-service-hi-jacking-b9dff0fbd5e9.html](2018-medium/posts/2019-01-05_Oracle-listener-static-service-hi-jacking-b9dff0fbd5e9.html)
  Evidence note: Warns that a dynamically registered service sharing the name of a static CDB$ROOT service can silently redirect multitenant common-user connections to a different pluggable database.

### 2018

- [UUID (aka GUID) vs. Oracle sequence number](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/uuid-aka-guid-vs-oracle-sequence-number-ab11aa7dbfe7) - 2018-12-31 | Medium | Oracle Database | Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2018-12-31_UUID--aka-GUID--vs--Oracle-sequence-number-ab11aa7dbfe7.html](2018-medium/posts/2018-12-31_UUID--aka-GUID--vs--Oracle-sequence-number-ab11aa7dbfe7.html)
  Evidence note: Benchmarks a 10-million-row table showing a NUMBER primary key from a cached SEQUENCE averages under 5 bytes, smaller and cheaper to generate than a SYS_GUID-based UUID column.
- [Index Only access with Oracle, MySQL, PostgreSQL, and Microsoft SQL Server](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/index-only-access-with-oracle-mysql-postgresql-and-microsoft-sql-server-302383103998) - 2018-12-26 | Medium | Oracle Database, PostgreSQL, Microsoft SQL Server, MySQL | Query Optimization, Indexing, Operations & Observability; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2018-12-26_Index-Only-access-with-Oracle--MySQL--PostgreSQL--and-Microsoft-SQL-Server-302383103998.html](2018-medium/posts/2018-12-26_Index-Only-access-with-Oracle--MySQL--PostgreSQL--and-Microsoft-SQL-Server-302383103998.html)
  Evidence note: Builds the same covering-index demo on four engines to show that PostgreSQL's Index Only Scan still visits the table for concurrently modified rows, unlike true Oracle index-only access.
- [We still need partitions and indexes, and I will continue to commute by train even when…](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/we-still-need-partitions-and-indexes-and-i-will-continue-to-commute-by-train-even-when-c21680956a13) - 2018-12-22 | Medium | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Indexing, Performance & Benchmarking, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2018-12-22_We-still-need-partitions-and-indexes--and-I-will-continue-to-commute-by-train-even-when--c21680956a13.html](2018-medium/posts/2018-12-22_We-still-need-partitions-and-indexes--and-I-will-continue-to-commute-by-train-even-when--c21680956a13.html)

- [Oracle VPD as a safeguard for DML](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/oracle-vpd-as-a-safeguard-for-dml-eabf77bc38bb) - 2018-12-17 | Medium | Oracle Database | Security, Events & Community; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2018-12-17_Oracle-VPD-as-a-safeguard-for-DML-eabf77bc38bb.html](2018-medium/posts/2018-12-17_Oracle-VPD-as-a-safeguard-for-DML-eabf77bc38bb.html)

  Evidence note: Demonstrates DBMS_RLS on the SCOTT schema so a role with full DML privileges can still only modify EMP rows in the SALES department, turning VPD into a safeguard rather than a restriction.
- [CPU Capacity planning from OEM metrics](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/cpu-capacity-planning-from-oem-metrics-aec8e4eec7f3) - 2018-12-14 | Medium | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure, Operations & Observability; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2018-12-14_CPU-Capacity-planning-from-OEM-metrics-aec8e4eec7f3.html](2018-medium/posts/2018-12-14_CPU-Capacity-planning-from-OEM-metrics-aec8e4eec7f3.html)

- [Minimal Oracle](https://db-blog.web.cern.ch/blog/franck-pachot/2018-12-minimal-oracle) - 2018-12-09 | CERN | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [cern/articles/154.json](cern/articles/154.json)
  Evidence note: Trims an 18.4 Oracle Home down to only the binaries, shared libraries, and message files sqlplus needs, tracking each missing shared-object error to decide what minimal deployment requires.
- [See you in OBUG Tech Days Belgium](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/see-you-in-obug-tech-days-belgium-afe4a2ad3b4e) - 2018-12-07 | Medium | Database agnostic | Events & Community; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2018-12-07_See-you-in-OBUG-Tech-Days-Belgium-afe4a2ad3b4e.html](2018-medium/posts/2018-12-07_See-you-in-OBUG-Tech-Days-Belgium-afe4a2ad3b4e.html)

- [Oracle Index compression for range scan on file names](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/oracle-index-compression-for-range-scan-on-file-names-5f85d14c49ff) - 2018-12-01 | Medium | Oracle Database | Indexing; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2018-12-01_Oracle-Index-compression-for-range-scan-on-file-names-5f85d14c49ff.html](2018-medium/posts/2018-12-01_Oracle-Index-compression-for-range-scan-on-file-names-5f85d14c49ff.html)
