- [Oracle Index compression for range scan on file names](https://db-blog.web.cern.ch/blog/franck-pachot/2018-11-oracle-index-compression-range-scan-file-names) - 2018-11-30 | CERN | Oracle Database | Indexing, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [cern/articles/150.json](cern/articles/150.json)
  Evidence note: Tests 12cR2 COMPRESS ADVANCED HIGH against uncompressed and ADVANCED LOW indexes on unique, prefix-redundant file-path values to measure real buffer-read savings for range-scan queries.
- [Oracle Adaptive Plan info in OTHER_XML](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/oracle-adaptive-plan-info-in-other-xml-384b5f244e0c) - 2018-11-27 | Medium | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Indexing, Internals & Storage; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2018-11-27_Oracle-Adaptive-Plan-info-in-OTHER-XML-384b5f244e0c.html](2018-medium/posts/2018-11-27_Oracle-Adaptive-Plan-info-in-OTHER-XML-384b5f244e0c.html)
  Evidence note: Compares dbms_xplan's default and adaptive formats to locate where an Oracle adaptive execution plan stores information about the inactive branches it skipped, using a natural join.
- [Oracle LIKE predicate and cardinality estimations](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/oracle-like-predicate-and-cardinality-estimations-a9eb6bb43c36) - 2018-11-11 | Medium | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Indexing; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2018-11-11_Oracle-LIKE-predicate-and-cardinality-estimations-a9eb6bb43c36.html](2018-medium/posts/2018-11-11_Oracle-LIKE-predicate-and-cardinality-estimations-a9eb6bb43c36.html)
  Evidence note: Summarizes a CERN test showing the optimizer estimates 1,346 rows, not 2, for a LIKE 'DBA_OBJECTS' predicate because the underscore wildcard degrades it to an unanchored pattern.
- [Oracle LIKE predicate and cardinality estimations](https://db-blog.web.cern.ch/blog/franck-pachot/2018-11-oracle-predicate-and-cardinality-estimations) - 2018-11-11 | CERN | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Indexing; archive: [cern/articles/148.json](cern/articles/148.json)
  Evidence note: Classifies LIKE predicates as equality, range, or full-scan-only based on wildcard position, explaining why an unescaped underscore in a query inflates cardinality estimates and skews plans.
- [What’s new in Oracle Database 19c and other #OOW18 feedback](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/whats-new-in-oracle-database-19c-and-other-oow18-feedback-d9c5cf094af4) - 2018-10-28 | Medium | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Migration & Compatibility, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2018-10-28_What-s-new-in-Oracle-Database-19c-and-other--OOW18-feedback-d9c5cf094af4.html](2018-medium/posts/2018-10-28_What-s-new-in-Oracle-Database-19c-and-other--OOW18-feedback-d9c5cf094af4.html)
  Evidence note: Recaps Oracle Open World 19c announcements under the Safe Harbor disclaimer, including confirmed Long Term Support through 2025 and the newly merged Oracle Code One conference.
- [An .rpm to install Oracle Database 18c](https://db-blog.web.cern.ch/blog/franck-pachot/2018-10-rpm-install-oracle-database-18c) - 2018-10-20 | CERN | Oracle Database | Transactions & Consistency, Migration & Compatibility, Operations & Observability; archive: [cern/articles/146.json](cern/articles/146.json)
  Evidence note: Compares the 18.3 RPM installer's automatic preinstall-package dependency resolution against the equivalent zip-based runInstaller method, noting both extract the identical 7.8GB build.
- [A new blog post on the Databases at CERN blog about the new 18.3 installation with .rpm:](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/a-new-blog-post-on-the-databases-at-cern-blog-about-the-new-18-3-installation-with-rpm-39c2d615e617) - 2018-10-20 | Medium | Oracle Database | Operations & Observability; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2018-10-20_A-new-blog-post-on-the-Databases-at-CERN-blog-about-the-new-18-3-installation-with--rpm--39c2d615e617.html](2018-medium/posts/2018-10-20_A-new-blog-post-on-the-Databases-at-CERN-blog-about-the-new-18-3-installation-with--rpm--39c2d615e617.html)
  Evidence note: Links to CERN coverage of the newly released 18.3 RPM installer for Oracle Database, announced at Oracle Open World a year before the RPM actually shipped.
- [ODC Appreciation Day: Reduce CPU usage by running the business logic in the Oracle Database](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/odc-appreciation-day-reduce-cpu-usage-by-running-the-business-logic-in-the-oracle-database-ad502e54f145) - 2018-10-11 | Medium | Oracle Database | General Database; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2018-10-11_ODC-Appreciation-Day--Reduce-CPU-usage-by-running-the-business-logic-in-the-Oracle-Database-ad502e54f145.html](2018-medium/posts/2018-10-11_ODC-Appreciation-Day--Reduce-CPU-usage-by-running-the-business-logic-in-the-Oracle-Database-ad502e54f145.html)
  Evidence note: Links to a CERN comparison of where business logic runs, SQL, PL/SQL, or JavaScript, and in which tier, showing how co-locating logic with data cuts CPU and licensing cost.
- [ODC Appreciation Day : Reduce CPU usage by running the business logic in the Oracle Database](https://db-blog.web.cern.ch/blog/franck-pachot/2018-10-odc-appreciation-day-reduce-cpu-usage-running-business-logic-oracle) - 2018-10-11 | CERN | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Transactions & Consistency, Events & Community; archive: [cern/articles/145.json](cern/articles/145.json)
  Evidence note: Benchmarks CPU usage of the same transfer transaction run as PL/SQL in the database, JavaScript plus SQL in one process, and JavaScript client plus server SQL split across two processes.
- [Unindexed Foreign Keys in Oracle and PostgreSQL](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/unindexed-foreign-keys-in-oracle-and-postgresql-a674f552002e) - 2018-10-02 | Medium | Oracle Database, PostgreSQL | Indexing, Transactions & Consistency, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2018-10-02_Unindexed-Foreign-Keys-in-Oracle-and-PostgreSQL-a674f552002e.html](2018-medium/posts/2018-10-02_Unindexed-Foreign-Keys-in-Oracle-and-PostgreSQL-a674f552002e.html)
  Evidence note: Links to a CERN test using the pgSentinel extension confirming that, unlike Oracle, PostgreSQL's MVCC does not lock the full child table when a foreign key column lacks an index.
- [Unindexed Foreign Keys in Oracle and PostgreSQL](https://db-blog.web.cern.ch/blog/franck-pachot/2018-09-unindexed-foreign-keys-oracle-and-postgresql) - 2018-09-28 | CERN | Oracle Database, PostgreSQL | Indexing, Performance & Benchmarking, Transactions & Consistency, Internals & Storage, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [cern/articles/143.json](cern/articles/143.json)
  Evidence note: Runs matched insert, delete, and update combinations on an EMP/DEPT schema with pg_active_session_history to prove PostgreSQL's MVCC avoids the full-table lock Oracle takes on unindexed keys.
- [Efficient query on DBA_EXTENTS for access by FILE_ID / BLOCK_ID](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/efficient-query-on-dba-extents-for-access-by-file-id-block-id-675f3861b144) - 2018-09-28 | Medium | Oracle Database | General Database; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2018-09-28_Efficient-query-on-DBA-EXTENTS-for-access-by-FILE-ID---BLOCK-ID-675f3861b144.html](2018-medium/posts/2018-09-28_Efficient-query-on-DBA-EXTENTS-for-access-by-FILE-ID---BLOCK-ID-675f3861b144.html)
  Evidence note: Links to a CERN rewrite of a DBA_EXTENTS query starting from X$KTFBUE and X$KCCFE instead of segments, cutting a lookup on a 900TB, 7,000-datafile database down to one second.
- [Oracle write consistency, bug, and scalable multi-thread de-queuing](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/oracle-write-consistency-bug-and-scalable-multi-thread-de-queuing-abb00ed5bae7) - 2018-09-27 | Medium | Oracle Database | Transactions & Consistency; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2018-09-27_Oracle-write-consistency--bug--and-scalable-multi-thread-de-queuing-abb00ed5bae7.html](2018-medium/posts/2018-09-27_Oracle-write-consistency--bug--and-scalable-multi-thread-de-queuing-abb00ed5bae7.html)
  Evidence note: Links to a CERN reproduction of a write-consistency bug, first reported by Tom Kyte in 2011 and still present in 18c, shown through flashback query row versions between two sessions.
- [Oracle write consistency bug and multi-thread de-queuing](https://db-blog.web.cern.ch/blog/franck-pachot/2018-09-oracle-write-consistency-bug-and-multi-thread-de-queuing) - 2018-09-27 | CERN | Oracle Database | Transactions & Consistency; archive: [cern/articles/142.json](cern/articles/142.json)
  Evidence note: Reproduces a still-open Oracle write-consistency bug from 2011 where two sessions update the same rowset by subquery, then uses flashback versions to expose the resulting anomaly.
- [Efficiently query DBA_EXTENTS for FILE_ID / BLOCK_ID](https://db-blog.web.cern.ch/blog/franck-pachot/2018-09-efficiently-query-dbaextents-fileid-blockid) - 2018-09-27 | CERN | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Internals & Storage; archive: [cern/articles/140.json](cern/articles/140.json)
  Evidence note: Rewrites a 2006 dba-village view to force the plan to start from X$KCCFE and a materialized X$KTFBUE CTE, resolving a hot block's segment on a 900TB database in one second.
- [Upload large files to Oracle Cloud with the Object Storage REST API](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/upload-large-files-to-oracle-cloud-with-the-object-storage-rest-api-ecb7de0f2abf) - 2018-09-26 | Medium | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2018-09-26_Upload-large-files-to-Oracle-Cloud-with-the-Object-Storage-REST-API-ecb7de0f2abf.html](2018-medium/posts/2018-09-26_Upload-large-files-to-Oracle-Cloud-with-the-Object-Storage-REST-API-ecb7de0f2abf.html)
  Evidence note: Links to CERN's oci-curl bash function that multipart-uploads large Data Pump dumps and RMAN backup sets directly to OCI Object Storage without installing a client SDK.
- [Oracle Cloud: upload large files through the Object Store REST API](https://db-blog.web.cern.ch/blog/franck-pachot/2018-09-oracle-cloud-upload-large-files-through-object-store-rest-api) - 2018-09-26 | CERN | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Backup & Recovery, Cloud & Infrastructure, Security; archive: [cern/articles/139.json](cern/articles/139.json)
  Evidence note: Extends an earlier oci-curl bash function with multipart PUT requests to upload a 42GB test file to OCI Object Storage without installing any additional client tooling.
- [Start/Stop your Autonomous Databases](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/start-stop-your-autonomous-databases-333f1bfa6f90) - 2018-09-18 | Medium | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure, Operations & Observability; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2018-09-18_Start-Stop-your-Autonomous-Databases-333f1bfa6f90.html](2018-medium/posts/2018-09-18_Start-Stop-your-Autonomous-Databases-333f1bfa6f90.html)
  Evidence note: Combines two CERN write-ups' OCID and API-key setup with an oci-curl bash function into one script that lists and automatically stops every running Autonomous Database service.
- [Oracle Cloud: start/stop automatically the Autonomous Databases](https://db-blog.web.cern.ch/blog/franck-pachot/2018-09-oracle-cloud-startstop-automatically-autonomous-databases) - 2018-09-16 | CERN | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [cern/articles/138.json](cern/articles/138.json)
  Evidence note: Reuses the oci-curl bash function with jq filtering to list every Autonomous Database and Data Warehouse service's name, lifecycle state, and OCID, enabling scripted start and stop.
- [Oracle Cloud Infrastructure API Keys and OCID](https://db-blog.web.cern.ch/blog/franck-pachot/2018-09-oracle-cloud-infrastructure-api-keys-and-ocid) - 2018-09-16 | CERN | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [cern/articles/137.json](cern/articles/137.json)
  Evidence note: Generates a 2048-bit RSA key with openssl on Windows Subsystem for Linux and walks through the OCIDs needed to sign OCI REST API calls without installing the CLI or an SDK.
- [How to gather Oracle optimizer statistics with minimal risks of regression](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/how-to-gather-oracle-optimizer-statistics-with-minimal-risks-of-regression-e6476b3a5006) - 2018-09-14 | Medium | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Backup & Recovery, Operations & Observability; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2018-09-14_How-to-gather-Oracle-optimizer-statistics-with-minimal-risks-of-regression-e6476b3a5006.html](2018-medium/posts/2018-09-14_How-to-gather-Oracle-optimizer-statistics-with-minimal-risks-of-regression-e6476b3a5006.html)
  Evidence note: Summarizes CERN commands that gather statistics as unpublished pending stats, test them under optimizer_use_pending_statistics, and restore prior history if a regression appears.
- [Optimizer Statistics Gathering - pending and history](https://db-blog.web.cern.ch/blog/franck-pachot/2018-09-optimizer-statistics-gathering-pending-and-history) - 2018-09-12 | CERN | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Operations & Observability; archive: [cern/articles/136.json](cern/articles/136.json)
  Evidence note: Demonstrates gathering statistics as unpublished pending stats, testing them under optimizer_use_pending_statistics, then using diff_table_stats_in_history before publishing or restoring.
- [Recursive queries on IM_DOMAIN$ at each cursor execution](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/recursive-queries-on-im-domain-at-each-cursor-execution-33457ba4d2aa) - 2018-09-09 | Medium | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Internals & Storage, Data Modeling & SQL, Events & Community; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2018-09-09_Recursive-queries-on-IM-DOMAIN--at-each-cursor-execution-33457ba4d2aa.html](2018-medium/posts/2018-09-09_Recursive-queries-on-IM-DOMAIN--at-each-cursor-execution-33457ba4d2aa.html)
  Evidence note: Traces unexpected recursive queries against IM_DOMAIN dollar during hash-join execution on 12.2, tied to In-Memory Global Dictionary Join Groups even though In-Memory itself is disabled.
- [ASM Filter Driver — simple test on filtering](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/asm-filter-driver-simple-test-on-filtering-2a506f048ee5) - 2018-09-02 | Medium | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Operations & Observability, Development & Drivers; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2018-09-02_ASM-Filter-Driver---simple-test-on-filtering-2a506f048ee5.html](2018-medium/posts/2018-09-02_ASM-Filter-Driver---simple-test-on-filtering-2a506f048ee5.html)
  Evidence note: Confirms with a raw grep against the block device that ASM Filter Driver blocks external, non-ASM writes to a labeled disk once filtering is enabled on a 12.2 Grid Infrastructure install.
- [Download your SR content from MOS](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/download-your-sr-content-from-mos-bc4a726efbdb) - 2018-08-31 | Medium | Oracle Database | Security; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2018-08-31_Download-your-SR-content-from-MOS-bc4a726efbdb.html](2018-medium/posts/2018-08-31_Download-your-SR-content-from-MOS-bc4a726efbdb.html)
  Evidence note: Chains an exported Service Request Excel list through awk and lynx to bulk-download each My Oracle Support SR as a plain text file named by contact, SR number, and subject.
- [A tribute to Natural Join](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/a-tribute-to-natural-join/) - 2018-08-20 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/a-tribute-to-natural-join.json](dbi-services/articles/a-tribute-to-natural-join.json)
  Evidence note: A NATURAL JOIN between EMP and DEPT breaks silently and returns zero rows once both tables independently gain a same-named LOC column, showing the risk lies in unprojected column names, not NATURAL JOIN itself.
- [The size of Oracle Home: from 9GB to 600MB](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/the-size-of-oracle-home-from-9gb-to-600mb/) - 2018-08-16 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Cloud & Infrastructure, Security, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/the-size-of-oracle-home-from-9gb-to-600mb.json](dbi-services/articles/the-size-of-oracle-home-from-9gb-to-600mb.json)
  Evidence note: Running representative workloads against an Oracle 18c ORACLE_HOME and keeping only files actually touched showed the true footprint drops from 9GB, mostly libraries and patch storage, down to under 600MB.
- [Oracle Database on OpenShift](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-database-on-openshift/) - 2018-08-14 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Indexing, Internals & Storage, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-database-on-openshift.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-database-on-openshift.json)
  Evidence note: After pulling an Oracle Database image from Docker Store, importing it into OpenShift's Image Stream on a MiniShift VM required raising the disk allocation to 40GB beyond MiniShift's 20GB default.
- [ORACLE_HOME with symbolic link and postupgrade_fixups](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle_home-with-symbolic-link-and-postupgrade_fixups/) - 2018-08-13 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Migration & Compatibility; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle_home-with-symbolic-link-and-postupgrade_fixups.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle_home-with-symbolic-link-and-postupgrade_fixups.json)
  Evidence note: Running postupgrade_fixups.sql fails with ORA-29283 when ORACLE_HOME is a symbolic link, because DBMS_PREUP's UTL_FILE call to the preupgrade_dir directory object cannot resolve the linked path correctly.
- [18c runInstaller -silent](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/18c-runinstaller-silent/) - 2018-08-12 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Operations & Observability, Development & Drivers; archive: [dbi-services/articles/18c-runinstaller-silent.json](dbi-services/articles/18c-runinstaller-silent.json)
  Evidence note: Oracle 18c's root-level runInstaller is a shell wrapper around Perl's dbSetup.pl, and calling it with -silent -executePrereqs checks OS prerequisites before installation without launching the Java GUI.
- [TRANSPORT_CONNECT_TIMEOUT and RETRY_COUNT](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/transport_connect_timeout-and-retry_count/) - 2018-08-10 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/transport_connect_timeout-and-retry_count.json](dbi-services/articles/transport_connect_timeout-and-retry_count.json)
  Evidence note: Without a virtual IP, a client address list failing over between two Data Guard hosts waits the full 60-second default TCP timeout per address unless TRANSPORT_CONNECT_TIMEOUT and RETRY_COUNT are set explicitly.
- [ATP vs ADW – the Autonomous Database lockdown profiles](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/atp-vs-adw-the-autonomous-database-lockdown-profiles/) - 2018-08-09 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Indexing, Transactions & Consistency, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/atp-vs-adw-the-autonomous-database-lockdown-profiles.json](dbi-services/articles/atp-vs-adw-the-autonomous-database-lockdown-profiles.json)
  Evidence note: Oracle's ATP and ADW autonomous services run as PDBs inside the same CDB and differ mainly by their OLTP versus DWCS PDB lockdown profile, which enables the partitioning option only for the ATP profile.
- [MERGE JOIN CARTESIAN: a join method or a join type?](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/merge-join-cartesian-a-join-method-or-a-join-type/) - 2018-08-08 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Operations & Observability, Events & Community; archive: [dbi-services/articles/merge-join-cartesian-a-join-method-or-a-join-type.json](dbi-services/articles/merge-join-cartesian-a-join-method-or-a-join-type.json)
  Evidence note: Forcing the USE_MERGE_CARTESIAN hint on an EMP-DEPT join shows Oracle can implement an inner join by first computing a full Cartesian product and filtering afterward, classifying it as a join method, not a join type.
- [How much free space can be reclaimed from a segment?](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/how-much-free-space-can-be-reclaimed-from-a-segment/) - 2018-08-07 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Indexing, Internals & Storage; archive: [dbi-services/articles/how-much-free-space-can-be-reclaimed-from-a-segment.json](dbi-services/articles/how-much-free-space-can-be-reclaimed-from-a-segment.json)
  Evidence note: DBMS_SPACE.SPACE_USAGE's FS1 through FS4 free-space buckets let a PL/SQL loop over DBA_SEGMENTS estimate a lower-bound reclaimable free space per ASSM segment without running a full segment shrink.
- [Oracle 18c preinstall RPM on RedHat RHEL](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-18c-preinstall-rpm-on-redhat-rhel/) - 2018-08-03 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-18c-preinstall-rpm-on-redhat-rhel.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-18c-preinstall-rpm-on-redhat-rhel.json)
  Evidence note: Since Oracle 18c, the database preinstall RPM installs cleanly on RHEL and its derivatives, not just Oracle Linux, resolving every Linux prerequisite dependency through a single yum localinstall command.
- [Release 18.0.0.0.0 Version 18.3.0.0.0 On-Premises binaries](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/release-18-0-0-0-0-version-18-3-0-0-0-on-premises-binaries/) - 2018-07-24 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Migration & Compatibility, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/release-18-0-0-0-0-version-18-3-0-0-0-on-premises-binaries.json](dbi-services/articles/release-18-0-0-0-0-version-18-3-0-0-0-on-premises-binaries.json)
  Evidence note: Oracle 18c, the renamed 12cR2 patchset, appeared on OTN under the free developer license after Oracle Cloud stabilization, enabling version-matched on-prem binaries for 18.3 cloud databases.
- [Installing ZFS on OEL7 UEK4 for Docker storage](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/installing-zfs-on-oel7-uek4-for-docker-storage/) - 2018-07-20 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Cloud & Infrastructure, Development & Drivers; archive: [dbi-services/articles/installing-zfs-on-oel7-uek4-for-docker-storage.json](dbi-services/articles/installing-zfs-on-oel7-uek4-for-docker-storage.json)
  Evidence note: Using ZFS as a Docker storage driver on OEL7 with UEK4 requires installing matching kernel-uek-devel headers and DKMS first, since Docker's own documentation recommends ZFS for dense database-as-a-service workloads.
- [Installing ZFS on OEL7 UEK4](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/installing-zfs-on-oel7-uek4-2dd8bfcd54b5) - 2018-07-20 | Medium | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2018-07-20_Installing-ZFS-on-OEL7-UEK4-2dd8bfcd54b5.html](2018-medium/posts/2018-07-20_Installing-ZFS-on-OEL7-UEK4-2dd8bfcd54b5.html)
  Evidence note: Provides the yum and dkms commands to install OpenZFS on Oracle Linux 7 UEK4 in Oracle Cloud Compute, then creates a zpool intended for use as Docker storage.
- [Conferences 2018](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/conferences-2018-7b821155591) - 2018-07-20 | Medium | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Indexing, Performance & Benchmarking, Cloud & Infrastructure, Security, Operations & Observability, Events & Community; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2018-07-20_Conferences-2018-7b821155591.html](2018-medium/posts/2018-07-20_Conferences-2018-7b821155591.html)
  Evidence note: Lists year-end 2018 talks at POUG, DOAG, and Oak Table World covering join-method internals and how 12c/18c multitenant lockdown profiles reshape DBA-versus-application role split.
- [Google Cloud Spanner – inserting data](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/google-cloud-spanner-inserting-data/) - 2018-07-19 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/google-cloud-spanner-inserting-data.json](dbi-services/articles/google-cloud-spanner-inserting-data.json)
  Evidence note: Google Cloud Spanner's gcloud beta rows insert command handles only one row per invocation, so loading a million rows into a 3-node distributed instance required a shell loop, not bulk SQL.
- [Google Cloud Spanner – no decimal numeric data types](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/google-cloud-spanner-no-decimal-numeric-data-types/) - 2018-07-16 | dbi services | Database agnostic | High Availability & Replication, Distributed Databases, Transactions & Consistency, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/google-cloud-spanner-no-decimal-numeric-data-types.json](dbi-services/articles/google-cloud-spanner-no-decimal-numeric-data-types.json)
  Evidence note: Google Cloud Spanner originally offered only INT64 and IEEE-754 FLOAT64 data types, so storing exact decimal values like prices in a FLOAT64 column introduces binary rounding error absent from an Oracle NUMBER column.
- [Drilling down the pgSentinel Active Session History](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/drilling-down-the-pgsentinel-active-session-history/) - 2018-07-15 | dbi services | PostgreSQL | General Database; archive: [dbi-services/articles/drilling-down-the-pgsentinel-active-session-history.json](dbi-services/articles/drilling-down-the-pgsentinel-active-session-history.json)
  Evidence note: Grouping pg_active_session_history by backend_type over a 5-minute window and dividing the sample count by elapsed seconds derives an Average Active Sessions metric for PostgreSQL, matching Oracle's AAS from ASH.
- [Oracle: connection rate to the listener](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/oracle-connection-rate-to-the-listener-16601c06fd3a) - 2018-07-13 | Medium | Oracle Database | General Database; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2018-07-13_Oracle--connection-rate-to-the-listener-16601c06fd3a.html](2018-medium/posts/2018-07-13_Oracle--connection-rate-to-the-listener-16601c06fd3a.html)
  Evidence note: Chains adrci's alert-log query with awk to count Oracle listener connection establishments grouped by hour, starting from a chosen timestamp in the log.
- [pgSentinel: the sampling approach for PostgreSQL](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/pgsentinel-the-sampling-approach-for-postgresql/) - 2018-07-12 | dbi services | Oracle Database, PostgreSQL | Performance & Benchmarking, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/pgsentinel-the-sampling-approach-for-postgresql.json](dbi-services/articles/pgsentinel-the-sampling-approach-for-postgresql.json)
  Evidence note: The pgSentinel extension samples pg_stat_activity into a queryable Active Session History table for PostgreSQL, exposing wait detail during a PGIO SLOB-style run that plain 'top -c' output cannot reveal.
- [Data Guard: always set db_create_file_dest on the standby](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/data-guard-always-set-db_create_file_dest-on-the-standby/) - 2018-07-09 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Backup & Recovery, Migration & Compatibility, Cloud & Infrastructure, Operations & Observability, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/data-guard-always-set-db_create_file_dest-on-the-standby.json](dbi-services/articles/data-guard-always-set-db_create_file_dest-on-the-standby.json)
  Evidence note: Because db_file_name_convert changes require a Data Guard standby instance restart to take effect, setting the dynamic db_create_file_dest parameter instead lets unmatched new primary datafiles apply without downtime.
- [Stays One Step Ahead With Certification](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/stays-one-step-ahead-with-certification-bb900f473827) - 2018-07-07 | Medium | Oracle Database | Backup & Recovery; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2018-07-07_Stays-One-Step-Ahead-With-Certification-bb900f473827.html](2018-medium/posts/2018-07-07_Stays-One-Step-Ahead-With-Certification-bb900f473827.html)
  Evidence note: Credits OCP, OCE, and OCM 11g certification exams, taken since 8i, for forcing broad exposure to features like Resource Manager and RMAN before they became widely adopted.
- [strace the current Oracle session process](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/strace-the-current-oracle-session-process-38594cf6a11a) - 2018-07-06 | Medium | Oracle Database | General Database; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2018-07-06_strace-the-current-Oracle-session-process-38594cf6a11a.html](2018-medium/posts/2018-07-06_strace-the-current-Oracle-session-process-38594cf6a11a.html)
  Evidence note: Queries v$process and v$session for the current session's spid, then attaches strace in the background from sqlplus, using SET ESCAPE ON to avoid clashing with SQL substitution variables.
- [Oracle archivelog deletion policy](https://medium.com/@FranckPachot/oracle-archivelog-deletion-policy-e71f650e3300) - 2018-07-06 | Medium | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Backup & Recovery, Operations & Observability; archive: [2018-medium/posts/2018-07-06_Oracle-archivelog-deletion-policy-e71f650e3300.html](2018-medium/posts/2018-07-06_Oracle-archivelog-deletion-policy-e71f650e3300.html)
  Evidence note: Distinguishes the archivelog deletion policy clause applied on all standby, with or without a backed-up-once-to-disk qualifier, depending on which Data Guard site performs backups.
- [My tmux scripts to script demos](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/my-tmux-scripts-to-script-demos/) - 2018-07-06 | dbi services | Database agnostic | Cloud & Infrastructure, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/my-tmux-scripts-to-script-demos.json](dbi-services/articles/my-tmux-scripts-to-script-demos.json)
  Evidence note: Tmux scripts and a customized vim configuration, published on GitHub, drive semi-interactive conference demos by mapping vim macros to tmux-send commands that replay script lines into a shared attached session.
- [18c: Order by in WITH clause is not preserved](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/18c-order-by-in-with-clause-is-not-preserved/) - 2018-07-05 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization; archive: [dbi-services/articles/18c-order-by-in-with-clause-is-not-preserved.json](dbi-services/articles/18c-order-by-in-with-clause-is-not-preserved.json)
  Evidence note: When 12.2+ materializes a reused CTE into an In-Memory Cursor Duration Temp Table, ORDER BY inside the WITH clause no longer preserves row order across UNION ALL consumers, breaking ROWNUM logic.
- [18c: some optimization about redo size](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/18c-some-optimization-about-redo-size/) - 2018-07-03 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/18c-some-optimization-about-redo-size.json](dbi-services/articles/18c-some-optimization-about-redo-size.json)
  Evidence note: Updating 10,000 rows to the same uppercase value they already store produces about six times less redo than changing them to a new value, showing 18c still optimizes same-value UPDATEs.
- [Event Sourcing: CQN is not a replacement for CDC](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/event-sourcing-cqn-is-not-a-replacement-for-cdc/) - 2018-07-02 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Internals & Storage; archive: [dbi-services/articles/event-sourcing-cqn-is-not-a-replacement-for-cdc.json](dbi-services/articles/event-sourcing-cqn-is-not-a-replacement-for-cdc.json)
  Evidence note: Oracle's Continuous Query Notification, built for refreshing caches on slowly changing data, is not designed for event-sourcing microservice architectures that need Change Data Capture for frequent transactional changes.
- [Statspack installation scripts](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/statspack-installation-scripts/) - 2018-06-29 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Operations & Observability, Events & Community; archive: [dbi-services/articles/statspack-installation-scripts.json](dbi-services/articles/statspack-installation-scripts.json)
  Evidence note: A published GitHub script set installs Statspack with its own tablespace, scheduled snapshot and purge jobs, a fix for idle-event noise, and a modified collection level for execution plans and segment statistics.
- [PGIO, PG_STAT_ACTIVITY and PG_STAT_STATEMENTS](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/pgio-pg_stat_activity-and-pg_stat_statements/) - 2018-06-28 | dbi services | Oracle Database, PostgreSQL | Performance & Benchmarking, Cloud & Infrastructure, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/pgio-pg_stat_activity-and-pg_stat_statements.json](dbi-services/articles/pgio-pg_stat_activity-and-pg_stat_statements.json)
  Evidence note: Unlike Oracle's V$SESSION and V$SQL joined on SQL_ID, PostgreSQL's pg_stat_activity only shows the top-level PL/pgSQL call during a PGIO run, so pg_stat_statements must be enabled to see the actual executed statements.
- [Lighty for PostgreSQL](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/lighty-for-postgresql/) - 2018-06-27 | dbi services | Oracle Database, PostgreSQL | Performance & Benchmarking, Cloud & Infrastructure, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/lighty-for-postgresql.json](dbi-services/articles/lighty-for-postgresql.json)
  Evidence note: Orachrome's Lighty for PostgreSQL beta samples pg_stat_activity every 10 seconds into its own dedicated database, whose one-active-session average in its own dashboard is a sampling artifact rather than real overhead.
- [Kernel panic – not syncing: Out of memory and no killable processes](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/kernel-panic-not-syncing-out-of-memory-and-no-killable-processes/) - 2018-06-26 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/kernel-panic-not-syncing-out-of-memory-and-no-killable-processes.json](dbi-services/articles/kernel-panic-not-syncing-out-of-memory-and-no-killable-processes.json)
  Evidence note: A misconfigured vm.nr_hugepages value allocating nearly all server RAM caused a 'Kernel panic: Out of memory and no killable processes' at boot, fixed temporarily by forcing mem=4g at the GRUB prompt.
- [18c: No Active Data Guard required (and detected) when only CDB$ROOT and PDB$SEED are opened in read-only](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/18c-no-active-data-guard-required-and-detected-when-only-cdbroot-and-pdbseed-are-opened-in-read-only/) - 2018-06-24 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Cloud & Infrastructure, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/18c-no-active-data-guard-required-and-detected-when-only-cdbroot-and-pdbseed-are-opened-in-read-only.json](dbi-services/articles/18c-no-active-data-guard-required-and-detected-when-only-cdbroot-and-pdbseed-are-opened-in-read-only.json)
  Evidence note: Oracle 18c removes the Active Data Guard requirement for real-time-apply read-only opening of CDB$ROOT and PDB$SEED during online PDB cloning, as long as user PDBs stay closed while managed recovery applies changes.
- [Introduction to databases for {Power.Coders} with MySQL](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/introduction-to-database-for-power-coders-with-mysql/) - 2018-06-22 | dbi services | MySQL | General Database; archive: [dbi-services/articles/introduction-to-database-for-power-coders-with-mysql.json](dbi-services/articles/introduction-to-database-for-power-coders-with-mysql.json)
  Evidence note: Teaching data modeling and SQL basics to refugee coding-academy students used MySQL 8.0.19 and PHP, a stack chosen to match the students' existing MAMP curriculum rather than the instructor's usual Oracle environment.
- [Remote syslog from Linux and Solaris](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/remote-syslog-from-linux-and-solaris/) - 2018-06-20 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure, Security, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/remote-syslog-from-linux-and-solaris.json](dbi-services/articles/remote-syslog-from-linux-and-solaris.json)
  Evidence note: If SYSDBA auditing stays only in adump or the database trail, the DBA can erase it; forwarding Oracle audit records with rsyslog over TCP or UDP 514 to another host protects the evidence.
- [ADWC new OCI interface](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/adwc-new-oci-interface/) - 2018-06-17 | dbi services | Database agnostic | Migration & Compatibility, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/adwc-new-oci-interface.json](dbi-services/articles/adwc-new-oci-interface.json)
  Evidence note: Switching Autonomous Data Warehouse to the OCI console silently invalidated old PSM bookmarks with a generic START_SERVICE failed message, forcing use of the new console URL and OCI CLI.
- [Change Data Capture from Oracle with StreamSets Data Collector](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/change-data-capture-from-oracle-with-streamset-data-collector/) - 2018-06-11 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, High Availability & Replication, Cloud & Infrastructure, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/change-data-capture-from-oracle-with-streamset-data-collector.json](dbi-services/articles/change-data-capture-from-oracle-with-streamset-data-collector.json)
  Evidence note: StreamSets Data Collector's Oracle CDC origin, built on LogMiner rather than Oracle GoldenGate, streams changes through a Docker-deployed pipeline while inheriting LogMiner's datatype, DDL, and performance limitations.
- [Installing MAMP to play with PHP, MySQL and OpenFlights](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/installing-mamp-to-play-with-php-mysql-and-openflights/) - 2018-06-02 | dbi services | MySQL | Indexing, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/installing-mamp-to-play-with-php-mysql-and-openflights.json](dbi-services/articles/installing-mamp-to-play-with-php-mysql-and-openflights.json)
  Evidence note: Setting up MAMP with PHP and MySQL to load the OpenFlights open dataset for a database teaching exercise required disabling Apache's display_errors default before the first PHP scripts would run cleanly.
- [SQLcl connect target depends on previous connection](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/sqlcl-connect-target-depends-on-previous-connection/) - 2018-06-01 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Migration & Compatibility, Development & Drivers; archive: [dbi-services/articles/sqlcl-connect-target-depends-on-previous-connection.json](dbi-services/articles/sqlcl-connect-target-depends-on-previous-connection.json)
  Evidence note: In SQLcl, a bare 'connect / as sysdba' after connecting through a service name attempts a bequeath login that can fail or, with a matching sys password elsewhere, silently reconnect you to the wrong database.
- [OpenShift on my Windows 10 laptop with MiniShift](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/openshift-on-my-windows-10-laptop-with-minishift/) - 2018-05-31 | dbi services | Oracle Database, PostgreSQL, MySQL | Cloud & Infrastructure, Development & Drivers; archive: [dbi-services/articles/openshift-on-my-windows-10-laptop-with-minishift.json](dbi-services/articles/openshift-on-my-windows-10-laptop-with-minishift.json)
  Evidence note: Installing MiniShift 1.18.0 on Windows 10 with the VirtualBox driver fails to locate the VM's files whenever the minishift.exe binary and the default Windows profile directory sit on different drive letters.
- [Which Bitnami service to choose in the Oracle Cloud Infrastructure?](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/which-bitnami-service-to-choose-in-the-oracle-cloud-infrastructure/) - 2018-05-25 | dbi services | Oracle Database, PostgreSQL | Performance & Benchmarking, Internals & Storage, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/which-bitnami-service-to-choose-in-the-oracle-cloud-infrastructure.json](dbi-services/articles/which-bitnami-service-to-choose-in-the-oracle-cloud-infrastructure.json)
  Evidence note: Running Kevin Closson's pgio SLOB method with four 1GB cached-only schemas across Bitnami PostgreSQL images on different Linux distributions isolates whether the kernel changes CPU throughput.
- [Postgres, the fsync() issue, and ‘pgio’ (the SLOB method for PostgreSQL)](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/postgres-the-fsync-issue-and-pgio-the-slob-method-for-postgresql/) - 2018-05-24 | dbi services | Oracle Database, PostgreSQL | Performance & Benchmarking, Internals & Storage; archive: [dbi-services/articles/postgres-the-fsync-issue-and-pgio-the-slob-method-for-postgresql.json](dbi-services/articles/postgres-the-fsync-issue-and-pgio-the-slob-method-for-postgresql.json)
  Evidence note: PostgreSQL long relied on write() plus fsync() at checkpoints rather than O_DSYNC writes, which is why fsync error handling became a distinct risk in the long-running 'fsync issue' debate.
- [ADWC – System and session settings (DWCS lockdown profile)](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/adwc-system-and-session-settings-dwcs-lockdown-profile/) - 2018-05-24 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure, Security; archive: [dbi-services/articles/adwc-system-and-session-settings-dwcs-lockdown-profile.json](dbi-services/articles/adwc-system-and-session-settings-dwcs-lockdown-profile.json)
  Evidence note: Autonomous Data Warehouse's DWCS lockdown profile blocks almost every ALTER SYSTEM except a small allowlist and ALTER SYSTEM KILL SESSION, which ADMIN can inspect in V$LOCKDOWN_RULES.
- [12c upuserxt.lst, upobjxt.lst & Oracle Maintained objects/users](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12c-upuserxt-lst-upobjxt-lst-oracle-maintained-objectsusers/) - 2018-05-15 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12c-upuserxt-lst-upobjxt-lst-oracle-maintained-objectsusers.json](dbi-services/articles/12c-upuserxt-lst-upobjxt-lst-oracle-maintained-objectsusers.json)
  Evidence note: Since Oracle 12c, users and objects created at database creation carry ORACLE_MAINTAINED='Y', which lets upuserxt.lst and upobjxt.lst separate system accounts from application ones before upgrade.
- [ADWC – connect from your premises](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/adwc-connect-from-your-premises/) - 2018-05-14 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure, Security, Operations & Observability, Development & Drivers; archive: [dbi-services/articles/adwc-connect-from-your-premises.json](dbi-services/articles/adwc-connect-from-your-premises.json)
  Evidence note: SQLcl's thin JDBC driver can connect to Autonomous Data Warehouse directly from the downloaded wallet zip via 'set cloudconfig', unlike SQL Developer's Cloud PDB flow, which expects it unzipped.
- [SQL Developer Web on the Oracle Cloud](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/sql-developer-web-on-the-oracle-cloud/) - 2018-05-10 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Cloud & Infrastructure, Operations & Observability, Development & Drivers; archive: [dbi-services/articles/sql-developer-web-on-the-oracle-cloud.json](dbi-services/articles/sql-developer-web-on-the-oracle-cloud.json)
  Evidence note: SQL Developer Web on Oracle DBaaS worked only after Cloud Tooling was patched to the required dbaascli version as root, because the feature was announced before the tooling caught up.
- [Some ideas about Oracle Database on Docker](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/some-ideas-about-oracle-database-on-docker/) - 2018-05-08 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/some-ideas-about-oracle-database-on-docker.json](dbi-services/articles/some-ideas-about-oracle-database-on-docker.json)
  Evidence note: Faster Oracle Database Docker builds come from isolating the multi-GB install zip and yum prerequisites into cached intermediate layers, so later builds avoid resending the heavy context.
- [ADWC – the hidden gem: Zepplin Notebook](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/adwc-the-hidden-gem-zepplin-notebook/) - 2018-05-04 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/adwc-the-hidden-gem-zepplin-notebook.json](dbi-services/articles/adwc-the-hidden-gem-zepplin-notebook.json)
  Evidence note: Autonomous Data Warehouse's 'Manage Oracle ML Users' menu creates OML_DEVELOPER-role accounts needed to open the Apache-Zeppelin-based Oracle Machine Learning SQL Notebooks, separate from the regular ADMIN login.
- [ADWC – a Docker container to start/stop Oracle Cloud services](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/adwc-a-docker-container-to-startstop-oracle-cloud-services/) - 2018-05-03 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure, Operations & Observability, JSON & Documents; archive: [dbi-services/articles/adwc-a-docker-container-to-startstop-oracle-cloud-services.json](dbi-services/articles/adwc-a-docker-container-to-startstop-oracle-cloud-services.json)
  Evidence note: An Alpine container can install the PSM CLI and trap signals around 'psm ADWC start-service' and 'stop-service', so Autonomous Data Warehouse starts and stops without storing credentials in scripts.
- [ADWC: start/stop with PSM Command Line Interface](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/adwc-startstop-with-psm-command-line-interface/) - 2018-05-02 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Backup & Recovery, Cloud & Infrastructure, Development & Drivers; archive: [dbi-services/articles/adwc-startstop-with-psm-command-line-interface.json](dbi-services/articles/adwc-startstop-with-psm-command-line-interface.json)
  Evidence note: Though undocumented, PSM's ADWC start-service, stop-service, and restart-service commands exist in the CLI's own help output and are the fastest way to control Autonomous Data Warehouse billing by the hour.
- [ADWC: Creation of Autonomous Data Warehouse Cloud service](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/adwc-creation-of-autonomous-database-cloud-service/) - 2018-05-01 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/adwc-creation-of-autonomous-database-cloud-service.json](dbi-services/articles/adwc-creation-of-autonomous-database-cloud-service.json)
  Evidence note: Choosing Bring-Your-Own-License when creating an Autonomous Data Warehouse instance drops the price from $2.50 to $0.48 per OCPU-hour, and stopping the service between connections avoids paying for idle compute time.
- [Covering indexes in Oracle, and branch size](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/covering-indexes-in-oracle-and-branch-size/) - 2018-04-13 | dbi services | Oracle Database, Microsoft SQL Server | Query Optimization, Indexing, Performance & Benchmarking, Internals & Storage; archive: [dbi-services/articles/covering-indexes-in-oracle-and-branch-size.json](dbi-services/articles/covering-indexes-in-oracle-and-branch-size.json)
  Evidence note: Oracle lacks SQL Server's INCLUDE clause, so adding covering columns means appending them to the key itself, which changes clustering factor and branch block size because the full entry is sorted.
- [After IoT, IoP makes its way to the database](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/after-iot-iop-makes-its-way-to-the-database/) - 2018-04-01 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/after-iot-iop-makes-its-way-to-the-database.json](dbi-services/articles/after-iot-iop-makes-its-way-to-the-database.json)
  Evidence note: Querying V$SQLFN_METADATA in Oracle 18c on the Oracle Cloud reveals an undocumented TO_DOG_YEAR SQL function tagged version V13, alongside newer JSON_MERGEPATCH and ROUND_TIES_TO_EVEN function entries.
- [Docker: efficiently building images for large software](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/docker-efficiently-building-images-for-large-software/) - 2018-03-31 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/docker-efficiently-building-images-for-large-software.json](dbi-services/articles/docker-efficiently-building-images-for-large-software.json)
  Evidence note: Testing add, copy, rename, and append Dockerfile steps on an XFS-backed Docker daemon shows each RUN layer preserves prior file states, so a removed or renamed 100MB file still consumes image layer space.
- [Docker-CE on Oracle Enterprise Linux 7](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/docker-ce-on-oracle-enterprise-linux-7/) - 2018-03-29 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure, Events & Community; archive: [dbi-services/articles/docker-ce-on-oracle-enterprise-linux-7.json](dbi-services/articles/docker-ce-on-oracle-enterprise-linux-7.json)
  Evidence note: On Oracle Linux 7.4, the ol7_addons repository only offers the six-month-old docker-engine 17.06 package, so installing the actively updated Docker CE 17.12 requires adding CentOS's docker-ce repository instead.
- [Result cache invalidation caused by DML locks](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/result-cache-invalidation-caused-by-dml-locks/) - 2018-03-18 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Transactions & Consistency, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/result-cache-invalidation-caused-by-dml-locks.json](dbi-services/articles/result-cache-invalidation-caused-by-dml-locks.json)
  Evidence note: A DELETE statement matching zero rows still invalidates a dependent Oracle Result Cache entry, because the statement still acquires a Row Exclusive TM-mode lock declaring intent to modify that table.
- [18c PDB switchover](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/18c-pdb-switchover/) - 2018-03-11 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Backup & Recovery, Cloud & Infrastructure, Security; archive: [dbi-services/articles/18c-pdb-switchover.json](dbi-services/articles/18c-pdb-switchover.json)
  Evidence note: Oracle 18c's PDB-level switchover, built on refreshable PDBs, failed with ORA-46697 when mandatory TDE encryption was enabled on the source CDB, since 'alter pluggable database' offers no keystore-password option for it.
- [Enabled, Accepted, Fixed SQL Plan Baselines](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/enabled-accepted-fixed-sql-plan-baselines/) - 2018-03-09 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Indexing, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/enabled-accepted-fixed-sql-plan-baselines.json](dbi-services/articles/enabled-accepted-fixed-sql-plan-baselines.json)
  Evidence note: Testing 8 index-cost variations against a SQL Plan Baseline confirms Oracle only executes the currently Accepted plan among Enabled ones, ignoring cheaper newly loaded plans until they are manually accepted or fixed.
- [18c new Lost Write Protection](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/18c-new-lost-write-protection/) - 2018-03-03 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Internals & Storage, Backup & Recovery; archive: [dbi-services/articles/18c-new-lost-write-protection.json](dbi-services/articles/18c-new-lost-write-protection.json)
  Evidence note: Oracle 18c introduces a standalone Lost Write Protection tablespace that stores per-block SCNs to detect a silently dropped disk write, without needing a Data Guard standby as the earlier 11g mechanism did.
- [18c dbms_xplan note about failed SQL Plan Baseline](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/18c-dbms_xplan-note-about-failed-sql-plan-baseline/) - 2018-02-27 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Indexing, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/18c-dbms_xplan-note-about-failed-sql-plan-baseline.json](dbi-services/articles/18c-dbms_xplan-note-about-failed-sql-plan-baseline.json)
  Evidence note: Since Oracle 18c, DBMS_XPLAN.DISPLAY_CURSOR adds an explicit note when the optimizer failed to use a SQL Plan Baseline for a statement, after a data model change made the originally accepted plan unreproducible.
- [A free persistent Google Cloud service with Oracle XE](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/a-free-persistent-google-cloud-service-with-oracle-xe/) - 2018-02-26 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Indexing, Internals & Storage, Migration & Compatibility, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/a-free-persistent-google-cloud-service-with-oracle-xe.json](dbi-services/articles/a-free-persistent-google-cloud-service-with-oracle-xe.json)
  Evidence note: Google Cloud Shell's persistent home directory with 5GB storage lets Oracle Database XE 11gR2 survive between sessions on its free VM, installed by splitting the RPM zip file to work around the shell's upload size limit.
- [18c, Cloud First and Cloud Only features: think differently](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/18c-cloud-first-and-cloud-only-features-think-differently/) - 2018-02-26 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/18c-cloud-first-and-cloud-only-features-think-differently.json](dbi-services/articles/18c-cloud-first-and-cloud-only-features-think-differently.json)
  Evidence note: Oracle 18c's alert.log logs 'capabilities requested' versus 'detected' entries, revealing that some new features check for Oracle Cloud or Engineered Systems platform capabilities and never run identically on-premises.
- [PDB upgrade from 12c to 18c](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/pdb-upgrade-from-12c-to-18c/) - 2018-02-25 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Migration & Compatibility, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/pdb-upgrade-from-12c-to-18c.json](dbi-services/articles/pdb-upgrade-from-12c-to-18c.json)
  Evidence note: Unplugging a 12.2.0.1 PDB and plugging it into an 18.1 CDB still takes as long to upgrade the PDB dictionary as upgrading CDB$ROOT itself, despite the reduced metadata expected from multitenant's metadata-link design.
- [ODA Lite: What is this ‘odacli’ repository?](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oda-lite-what-is-this-odacli-repository/) - 2018-02-23 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Operations & Observability, Development & Drivers; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oda-lite-what-is-this-odacli-repository.json](dbi-services/articles/oda-lite-what-is-this-odacli-repository.json)
  Evidence note: ODA Lite's odacli configuration and operations are stored in an embedded Apache Derby database at /opt/oracle/dcs/repo, readable offline through SQuirreL SQL Client's JDBC driver once the DCS agent is stopped.
- [18c Read Only Oracle Home](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/18c-read-only-oracle-home/) - 2018-02-18 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Migration & Compatibility, Cloud & Infrastructure, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/18c-read-only-oracle-home.json](dbi-services/articles/18c-read-only-oracle-home.json)
  Evidence note: Oracle 18c's roohctl -enable moved beyond the old orabasetab flag: it relocates mutable config, logs, and database files outside the immutable Oracle Home, which fits container image usage.
- [(DESCRIPTION_LIST=(DESCRIPTION=(ADDRESS_LIST=(FAILOVER=YES)(LOAD_BALANCE=NO)](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/description_listdescriptionaddress_listfailoveryesload_balanceno/) - 2018-02-15 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Backup & Recovery, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/description_listdescriptionaddress_listfailoveryesload_balanceno.json](dbi-services/articles/description_listdescriptionaddress_listfailoveryesload_balanceno.json)
  Evidence note: strace on failed connect() calls shows DESCRIPTION_LIST load balancing randomly rotates between DESCRIPTION entries, while an ADDRESS_LIST inside one DESCRIPTION walks addresses in another order.
- [Full page logging in Postgres and Oracle](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/full-page-logging-in-postgres-and-oracle/) - 2018-02-14 | dbi services | Oracle Database, PostgreSQL | Performance & Benchmarking, Transactions & Consistency, Internals & Storage, Backup & Recovery; archive: [dbi-services/articles/full-page-logging-in-postgres-and-oracle.json](dbi-services/articles/full-page-logging-in-postgres-and-oracle.json)
  Evidence note: With full_page_writes=on, PostgreSQL logs the whole 8KB page the first time it is dirtied after a checkpoint, behaving like Oracle begin-backup mode rather than normal Oracle redo.
- [V$MYSTAT delta values](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/vmystat-delta-values/) - 2018-02-12 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/vmystat-delta-values.json](dbi-services/articles/vmystat-delta-values.json)
  Evidence note: A pair of generated SQL scripts capture V$MYSTAT statistic deltas between two points in a session and display them as one labeled row instead of requiring two separate queries and a manual subtraction.
- [Server process name in Postgres and Oracle](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/server-process-name-in-postgres-and-oracle/) - 2018-02-09 | dbi services | Oracle Database, PostgreSQL | Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/server-process-name-in-postgres-and-oracle.json](dbi-services/articles/server-process-name-in-postgres-and-oracle.json)
  Evidence note: Oracle background processes all appear as the generic 'oracle' executable in ps, while PostgreSQL exposes each backend's database, client address, and active command directly in its process title.
- [12cR2 PDB archive](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12cr2-pdb-archive/) - 2018-02-07 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Backup & Recovery, Operations & Observability, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12cr2-pdb-archive.json](dbi-services/articles/12cr2-pdb-archive.json)
  Evidence note: An October 2017 Release Update fixes 12.2's PDB Archive feature, a zipped XML descriptor plus datafiles for PDB transport, so it now also works when the PDB's datafiles reside on ASM instead of only a regular filesystem.
- [JAN18: Database 11gR2 PSU, 12cR1 ProactiveBP, 12cR2 RU](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/jan18-database-11gr2-psu-12cr1-proactivebp-12cr2-ru/) - 2018-02-06 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/jan18-database-11gr2-psu-12cr1-proactivebp-12cr2-ru.json](dbi-services/articles/jan18-database-11gr2-psu-12cr1-proactivebp-12cr2-ru.json)
  Evidence note: Oracle patch terminology differs by release: 11.2.0.4 uses a PSU, 12.1.0.2 recommends a Proactive Bundle Patch over its plain PSU, and 12.2.0.1 uses a Release Update, all traced to My Oracle Support Doc ID 756671.1.
- [Multitenant, PDB, ‘save state’, services and standby databases](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/multitenant-pdb-save-state-services-and-standby-databases/) - 2018-02-05 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Security; archive: [dbi-services/articles/multitenant-pdb-save-state-services-and-standby-databases.json](dbi-services/articles/multitenant-pdb-save-state-services-and-standby-databases.json)
  Evidence note: Connecting through a PDB's own name as its default service, instead of a custom service, breaks down once a standby database registers the identically-named PDB service to the same listener as the primary.
- [Result Cache: when *not* to use it](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/result-cache-when-not-to-use-it/) - 2018-01-29 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking; archive: [dbi-services/articles/result-cache-when-not-to-use-it.json](dbi-services/articles/result-cache-when-not-to-use-it.json)
  Evidence note: Result Cache pays off for repeated calls with the same argument; using many distinct IDs instead creates contention, and DML invalidation makes each miss more expensive than the uncached path.
- [Testing Oracle SQL online](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/testing-oracle-sql-online/) - 2018-01-27 | dbi services | Oracle Database, PostgreSQL, Microsoft SQL Server, MySQL | Query Optimization, Data Modeling & SQL, JSON & Documents; archive: [dbi-services/articles/testing-oracle-sql-online.json](dbi-services/articles/testing-oracle-sql-online.json)
  Evidence note: SQL Fiddle, Rextester, db<>fiddle, and Oracle Live SQL run free online Oracle instances limited to the older free 11gR2 XE version, because only freely licensed Oracle software can legally back a shared public service.
- [Explain Plan format](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/explain-plan-format/) - 2018-01-24 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/explain-plan-format.json](dbi-services/articles/explain-plan-format.json)
  Evidence note: DBMS_XPLAN's format string groups columns under BASIC, TYPICAL, ALL, and ADVANCED levels, and options like 'allstats last' add per-execution row and buffer statistics on top of the default typical plan display.
- [Unplug an Encrypted PDB (ORA-46680: master keys of the container database must be exported)](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/unplug-an-encrypted-pdb-ora-46680-master-keys-of-the-container-database-must-be-exported/) - 2018-01-20 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure, Security; archive: [dbi-services/articles/unplug-an-encrypted-pdb-ora-46680-master-keys-of-the-container-database-must-be-exported.json](dbi-services/articles/unplug-an-encrypted-pdb-ora-46680-master-keys-of-the-container-database-must-be-exported.json)
  Evidence note: Unplugging a TDE-encrypted PDB that raises ORA-46680 requires first exporting the PDB's own master key from V$ENCRYPTION_KEYS while it is open, since only the CDB-level wallet key gets exported automatically.
- [Spectre and Meltdown on Oracle Public Cloud UEK – LIO](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/spectre-and-meltdown-on-oracle-public-cloud-uek/) - 2018-01-14 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/spectre-and-meltdown-on-oracle-public-cloud-uek.json](dbi-services/articles/spectre-and-meltdown-on-oracle-public-cloud-uek.json)
  Evidence note: Disabling KPTI, IBRS, and IBPB mitigations on Oracle Cloud UEK gives only about 5% higher single-session SLOB logical reads, but the gain becomes more visible once two or more concurrent SLOB sessions run together.
- [Spectre/Meltdown on Oracle Public Cloud UEK – PIO](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/spectremeltdown-on-oracle-public-cloud-uek-pio/) - 2018-01-13 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/spectremeltdown-on-oracle-public-cloud-uek-pio.json](dbi-services/articles/spectremeltdown-on-oracle-public-cloud-uek-pio.json)
  Evidence note: Against the logical-read expectation, SLOB tests on Oracle Cloud UEK showed higher physical-read IOPS with Spectre/Meltdown KPTI enabled than with nopti, across several concurrency levels.
- [Spectre and Meltdown, Oracle Database, AWS, SLOB](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/spectre-and-meltdown-oracle-database-aws-slob/) - 2018-01-09 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Transactions & Consistency, Migration & Compatibility, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/spectre-and-meltdown-oracle-database-aws-slob.json](dbi-services/articles/spectre-and-meltdown-oracle-database-aws-slob.json)
  Evidence note: Comparing SLOB cached-read CPU performance on an AWS M4.xlarge instance before and after Amazon applied Spectre and Meltdown microcode patches shows little degradation for a workload with no heavy system-call activity.
- [Keep your orapw password file secure](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/keep-your-orapw-password-file-secure/) - 2018-01-05 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Security, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/keep-your-orapw-password-file-secure.json](dbi-services/articles/keep-your-orapw-password-file-secure.json)
  Evidence note: Giving a schema user read access to the ORACLE_HOME/dbs directory object lets that user BFILE-read the orapw password file and reuse its hash for remote authentication without knowing the password.

### 2017

- [12c Multitenant internals: PDB_PLUG_IN_VIOLATIONS](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12c-multitenant-internals-pdb_plug_in_violations/) - 2017-12-30 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12c-multitenant-internals-pdb_plug_in_violations.json](dbi-services/articles/12c-multitenant-internals-pdb_plug_in_violations.json)
  Evidence note: When a common-user DDL statement fails on a closed PDB, Oracle records the failure in PDB_PLUG_IN_VIOLATIONS rather than blocking the CDB$ROOT change, then replays the DDL against that PDB the next time it opens.
- [12c Multitenant internals: PDB replay DDL for common users](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12c-multitenant-internals-pdb-replay-ddl-for-common-users/) - 2017-12-29 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Transactions & Consistency, Internals & Storage, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12c-multitenant-internals-pdb-replay-ddl-for-common-users.json](dbi-services/articles/12c-multitenant-internals-pdb-replay-ddl-for-common-users.json)
  Evidence note: Common users, roles, and profiles created in CDB$ROOT are copied to each PDB by replaying DDL from PDB_SYNC$, not by metadata links, and Oracle tracks progress with a per-container REPLAY#.
- [Drop PDB including datafiles may keep files open](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/drop-pdb-including-datafiles-may-keep-files-open/) - 2017-12-25 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/drop-pdb-including-datafiles-may-keep-files-open.json](dbi-services/articles/drop-pdb-including-datafiles-may-keep-files-open.json)
  Evidence note: Dropping a PDB with INCLUDING DATAFILES can leave its datafile inodes open by parallel background processes from an earlier plug-in step, so filesystem space is not reclaimed until those processes close the file handles.
- [12cR2 Subquery Elimination](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12cr2-subquery-elimination/) - 2017-12-21 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12cr2-subquery-elimination.json](dbi-services/articles/12cr2-subquery-elimination.json)
  Evidence note: Oracle 12cR2 adds Subquery Elimination, removing an EXISTS or IN semi-join subquery, and its extra full table scan, whenever the optimizer proves the subquery reads the same table with no filtering predicate.
- [Oracle docker image from docker store](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-docker-image-from-docker-store/) - 2017-12-15 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-docker-image-from-docker-store.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-docker-image-from-docker-store.json)
  Evidence note: Oracle Database images from Docker Store needed only a one-time OTN license acceptance in the Store UI, replacing the older model of distributing sealed installation media.
- [impdp logtime=all metrics=y and 12cR2 parallel metadata](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/impdp-logtimeall-metricsy-and-12cr2-parallel-metadata/) - 2017-11-28 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Operations & Observability, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/impdp-logtimeall-metricsy-and-12cr2-parallel-metadata.json](dbi-services/articles/impdp-logtimeall-metricsy-and-12cr2-parallel-metadata.json)
  Evidence note: With LOGTIME=ALL and METRICS=Y, impdp timestamps each completed object type and reports per-worker counts, making it clear 12cR2 can process metadata such as TABLE objects in parallel.
- [12c Multitenant Internals: compiling system package from PDB](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12c-multitenant-internals-compiling-system-package-from-pdb/) - 2017-11-22 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Cloud & Infrastructure, Events & Community; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12c-multitenant-internals-compiling-system-package-from-pdb.json](dbi-services/articles/12c-multitenant-internals-compiling-system-package-from-pdb.json)
  Evidence note: Tracing ALTER PACKAGE DBMS_SYSTEM COMPILE from a PDB shows Oracle switches to CDB$ROOT to read SOURCE$, yet some system DDL issued from a PDB is silently reduced to a no-op.
- [Unstructured vs. structured](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/unstructed-vs-structured/) - 2017-11-18 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Backup & Recovery; archive: [dbi-services/articles/unstructed-vs-structured.json](dbi-services/articles/unstructed-vs-structured.json)
  Evidence note: Tracing DBMS_BACKUP_RESTORE.REFRESHAGEDFILES in the recovery area exposed an archivelog deletion policy bug: swapping 'BACKED UP ... TIMES TO' and 'APPLIED' changes what Oracle reclaims.
- [CBO, FIRST_ROWS and VIEW misestimate](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/cbo-first_rows-and-view-misestimate/) - 2017-11-17 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Indexing, Migration & Compatibility, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/cbo-first_rows-and-view-misestimate.json](dbi-services/articles/cbo-first_rows-and-view-misestimate.json)
  Evidence note: A FIRST_ROWS(10) query against a view containing an ORDER BY, wrapped in a subquery calling a PL/SQL function, ended up calling that function for a million rows instead of ten after a migration from 10g to 12c.
- [Dynamic Sampling vs. Extended Statistics](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/dynamic-sampling-vs-extended-statistics/) - 2017-11-14 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/dynamic-sampling-vs-extended-statistics.json](dbi-services/articles/dynamic-sampling-vs-extended-statistics.json)
  Evidence note: With optimizer_dynamic_sampling=4 and adaptive stats off, a case-insensitive UPPER() filter underestimated rows in a large table and pushed the optimizer toward an expensive nested-loop plan.
- [Multitenant internals: INT$ and INT$INT$ views](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/multitenant-internals-int-and-intint-views/) - 2017-11-05 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Migration & Compatibility, Cloud & Infrastructure, Events & Community; archive: [dbi-services/articles/multitenant-internals-int-and-intint-views.json](dbi-services/articles/multitenant-internals-int-and-intint-views.json)
  Evidence note: Oracle's doubled INT$INT$ dictionary views, such as INT$INT$DBA_CONSTRAINTS, merge CDB$ROOT and PDB data transparently for common views, layered on top of the simpler single INT$ extended-data-view mechanism.
- [Multitenant dictionary: what is consolidated and what is not](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/multitenant-dictionary-what-is-consolidated-and-what-is-not/) - 2017-11-05 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Indexing, Internals & Storage, Migration & Compatibility, Events & Community; archive: [dbi-services/articles/multitenant-dictionary-what-is-consolidated-and-what-is-not.json](dbi-services/articles/multitenant-dictionary-what-is-consolidated-and-what-is-not.json)
  Evidence note: Contrary to Oracle documentation stating Oracle-supplied objects live only in the root, system PL/SQL package source is root-only via metadata links, but system table definitions like COL$ are replicated into every PDB.
- [Quick history on database growth](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/quick-history-on-database-growth/) - 2017-11-02 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/quick-history-on-database-growth.json](dbi-services/articles/quick-history-on-database-growth.json)
  Evidence note: Joining DBA_HIST_SEG_STAT to DBA_HIST_SEG_STAT_OBJ, even in Standard Edition without Diagnostic Pack, flags segments whose hourly growth exceeds 1% of database size so abnormal expansion stands out.
- [Dbvisit replicate – SQL Developer chart](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/dbvisit-replicate-sql-developer-chart/) - 2017-10-31 | dbi services | Database agnostic | High Availability & Replication; archive: [dbi-services/articles/dbvisit-replicate-sql-developer-chart.json](dbi-services/articles/dbvisit-replicate-sql-developer-chart.json)
  Evidence note: A custom SQL Developer chart report queries DBVREP.DBRSCOMMON_LAG_STATS to plot Dbvisit Replicate's MINE-to-APPLY replication lag in minutes over time, visualizing a deliberately introduced replication stop.
- [Max PDBs in Standard Edition](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/max-pdbs-in-standard-edition/) - 2017-10-26 | dbi services | Oracle Database | General Database; archive: [dbi-services/articles/max-pdbs-in-standard-edition.json](dbi-services/articles/max-pdbs-in-standard-edition.json)
  Evidence note: In 12.2 Standard Edition unexpectedly allows creating a second PDB via CON_ID>3, unlike 12.1's ORA-65010 block, so admins must explicitly set MAX_PDBS to 1.
- [Database is more than persistence. It is shared. My thoughts about Microservice Database.](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/database-is-more-than-persistence-it-is-shared-my-thoughts-about-microservice-database/) - 2017-10-22 | dbi services | Database agnostic | Transactions & Consistency, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/database-is-more-than-persistence-it-is-shared-my-thoughts-about-microservice-database.json](dbi-services/articles/database-is-more-than-persistence-it-is-shared-my-thoughts-about-microservice-database.json)
  Evidence note: Argues that treating a database as an isolated per-developer 'persistence layer' container misses the point: sharing across concurrent users, not persistence, is what makes it hard.
- [Archivelog deletion policy on Data Guard configuration](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/archivelog-deletion-policy-on-data-guard-configuration/) - 2017-10-22 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Backup & Recovery, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/archivelog-deletion-policy-on-data-guard-configuration.json](dbi-services/articles/archivelog-deletion-policy-on-data-guard-configuration.json)
  Evidence note: Shows that CONFIGURE ARCHIVELOG DELETION POLICY clause order matters: putting BACKED UP 1 TIMES TO DISK before APPLIED ON ALL STANDBY leaves archivelogs unreclaimable and fills the recovery area.
- [VirtualBox 5.2 exports the VM to the Oracle Cloud](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/virtualbox-5-2-exports-the-vm-to-the-oracle-cloud/) - 2017-10-21 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/virtualbox-5-2-exports-the-vm-to-the-oracle-cloud.json](dbi-services/articles/virtualbox-5-2-exports-the-vm-to-the-oracle-cloud.json)
  Evidence note: VirtualBox 5.2 can export a VM directly to an Oracle Cloud Compute image, producing per-disk tar.gz files containing raw .img disks later uploaded to Compute Classic.

- [ODC Appreciation Day : Javascript in the database](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/odc-appreciation-day-javascript-in-the-database/) - 2017-10-10 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Development & Drivers, Events & Community; archive: [dbi-services/articles/odc-appreciation-day-javascript-in-the-database.json](dbi-services/articles/odc-appreciation-day-javascript-in-the-database.json)
  Evidence note: Explains why Oracle's Multilingual Engine runs JavaScript compiled rather than interpreted in the database, correcting the author's earlier assumption that it mirrors browser-side execution.
- [Autonomous Database](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/autonomous-database/) - 2017-10-08 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Distributed Databases, Migration & Compatibility, Cloud & Infrastructure, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/autonomous-database.json](dbi-services/articles/autonomous-database.json)

- [Oracle 18c RU and RUR for pioneers and followers](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-18c-ru-and-rur-for-pioneers-and-followers/) - 2017-10-03 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Migration & Compatibility, Security, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-18c-ru-and-rur-for-pioneers-and-followers.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-18c-ru-and-rur-for-pioneers-and-followers.json)
