- [ODA X7](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oda-x7/) - 2017-10-03 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oda-x7.json](dbi-services/articles/oda-x7.json)
  Evidence note: Compares ODA X7-2S and X7-2M hardware specs against the X6 generation, noting the Small model adds KVM virtualization while cores, memory, and NVMe flash capacity increase on the Medium model.
- [“_suppress_identifiers_on_dupkey” – the SAP workaround for bad design](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/_suppress_identifiers_on_dupkey-the-sap-workaround-for-bad-design/) - 2017-09-29 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Indexing, Transactions & Consistency, Internals & Storage, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/_suppress_identifiers_on_dupkey-the-sap-workaround-for-bad-design.json](dbi-services/articles/_suppress_identifiers_on_dupkey-the-sap-workaround-for-bad-design.json)
  Evidence note: Explains why the insert-then-catch-dup_val_on_index-then-update upsert pattern is unsafe under concurrency and why Oracle added an underscore parameter to accommodate SAP's use of it.
- [When deterministic function is not](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/when-deterministic-function-is-not/) - 2017-09-26 | dbi services | Oracle Database, PostgreSQL | Indexing, Performance & Benchmarking, Internals & Storage; archive: [dbi-services/articles/when-deterministic-function-is-not.json](dbi-services/articles/when-deterministic-function-is-not.json)
  Evidence note: Tests what happens when a function-based index is declared on a non-deterministic function, contrasting Oracle's in-place row updates with Postgres HOT/WARM's insert-and-mark-stale MVCC design.
- [Am I a DBA 3.0 or just an SQL*DBA?](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/am-i-a-dba-3-0-or-just-an-sqldba/) - 2017-09-24 | dbi services | Oracle Database, Db2 | Cloud & Infrastructure, Events & Community; archive: [dbi-services/articles/am-i-a-dba-3-0-or-just-an-sqldba.json](dbi-services/articles/am-i-a-dba-3-0-or-just-an-sqldba.json)
  Evidence note: Challenges sweeping 'DBA 3.0' and 'death of the DBA' narratives by walking through the author's own career since 1994 to show how the role varies by company size and region.
- [Wrong result with multitenant, dba_contraints and current_schema](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/wrong-result-with-multitenant-dba_contraints-and-current_schema/) - 2017-09-23 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Migration & Compatibility, Cloud & Infrastructure, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/wrong-result-with-multitenant-dba_contraints-and-current_schema.json](dbi-services/articles/wrong-result-with-multitenant-dba_contraints-and-current_schema.json)
  Evidence note: Reproduces a dictionary bug where querying DBA_CONSTRAINTS after ALTER SESSION SET CURRENT_SCHEMA in a PDB returns constraints for the wrong owner, a multitenant dictionary-separation side effect.
- [Active Data Guard services in Multitenant](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/active-data-guard-services-in-multitenant/) - 2017-09-15 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/active-data-guard-services-in-multitenant.json](dbi-services/articles/active-data-guard-services-in-multitenant.json)
  Evidence note: Shows that a PDB registers the same default service name on both primary and standby, so DBAs must create distinct services per PDB to avoid two databases sharing one listener registration.
- [12c Access Control Lists](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12c-access-control-lists/) - 2017-09-10 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Security; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12c-access-control-lists.json](dbi-services/articles/12c-access-control-lists.json)
  Evidence note: Walks through the simplified 12c syntax for granting network ACL privileges, showing the ORA-24247 error a non-SYS user hits with UTL_TCP before the access control list is assigned.
- [Create constraints in your datawarehouse – why and how](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/create-constraints-in-your-datawarehouse-why-and-how/) - 2017-09-08 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization; archive: [dbi-services/articles/create-constraints-in-your-datawarehouse-why-and-how.json](dbi-services/articles/create-constraints-in-your-datawarehouse-why-and-how.json)
  Evidence note: Demonstrates on a 10-million-row fact table with three dimensions why declaring RELY NOVALIDATE foreign keys in a data warehouse avoids ETL overhead while still helping the optimizer.
- [impdp content=metadata_only locks the stats](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/impdp-contentmetadata_only-locks-the-stats/) - 2017-09-06 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Indexing, Transactions & Consistency, Migration & Compatibility, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/impdp-contentmetadata_only-locks-the-stats.json](dbi-services/articles/impdp-contentmetadata_only-locks-the-stats.json)
  Evidence note: Confirms that Data Pump import with content=metadata_only has locked table statistics since 10.2, stopping the auto stats gathering job from overwriting them with zero rows after migration.
- [12c dbms_stats.gather_table_stats on GTT do not commit](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12c-dbms_stats-gather_table_stats-on-gtt-do-not-commit/) - 2017-09-05 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Transactions & Consistency, Operations & Observability, Events & Community; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12c-dbms_stats-gather_table_stats-on-gtt-do-not-commit.json](dbi-services/articles/12c-dbms_stats-gather_table_stats-on-gtt-do-not-commit.json)
  Evidence note: Tests whether DBMS_STATS.GATHER_TABLE_STATS commits a transaction when run on a global temporary table with private session statistics, correcting an 11g assumption carried into 12c.
- [When PDB name conflicts with CDB name](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/when-pdb-name-conflicts-with-cdb-name/) - 2017-09-03 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Cloud & Infrastructure, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/when-pdb-name-conflicts-with-cdb-name.json](dbi-services/articles/when-pdb-name-conflicts-with-cdb-name.json)
  Evidence note: Recommends naming a PDB the way you would name a standalone database, and warns against giving a single-tenant CDB and its one PDB the same name because of listener service-name collisions.
- [Postgres vs. Oracle access paths XI – Sample Scan](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-xi/) - 2017-08-26 | dbi services | Oracle Database, PostgreSQL | Internals & Storage, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-xi.json](dbi-services/articles/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-xi.json)
  Evidence note: Runs a Postgres Sample Scan with TABLESAMPLE BERNOULLI(5) on a 10000-row table and shows it still reads all 1429 pages, because random sampling needs to know the row count before picking 5%.
- [Postgres vs. Oracle access paths X – Update](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-x-update/) - 2017-08-24 | dbi services | Oracle Database, PostgreSQL | Indexing, Internals & Storage; archive: [dbi-services/articles/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-x-update.json](dbi-services/articles/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-x-update.json)
  Evidence note: Compares an Oracle ROWID and a Postgres TID for the same row, decoding file, block, and row offset from each, before showing how an UPDATE changes the row's physical location.
- [Postgres vs. Oracle access paths IX – Tid Scan](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-ix/) - 2017-08-23 | dbi services | Oracle Database, PostgreSQL | Indexing, Internals & Storage; archive: [dbi-services/articles/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-ix.json](dbi-services/articles/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-ix.json)
  Evidence note: Decomposes how Oracle's TABLE ACCESS BY ROWID and Postgres's equivalent Tid Scan fetch a row once the index entry supplies its 10-byte ROWID or its (block,offset) TID.
- [Improving Statspack Experience](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/improving-statspack-experience/) - 2017-08-23 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Events & Community; archive: [dbi-services/articles/improving-statspack-experience.json](dbi-services/articles/improving-statspack-experience.json)
  Evidence note: Shares a DBMS_SCHEDULER script, contributed by a colleague, that replaces the author's older DBMS_JOB-based statspack.snap and statspack.purge scheduling across each RAC instance.
- [Bequeath connect to PDB: set container in logon trigger?](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/bequeath-connect-to-pdb-set-container-in-logon-trigger/) - 2017-08-23 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Migration & Compatibility, Cloud & Infrastructure, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/bequeath-connect-to-pdb-set-container-in-logon-trigger.json](dbi-services/articles/bequeath-connect-to-pdb-set-container-in-logon-trigger.json)
  Evidence note: Warns against the workaround of creating a common user with a logon trigger that runs ALTER SESSION SET CONTAINER to fake a bequeath connection into a PDB, since it needs a common_user_prefix change.
- [Postgres vs. Oracle access paths VIII – Index Scan and Filter](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-viii/) - 2017-08-20 | dbi services | Oracle Database, PostgreSQL | Indexing; archive: [dbi-services/articles/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-viii.json](dbi-services/articles/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-viii.json)
  Evidence note: Adds a condition that eliminates all rows via the index but still needs a table lookup for extra columns, comparing Postgres's Index Scan with Filter to the equivalent Oracle access path.
- [Get trace file from server to client](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/get-trace-file-from-server-to-client/) - 2017-08-20 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Transactions & Consistency; archive: [dbi-services/articles/get-trace-file-from-server-to-client.json](dbi-services/articles/get-trace-file-from-server-to-client.json)
  Evidence note: Shares a SQL*Plus script that reads a trace file through UTL_FILE and DBMS_OUTPUT and spools it to the client, useful when the DBA has no filesystem access to the server.
- [ODA X6 2S 2M 2L HA: Small Medium Large and High Availability](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oda-x6-small-medium-large-and-high-availability/) - 2017-08-14 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Internals & Storage; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oda-x6-small-medium-large-and-high-availability.json](dbi-services/articles/oda-x6-small-medium-large-and-high-availability.json)
  Evidence note: Lists the core counts, rack units, and USB/serial console details distinguishing the ODA X6-2S, 2M, 2L, and 2-node HA appliance models.
- [Postgres vs. Oracle access paths VI – Index Scan](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-vi/) - 2017-08-09 | dbi services | Oracle Database, PostgreSQL | Query Optimization, Indexing, Internals & Storage; archive: [dbi-services/articles/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-vi.json](dbi-services/articles/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-vi.json)
  Evidence note: Runs the same one-row lookup after VACUUM to show Postgres switching from Index Only Scan to Index Scan once an unindexed column is selected, forcing an extra heap fetch.
- [Postgres vs. Oracle access paths V – FIRST ROWS and MIN/MAX](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-v/) - 2017-08-08 | dbi services | Oracle Database, PostgreSQL | Query Optimization, Indexing; archive: [dbi-services/articles/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-v.json](dbi-services/articles/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-v.json)
  Evidence note: Compares Oracle's FIRST_ROWS costing and Postgres's startup-cost model for queries that only need the first rows, using an ORDER BY on an indexed column to avoid a blocking sort.
- [Postgres vs. Oracle access paths IV – Order By and Index](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-iv/) - 2017-08-05 | dbi services | Oracle Database, PostgreSQL | Indexing, Transactions & Consistency; archive: [dbi-services/articles/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-iv.json](dbi-services/articles/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-iv.json)
  Evidence note: Extends an earlier Index Full Scan demo with an ORDER BY, contrasting how Oracle threads visibility checks through undo with Postgres's index-order-only leaf traversal.
- [Exadata Capacity on Demand and Elastic Rack](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/exadata-capacity-on-demand-and-elastic-rack/) - 2017-08-04 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/exadata-capacity-on-demand-and-elastic-rack.json](dbi-services/articles/exadata-capacity-on-demand-and-elastic-rack.json)
  Evidence note: Tabulates the minimum, maximum, and increment core counts for Capacity on Demand across Exadata X2 through X7 models, sourced from the OEDA configuration file, including 1/8 Rack exceptions.
- [Postgres vs. Oracle access paths III – Partial Index](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-iii/) - 2017-08-03 | dbi services | Oracle Database, PostgreSQL | Query Optimization, Indexing; archive: [dbi-services/articles/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-iii.json](dbi-services/articles/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-iii.json)
  Evidence note: Shows that summing an indexed column triggers an Index Only Scan in Postgres but a full table scan in Oracle unless hinted, because Oracle index entries alone can't satisfy visibility checks.
- [Postgres vs. Oracle access paths II – Index Only Scan](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-ii/) - 2017-08-02 | dbi services | Oracle Database, PostgreSQL | Query Optimization, Indexing, Transactions & Consistency; archive: [dbi-services/articles/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-ii.json](dbi-services/articles/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-ii.json)
  Evidence note: Explains that Postgres MVCC visibility applies only to table tuples while Oracle's applies to index blocks too, which is why an index-only query can still force Oracle into a sequential scan.
- [Oracle Cloud: script to stop all PaaS services](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-cloud-script-to-stop-all-paas-services/) - 2017-08-02 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure, JSON & Documents; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-cloud-script-to-stop-all-paas-services.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-cloud-script-to-stop-all-paas-services.json)
  Evidence note: Provides a curl-and-jq shell script against the Oracle Cloud REST API that queries all non-stopped PaaS service instances and stops them, useful for cutting metered cloud costs overnight.
- [Postgres vs. Oracle access paths – intro](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-0/) - 2017-08-01 | dbi services | Oracle Database, PostgreSQL | Query Optimization, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-0.json](dbi-services/articles/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-0.json)
  Evidence note: Opens a series comparing Postgres query-planning terminology, pages, tuples, relations, with Oracle's blocks, rows, tables, stating the intent to learn Postgres from an Oracle DBA's perspective.
- [Postgres vs. Oracle access paths I – Seq Scan](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-i/) - 2017-08-01 | dbi services | Oracle Database, PostgreSQL | Query Optimization, Indexing, Internals & Storage, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-i.json](dbi-services/articles/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-i.json)
  Evidence note: Creates a 10000-row, 1429-page demo table to show Postgres's seq_page_cost constant driving the cost of a full sequential scan, kicking off a comparison series with Oracle access paths.
- [Postgres unique constraint](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/postgres-unique-constraint/) - 2017-08-01 | dbi services | Oracle Database, PostgreSQL | High Availability & Replication, Data Modeling & SQL, Events & Community; archive: [dbi-services/articles/postgres-unique-constraint.json](dbi-services/articles/postgres-unique-constraint.json)
  Evidence note: Uses a primary-key update tweet as a starting point to examine how Postgres and Oracle each validate a unique constraint when two rows swap values within a single transaction.
- [PostgreSQL on Cygwin](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/postgresql-on-cygwin/) - 2017-08-01 | dbi services | Oracle Database, PostgreSQL | Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/postgresql-on-cygwin.json](dbi-services/articles/postgresql-on-cygwin.json)
  Evidence note: Installs PostgreSQL through the Cygwin package manager on Windows 10 rather than a Linux VM, covering the pg_config dependency and setting up Cygserver to run it as a Windows service.
- [Display Data Guard configuration in SQL Developer](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/display-data-guard-configuration-in-sql-developer/) - 2017-08-01 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/display-data-guard-configuration-in-sql-developer.json](dbi-services/articles/display-data-guard-configuration-in-sql-developer.json)
  Evidence note: Shows SQL Developer 17.2's new DBA-view Data Guard display and uses it to illustrate cascading redo shipping across two standby databases with independent LogXptMode settings.
- [Postgres vs. Oracle access paths VII – Bitmap Index Scan](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-vii/) - 2017-07-31 | dbi services | Oracle Database, PostgreSQL | Query Optimization, Indexing, Internals & Storage; archive: [dbi-services/articles/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-vii.json](dbi-services/articles/postgres-vs-oracle-access-paths-vii.json)
  Evidence note: Contrasts a well-clustered index scan reading about 150 blocks for 1000 rows with the worst-case clustering factor that forces Postgres into a Bitmap Index Scan to reduce block re-reads.
- [ODA X6 installation: re-image](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oda-x6-installation-re-image/) - 2017-06-28 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Migration & Compatibility, Operations & Observability, Development & Drivers; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oda-x6-installation-re-image.json](dbi-services/articles/oda-x6-installation-re-image.json)
  Evidence note: Gives a cheat sheet for re-imaging a freshly racked Oracle Database Appliance through the ILOM management network interface before the public network is configured.
- [12c Multitenant Internals: VPD for V$ views](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12c-multitenant-internals-vpd-for-v-views/) - 2017-06-27 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12c-multitenant-internals-vpd-for-v-views.json](dbi-services/articles/12c-multitenant-internals-vpd-for-v-views.json)
  Evidence note: Explains that V$ fixed views, unlike dictionary views, have no metadata to link and instead rely on CON_ID filtering so they remain queryable even in NOMOUNT or MOUNT state.
- [12cR2 Application Containers and Foreign Keys](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12cr2-application-containers-and-foreign-keys/) - 2017-06-19 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure, Operations & Observability, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12cr2-application-containers-and-foreign-keys.json](dbi-services/articles/12cr2-application-containers-and-foreign-keys.json)
  Evidence note: Reproduces bug 21955394 where a foreign key in an application PDB referencing a root data-link table fails a DELETE with ORA-02291 because parent-key lookup only checks the current container.
- [12c NSSn process for Data Guard SYNC transport](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12c-nssn-process-for-data-guard-sync-transport/) - 2017-06-15 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12c-nssn-process-for-data-guard-sync-transport.json](dbi-services/articles/12c-nssn-process-for-data-guard-sync-transport.json)
  Evidence note: Confirms by ps -edf that Oracle spawns one NSSn background process per LOG_ARCHIVE_DEST_n destination configured for SYNC redo transport, matching the destination numbers.
- [12cR2 PDB refresh as a poor-man standby?](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12cr2-pdb-refresh-as-a-poor-man-standby/) - 2017-06-12 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Internals & Storage, Backup & Recovery, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12cr2-pdb-refresh-as-a-poor-man-standby.json](dbi-services/articles/12cr2-pdb-refresh-as-a-poor-man-standby.json)
  Evidence note: Tests whether a refreshable PDB, which ships and applies redo to sync datafiles, could substitute for disaster recovery, concluding it's unsupported for production so far.
- [12cR2 auditing all users with a role granted](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12cr2-auditing-all-users-with-a-role-granted/) - 2017-06-04 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Security; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12cr2-auditing-all-users-with-a-role-granted.json](dbi-services/articles/12cr2-auditing-all-users-with-a-role-granted.json)
  Evidence note: Shows the new 12.2 unified audit syntax auditing users by granted role, letting a single policy automatically cover every user later granted the DBA role.
- [New release model for JUL17 (or Oracle 17.3): RU and RUR](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/new-release-model-for-jul17-ru-and-rur/) - 2017-05-30 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Security, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/new-release-model-for-jul17-ru-and-rur.json](dbi-services/articles/new-release-model-for-jul17-ru-and-rur.json)
  Evidence note: Tracks evolving rumors and confirmed bug references to predict the version number and release date of Oracle's new annual Release Update model ahead of its official announcement.
- [Which privilege for CREATE PLUGGABLE DATABASE from DB LINK?](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/which-privilege-for-create-pluggable-database-from-db-link/) - 2017-05-29 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure, Security; archive: [dbi-services/articles/which-privilege-for-create-pluggable-database-from-db-link.json](dbi-services/articles/which-privilege-for-create-pluggable-database-from-db-link.json)
  Evidence note: Clarifies that remote PDB cloning needs CREATE SESSION on the source CDB plus either CREATE PLUGGABLE DATABASE or the undocumented SYSOPER privilege granted locally on the source PDB.
- [Oracle 12cR2: exchange partition deferred invalidation](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-12cr2-exchange-partition-deferred-invalidation/) - 2017-05-29 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Indexing; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-12cr2-exchange-partition-deferred-invalidation.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-12cr2-exchange-partition-deferred-invalidation.json)
  Evidence note: Shows that EXCHANGE PARTITION can use the same rolling cursor invalidation as DBMS_STATS, but only when the exchanged table has matching indexes, else invalidation is immediate.
- [12cR2 needs to connect with password for Cross-PDB DML](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12cr2-needs-to-connect-with-password-for-cross-pdb-dml/) - 2017-05-26 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12cr2-needs-to-connect-with-password-for-cross-pdb-dml.json](dbi-services/articles/12cr2-needs-to-connect-with-password-for-cross-pdb-dml.json)
  Evidence note: Shows that CONTAINERS() DML relies on an implicit database link requiring a password, so connecting AS SYSDBA without one raises ORA-01017 unlike CONTAINERS() queries.
- [random “ORA-01017: invalid username/password” in 12cR2](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/random-ora-01017-invalid-usernamepassword-in-12cr2/) - 2017-05-16 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Backup & Recovery; archive: [dbi-services/articles/random-ora-01017-invalid-usernamepassword-in-12cr2.json](dbi-services/articles/random-ora-01017-invalid-usernamepassword-in-12cr2.json)
  Evidence note: Traces intermittent ORA-01017 errors on 12.2.0.1 sysdba logins in a VirtualBox training lab to entropy starvation in /dev/random, absent when connecting to 12.1.0.2 instead.
- [12cR2 Cross-container DML – insert into container()](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12cr2-cross-container-dml-insert-into-container/) - 2017-05-13 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Transactions & Consistency, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12cr2-cross-container-dml-insert-into-container.json](dbi-services/articles/12cr2-cross-container-dml-insert-into-container.json)
  Evidence note: Demonstrates INSERT INTO CONTAINERS(table) from CDB$ROOT writing directly into a table in a specific PDB by CON_ID, one of the new documented cross-PDB DML operations in 12.2.
- [What is in a transportable tablespace dumpfile?](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/what-is-in-a-transportable-tablespace-dumpfile/) - 2017-05-08 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Backup & Recovery, Data Modeling & SQL, Events & Community; archive: [dbi-services/articles/what-is-in-a-transportable-tablespace-dumpfile.json](dbi-services/articles/what-is-in-a-transportable-tablespace-dumpfile.json)
  Evidence note: Runs expdp with transport_tablespaces to show that a transportable-tablespace dump file holds only metadata objects like PLUGTS_BLK and constraints, not the actual data blocks.
- [Can you open PDB$SEED read write?](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/can-you-open-pdbseed-read-write/) - 2017-05-07 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Migration & Compatibility; archive: [dbi-services/articles/can-you-open-pdbseed-read-write.json](dbi-services/articles/can-you-open-pdbseed-read-write.json)
  Evidence note: Explains that PDB$SEED can only be opened read-write yourself in 12.2 local-undo mode to customize its UNDO tablespace, otherwise ALTER PLUGGABLE DATABASE PDB$SEED OPEN FORCE raises ORA-65017.
- [12c nologging and Data Guard](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12c-nologging-and-data-guard/) - 2017-05-03 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Internals & Storage, Backup & Recovery, Migration & Compatibility, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12c-nologging-and-data-guard.json](dbi-services/articles/12c-nologging-and-data-guard.json)
  Evidence note: Shows how to disable force logging temporarily during a Data Pump import to cut migration downtime, then re-enable it and resynchronize the standby's nonlogged blocks afterward.
- [12cR2 partial PDB backup](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12cr2-partial-pdb-backup/) - 2017-05-01 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Backup & Recovery, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12cr2-partial-pdb-backup.json](dbi-services/articles/12cr2-partial-pdb-backup.json)
  Evidence note: Demonstrates that a partial PDB backup in 12.1 cannot be restored without the CDB$ROOT since rollback needs the shared UNDO tablespace, while 12.2 local UNDO removes that requirement.
- [Listener and Virtual IP](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/listener-and-virtual-ip/) - 2017-04-25 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Security, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/listener-and-virtual-ip.json](dbi-services/articles/listener-and-virtual-ip.json)
  Evidence note: Explains that a listener bound to the hostname in /etc/hosts already listens on all interfaces including the VIP, so a separate VIP-specific listener.ora entry is unnecessary unless an IP is explicit.
- [12cR2 RMAN> REPAIR](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12cr2-rman-repair/) - 2017-04-23 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Backup & Recovery; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12cr2-rman-repair.json](dbi-services/articles/12cr2-rman-repair.json)
  Evidence note: Shows that RMAN's REPAIR command in 12.2 accepts direct targets like FAILURE, DATAFILE, or CORRUPTION beyond the 12.1.0.2 REPAIR FAILURE syntax, discoverable via the RMAN-01008 error text.
- [Data Pump LOGTIME, DUMPFILE, PARFILE, DATA_PUMP_DIR in 12c](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/data-pump-logtime-dumpfile-parfile-data_pump_dir-in-12c/) - 2017-04-22 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Indexing, Migration & Compatibility, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/data-pump-logtime-dumpfile-parfile-data_pump_dir-in-12c.json](dbi-services/articles/data-pump-logtime-dumpfile-parfile-data_pump_dir-in-12c.json)
  Evidence note: Diffs the impdp help output between 12.1 and 12.2 to highlight enhancements to the LOGTIME, DUMPFILE, PARFILE and DATA_PUMP_DIR parameters carried over from 12.1.
- [SecureFiles on multi-datafiles tablespaces](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/securefiles-on-multi-datafiles-tablespaces/) - 2017-04-21 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Internals & Storage, Migration & Compatibility; archive: [dbi-services/articles/securefiles-on-multi-datafiles-tablespaces.json](dbi-services/articles/securefiles-on-multi-datafiles-tablespaces.json)
  Evidence note: Reproduces uneven datafile filling in a multi-datafile tablespace during an 11g-to-12c migration, traced to LOB segments converted to SecureFiles with transform=lob_storage:securefile.
- [SQLcl on Bash on Ubuntu on Windows](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/sqlcl-on-bash-on-ubuntu-on-windows/) - 2017-04-16 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure, Security; archive: [dbi-services/articles/sqlcl-on-bash-on-ubuntu-on-windows.json](dbi-services/articles/sqlcl-on-bash-on-ubuntu-on-windows.json)
  Evidence note: Sets up SQLcl inside the Windows Subsystem for Linux (Bash on Ubuntu) after the Windows 10 Creators Update, comparing the experience to running the same tool under Cygwin.
- [Service “696c6f76656d756c746974656e616e74” has 1 instance(s).](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/service-696c6f76656d756c746974656e616e74-has-1-instances/) - 2017-04-08 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/service-696c6f76656d756c746974656e616e74-has-1-instances.json](dbi-services/articles/service-696c6f76656d756c746974656e616e74-has-1-instances.json)
  Evidence note: Decodes a cryptic hex-named listener service registered per PDB in 12.2 as the PDB's GUID, an internal service used for features like Proxy PDB that should not be used for client connections.
- [12cR2 DML monitoring and Statistics Advisor](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12cr2-dml-monitoring-and-statistics-advisor/) - 2017-04-06 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Operations & Observability, Development & Drivers; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12cr2-dml-monitoring-and-statistics-advisor.json](dbi-services/articles/12cr2-dml-monitoring-and-statistics-advisor.json)
  Evidence note: Enables an undocumented DBMS_STATS trace bit to watch the MMON slave trace and reveal how the 12.2 Statistics Advisor tracks DML frequency to detect volatile tables for stats locking.
- [12cR2 DBCA, Automatic Memory Management, and -databaseType](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12cr2-dbca-automatic-memory-management-and-databasetype/) - 2017-04-03 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12cr2-dbca-automatic-memory-management-and-databasetype.json](dbi-services/articles/12cr2-dbca-automatic-memory-management-and-databasetype.json)
  Evidence note: Explains the DBT-11211/INS-35178 error blocking Automatic Memory Management above 4GB physical memory in 12.2, contrasting it with sizing SGA and PGA separately under ASMM.
- [When automatic reoptimization plan is less efficient](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/when-automatic-reoptimization-plan-is-less-efficient/) - 2017-04-02 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/when-automatic-reoptimization-plan-is-less-efficient.json](dbi-services/articles/when-automatic-reoptimization-plan-is-less-efficient.json)
  Evidence note: Shows a case where 12c auto-reoptimization based on cardinality feedback produces a worse plan on the second execution before self-correcting back to the original fast plan by the fourth.
- [JAN17 Proactive Bundle Patch + Adaptive Statistics control](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/jan17-proactive-bundle-patch-adaptive-statistics-control/) - 2017-03-27 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Migration & Compatibility, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/jan17-proactive-bundle-patch-adaptive-statistics-control.json](dbi-services/articles/jan17-proactive-bundle-patch-adaptive-statistics-control.json)
  Evidence note: Recommends the latest 12.1 patchset plus the January 2017 Proactive Bundle Patch and two specific patches needed to fully control Adaptive Statistics before a migration.
- [Purging Unified Audit Trail in 12cR2](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/purging-unified-audit-trail-in-12cr2/) - 2017-03-19 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Security; archive: [dbi-services/articles/purging-unified-audit-trail-in-12cr2.json](dbi-services/articles/purging-unified-audit-trail-in-12cr2.json)
  Evidence note: Traces the 12.2 unified audit purge and finds it now checks IS_DROPPABLE_PARTITION on the partitioned AUD$UNIFIED table, letting old records be dropped instead of a slow row-by-row delete.
- [How many members for standby redo logs?](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/how-many-members-for-standby-redo-logs/) - 2017-03-18 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Transactions & Consistency, Internals & Storage; archive: [dbi-services/articles/how-many-members-for-standby-redo-logs.json](dbi-services/articles/how-many-members-for-standby-redo-logs.json)
  Evidence note: Argues that the traditional two-member redo log recommendation still protects against file loss, and explains why standby redo logs are only written to, not read, until a role transition.
- [Oracle 12cR2 on Windows: Virtual Accounts](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-12cr2-on-windows-virtual-accounts/) - 2017-03-16 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Security, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-12cr2-on-windows-virtual-accounts.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-12cr2-on-windows-virtual-accounts.json)
  Evidence note: Explains Windows 2008 R2's Virtual Accounts as a new install-time option for the process running Oracle 12.2, avoiding the password-management overhead of the 12.1 Oracle Home User.
- [Google Cloud Platform instances and Oracle Database](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/google-cloud-platform-instances-and-oracle-database-performance/) - 2017-03-12 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Cloud & Infrastructure, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/google-cloud-platform-instances-and-oracle-database-performance.json](dbi-services/articles/google-cloud-platform-instances-and-oracle-database-performance.json)
  Evidence note: Benchmarks Google Cloud IaaS instance types with a cached SLOB workload, while noting that Oracle currently refuses to count Google Cloud vCPUs as an authorized licensing metric.
- [12cR2: TNS_ADMIN in env.ora](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12cr2-tns_admin-in-env-ora/) - 2017-03-10 | dbi services | Oracle Database | General Database; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12cr2-tns_admin-in-env-ora.json](dbi-services/articles/12cr2-tns_admin-in-env-ora.json)
  Evidence note: Shows that the new 12.2 $ORACLE_HOME/env.ora file lets you redirect TNS_ADMIN to a shared network directory without symbolic links, verified with strace tracing which sqlnet.ora tnsping opens.
- [Oracle 12cR2, RAC, Cloud, ACFS, PDB thin clones and asmadmin](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-12cr2-rac-cloud-acfs-pdb-thin-clones-and-asmadmin/) - 2017-03-09 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Internals & Storage, Backup & Recovery, Cloud & Infrastructure, Security; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-12cr2-rac-cloud-acfs-pdb-thin-clones-and-asmadmin.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-12cr2-rac-cloud-acfs-pdb-thin-clones-and-asmadmin.json)
  Evidence note: Hits an ORA-28357/ORA-17517 wallet and storage-snapshot failure when creating a PDB as a snapshot copy on ACFS in the Oracle Public Cloud, traced to the TDE keystore password requirement.
- [Oracle 12cR2: changes for login.sql](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-12cr2-changes-for-login-sql/) - 2017-03-07 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Security; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-12cr2-changes-for-login-sql.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-12cr2-changes-for-login-sql.json)
  Evidence note: Shows that 12.2 stops running login.sql from the current working directory as a security fix, tracing with bash -x how SQL*Plus resolves the script path differently than in 12.1.
- [Misleading wait event names clarified in V$EVENT_NAME](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/misleading-wait-event-names-clarified-in-vevent_name/) - 2017-03-06 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, High Availability & Replication, Transactions & Consistency, Internals & Storage, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/misleading-wait-event-names-clarified-in-vevent_name.json](dbi-services/articles/misleading-wait-event-names-clarified-in-vevent_name.json)
  Evidence note: Highlights the new DISPLAY_NAME column added to V$EVENT_NAME in 12c, listing several internally-named wait events whose display name better matches user-facing meaning.
- [Purging Unified Audit Trail in 12cR1](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/purging-unified-audit-trail-in-12cr1/) - 2017-03-04 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Security; archive: [dbi-services/articles/purging-unified-audit-trail-in-12cr1.json](dbi-services/articles/purging-unified-audit-trail-in-12cr1.json)
  Evidence note: Traces DBMS_AUDIT_MGMT.CLEAN_AUDIT_TRAIL on a 12.1 Unified Audit Trail filled with hundreds of thousands of failed logins to determine whether the purge does slow deletes or fast truncates.
- [Oracle 12cR2: Online tablespace encryption](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-12cr2-online-tablespace-encryption/) - 2017-02-26 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Cloud & Infrastructure, Security; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-12cr2-online-tablespace-encryption.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-12cr2-online-tablespace-encryption.json)
  Evidence note: Runs a four-phase SLOB workload while encrypting and decrypting a tablespace online in 12.2, using ASH visualization to show the extra 'db file parallel write' overhead during each transition.
- [12cR2: Recover nonlogged blocks after NOLOGGING in Data Guard](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12cr2-recover-nonlogged-blocks-after-nologging-in-data-guard/) - 2017-02-26 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Indexing, High Availability & Replication, Internals & Storage, Backup & Recovery; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12cr2-recover-nonlogged-blocks-after-nologging-in-data-guard.json](dbi-services/articles/12cr2-recover-nonlogged-blocks-after-nologging-in-data-guard.json)
  Evidence note: Demonstrates the 12.2 RECOVER DATABASE NONLOGGED BLOCK command that lets a standby fetch and apply blocks left unrecoverable by a NOLOGGING direct-path insert on primary.
- [12c Unified Auditing and AUDIT_TRAIL=DB in mixed mode](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12c-unified-auditing-and-audit_traildb-in-mixed-mode/) - 2017-02-23 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Migration & Compatibility, Security; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12c-unified-auditing-and-audit_traildb-in-mixed-mode.json](dbi-services/articles/12c-unified-auditing-and-audit_traildb-in-mixed-mode.json)
  Evidence note: Explains that enabling the old AUDIT_TRAIL=DB parameter alongside Unified Auditing puts a 12c database into 'mixed mode', where V$OPTION still reports Unified Auditing as FALSE.
- [12cR2: lockdown profiles and ORA-01219](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12cr2-lockdown-profiles-and-ora-01219/) - 2017-02-21 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Transactions & Consistency, Internals & Storage, Backup & Recovery, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12cr2-lockdown-profiles-and-ora-01219.json](dbi-services/articles/12cr2-lockdown-profiles-and-ora-01219.json)
  Evidence note: Reproduces losing a PDB datafile in a lab, showing the ORA-01113/ORA-01157 sequence when opening the CDB and the steps taken to offline the tablespace so the rest of the database can open.
- [12cR2 real-time materialized view (on query computation)](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12cr2-real-time-materialized-view-on-query-computation/) - 2017-02-17 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Indexing, High Availability & Replication, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12cr2-real-time-materialized-view-on-query-computation.json](dbi-services/articles/12cr2-real-time-materialized-view-on-query-computation.json)
  Evidence note: Demonstrates 12.2's on-query computation feature where a stale materialized view is joined with its materialized view log at query time to deliver a fresh result without a refresh.
- [12cR2 DBCA can create a standby database](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12cr2-dbca-can-create-a-standby-database/) - 2017-02-12 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Backup & Recovery, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12cr2-dbca-can-create-a-standby-database.json](dbi-services/articles/12cr2-dbca-can-create-a-standby-database.json)
  Evidence note: Tests the new 'dbca -silent -createDuplicateDB -createAsStandby' option in 12.2, finding it fails with DBT-16056/DBT-16057 because it only supports single-instance, non-container databases.
- [Oracle Public Cloud: LIOPS with 4 OCPU in PaaS](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-public-cloud-liops-with-4-ocpu-in-paas/) - 2017-02-10 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-public-cloud-liops-with-4-ocpu-in-paas.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-public-cloud-liops-with-4-ocpu-in-paas.json)
  Evidence note: Reruns a cached SLOB logical-reads benchmark on Oracle PaaS DBaaS with a different CPU model than an earlier IaaS test, comparing logical I/O throughput per OCPU across the two service types.
- [Oracle Public Cloud: 2 OCPU for 1 proc. license](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-public-cloud-2-ocpu-for-1-proc-license/) - 2017-02-08 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-public-cloud-2-ocpu-for-1-proc-license.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-public-cloud-2-ocpu-for-1-proc-license.json)
  Evidence note: Runs cached SLOB on an Oracle Public Cloud OC5 shape offering 2 OCPUs per processor license, comparing the resulting logical-read throughput to an earlier AWS bring-your-own-license test.
- [Exadata Express Cloud Service: SQL and Optimizer trace](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/exadata-express-cloud-service-sql-and-optimizer-trace/) - 2017-02-06 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/exadata-express-cloud-service-sql-and-optimizer-trace.json](dbi-services/articles/exadata-express-cloud-service-sql-and-optimizer-trace.json)
  Evidence note: Shows how to read a SQL trace or optimizer 10053 trace on the filesystem-less Exadata Express Cloud Service by querying V$DIAG_TRACE_FILE_CONTENTS for the session's trace file.
- [Oracle Public Cloud 12cR2: TDE is not an option](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-public-cloud-12cr2-tde-is-not-an-option/) - 2017-02-05 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure, Security; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-public-cloud-12cr2-tde-is-not-an-option.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-public-cloud-12cr2-tde-is-not-an-option.json)
  Evidence note: Explains the encrypt_new_tablespaces parameter, defaulting to CLOUD_ONLY, that forces every new Oracle Public Cloud tablespace to be TDE-encrypted regardless of the CREATE TABLESPACE DDL.
- [CBO parse time – matching time with transformation](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/cbo-parse-time-match-time-with-transformation/) - 2017-02-04 | dbi services | Database agnostic | Query Optimization; archive: [dbi-services/articles/cbo-parse-time-match-time-with-transformation.json](dbi-services/articles/cbo-parse-time-match-time-with-transformation.json)
  Evidence note: Uses a 10053 trace enhanced by an April 2016 PSU bug fix to annotate accumulated CPU and elapsed parse time against specific query-transformation phases like join predicate pushdown.
- [Exadata Express Cloud Service: MAX_PDB_STORAGE](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/exadata-express-cloud-service-max_pdb_storage/) - 2017-02-03 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Transactions & Consistency, Internals & Storage, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/exadata-express-cloud-service-max_pdb_storage.json](dbi-services/articles/exadata-express-cloud-service-max_pdb_storage.json)
  Evidence note: Shows that creating a tablespace in the PDBaaS Exadata Express Cloud Service inherits db_create_file_dest from the CDB administrator and raises ORA-65250 if a datafile path is specified.
- [Running SLOB on Exadata Express Cloud Service](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/running-slob-on-exadata-express-cloud-service/) - 2017-02-01 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Internals & Storage, Cloud & Infrastructure, Security; archive: [dbi-services/articles/running-slob-on-exadata-express-cloud-service.json](dbi-services/articles/running-slob-on-exadata-express-cloud-service.json)
  Evidence note: Runs the SLOB benchmark remotely against Exadata Express Cloud Service using the client credentials wallet and TNS_ADMIN over SQL*Net-over-SSL, without SYSDBA or host access.
- [Amazon AWS instances and Oracle database performance](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/amazon-aws-instances-and-oracle-database-performance/) - 2017-02-01 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Transactions & Consistency, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/amazon-aws-instances-and-oracle-database-performance.json](dbi-services/articles/amazon-aws-instances-and-oracle-database-performance.json)
  Evidence note: Compares cached SLOB logical-reads-per-second across AWS T2, M4, R4 and C4 instance types under a 2-processor-license vCPU budget to find the best-performing shape for bring-your-own-license Oracle.
- [Oracle Core factor and Oracle or non-Oracle Cloud](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-core-factor-and-non-oracle-cloud/) - 2017-01-30 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-core-factor-and-non-oracle-cloud.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-core-factor-and-non-oracle-cloud.json)
  Evidence note: Walks through Oracle's Processor Core Factor Table and cloud licensing documents to explain how to count AWS vCPUs, Azure cores, and other cloud or virtualized CPUs against a processor license.
- [Exadata Express Cloud Service: PDB_ADMIN privileges](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/exadata-express-cloud-service-pdb_admin-privileges/) - 2017-01-24 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Backup & Recovery, Cloud & Infrastructure, Security; archive: [dbi-services/articles/exadata-express-cloud-service-pdb_admin-privileges.json](dbi-services/articles/exadata-express-cloud-service-pdb_admin-privileges.json)
  Evidence note: Runs a recursive DBA_ROLE_PRIVS query to enumerate which system privileges the PDB_ADMIN user actually holds through its PDB_DBA role on the Exadata Express Cloud Service.
- [12cR2: no cardinality feedback for small queries](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12cr2-no-cardinality-feedback-for-small-queries/) - 2017-01-05 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12cr2-no-cardinality-feedback-for-small-queries.json](dbi-services/articles/12cr2-no-cardinality-feedback-for-small-queries.json)
  Evidence note: Uses strings against the oracle binary to trace bug 23596611, which bypasses 12c cardinality feedback re-optimization for queries classified internally as 'small', a change not yet documented.
- [NLS defaults for LANGUAGE and TERRITORY](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/nls-defaults-for-language-and-territory/) - 2017-01-04 | dbi services | Oracle Database | General Database; archive: [dbi-services/articles/nls-defaults-for-language-and-territory.json](dbi-services/articles/nls-defaults-for-language-and-territory.json)
  Evidence note: Lists, for every value in V$NLS_VALID_VALUES, the derived NLS_DATE_FORMAT, NLS_NUMERIC_CHARACTERS and NLS_CURRENCY defaults set when NLS_TERRITORY or NLS_LANGUAGE is changed.

### 2016

- [Oracle 12cR2: AWR views in multitenant](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-12cr2-awr-views-in-multitenant/) - 2016-12-19 | dbi services | Oracle Database | General Database; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-12cr2-awr-views-in-multitenant.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-12cr2-awr-views-in-multitenant.json)
  Evidence note: Maps which AWR dictionary views read from CDB-level snapshots, PDB-level snapshots, or both in 12.2, now that AWR snapshots can be taken at either the CDB or PDB level.
- [RMAN> TRANSPORT TABLESPACE](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/rman-transport-tablespace/) - 2016-12-18 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, High Availability & Replication, Backup & Recovery, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/rman-transport-tablespace.json](dbi-services/articles/rman-transport-tablespace.json)
  Evidence note: Shows RMAN's TRANSPORT TABLESPACE command restoring a tablespace via TSPITR into an auxiliary instance so it can be exported without the source going read-only, and why it can't run from a PDB.
- [Oracle Database 12c Release 2 Multitenant (Oracle Press)](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-database-12c-release-2-multitenant-oracle-press/) - 2016-12-14 | dbi services | Oracle Database | General Database; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-database-12c-release-2-multitenant-oracle-press.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-database-12c-release-2-multitenant-oracle-press.json)
  Evidence note: Recounts co-authoring the Oracle Press multitenant book with two colleagues, describing the technical and English-language review process with additional named reviewers.
- [Oracle 12cR2: Statistics Advisor](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-12cr2-statistics-advisor/) - 2016-12-07 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-12cr2-statistics-advisor.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-12cr2-statistics-advisor.json)
  Evidence note: Summarizes the V$STATS_ADVISOR_RULES catalog, walking through best-practice rules such as always enabling the automatic statistics job and purging statistics history from SYSAUX.
- [Multitenant internals – Summary](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/multitenant-internals-summary/) - 2016-12-05 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Migration & Compatibility, Security, Events & Community; archive: [dbi-services/articles/multitenant-internals-summary.json](dbi-services/articles/multitenant-internals-summary.json)
  Evidence note: Indexes the author's series of multitenant-internals posts covering metadata links, object links, AWR views, dictionary upgrades, and shared-pool row cache behavior in CDB/PDB separation.
- [Histograms on character strings between 11.2.0.3 and 11.2.0.4](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/histograms-on-character-strings-between-11-2-0-3-and-11-2-0-4/) - 2016-11-30 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Operations & Observability, Events & Community; archive: [dbi-services/articles/histograms-on-character-strings-between-11-2-0-3-and-11-2-0-4.json](dbi-services/articles/histograms-on-character-strings-between-11-2-0-3-and-11-2-0-4.json)
  Evidence note: Shows that a frequency histogram gathered in 11.2.0.3 produces a wrong low-cardinality estimate under the 11.2.0.4 optimizer's out-of-range predicate density calculation for a two-value flag column.
- [Encryption in Oracle Public Cloud](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/encryption-in-oracle-public-cloud/) - 2016-11-29 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Cloud & Infrastructure, Security; archive: [dbi-services/articles/encryption-in-oracle-public-cloud.json](dbi-services/articles/encryption-in-oracle-public-cloud.json)
  Evidence note: Explains the ORA-28374 typed-master-key error hit when creating a tablespace in a new PDB on Oracle Public Cloud, tracing it to Transparent Data Encryption being enforced without option in DBaaS.
- [Observing DML restarts caused by invalidations](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/observing-dml-restarts-caused-by-invalidations/) - 2016-11-24 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Transactions & Consistency, Internals & Storage, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/observing-dml-restarts-caused-by-invalidations.json](dbi-services/articles/observing-dml-restarts-caused-by-invalidations.json)
  Evidence note: Reproduces a DML restart on a hash-partitioned table by invalidating a cursor after it acquired a partition lock, showing the restart appears as a separate EXEC_SQL_ID in SQL Monitoring.
- [12cR2: Upgrade by remote clone with TDE in DBaaS](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12cr2-upgrade-by-remote-clone-with-tde-in-dbaas/) - 2016-11-23 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Migration & Compatibility, Cloud & Infrastructure, Security; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12cr2-upgrade-by-remote-clone-with-tde-in-dbaas.json](dbi-services/articles/12cr2-upgrade-by-remote-clone-with-tde-in-dbaas.json)
  Evidence note: Walks through exporting and importing TDE encryption keys with ADMINISTER KEY MANAGEMENT so a 12.1 PDB's encrypted tablespaces stay readable after remote-cloning it into a 12.2 DBaaS service.
- [12cR2: Upgrade by remote clone – workaround ORA-17630 in DBaaS](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12cr2-upgrade-by-remote-clone-workaround-ora-17630-in-dbaas/) - 2016-11-20 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Migration & Compatibility, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12cr2-upgrade-by-remote-clone-workaround-ora-17630-in-dbaas.json](dbi-services/articles/12cr2-upgrade-by-remote-clone-workaround-ora-17630-in-dbaas.json)
  Evidence note: Diagnoses ORA-17628/ORA-17630 remote-file-protocol version mismatch when remote-cloning a 12.1 PDB directly into a 12.2 CDB, and shows the workaround needed in DBaaS.
- [12cR2: How long to upgrade a PDB?](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12cr2-how-long-to-upgrade-a-pdb/) - 2016-11-20 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Migration & Compatibility, Cloud & Infrastructure, Security, Development & Drivers; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12cr2-how-long-to-upgrade-a-pdb.json](dbi-services/articles/12cr2-how-long-to-upgrade-a-pdb.json)
  Evidence note: Times a full CDB$ROOT catalog upgrade from 12.1.0.2 to 12.2.0.1 component by component to establish a baseline before comparing it to the elapsed time of upgrading a PDB by unplug/plug.
- [12cR2: Upgrade by unplug/plug in the Oracle Cloud Service](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12cr2-upgrade-by-unplugplug-in-the-oracle-cloud-service/) - 2016-11-19 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Migration & Compatibility, Cloud & Infrastructure, Security; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12cr2-upgrade-by-unplugplug-in-the-oracle-cloud-service.json](dbi-services/articles/12cr2-upgrade-by-unplugplug-in-the-oracle-cloud-service.json)
  Evidence note: Demonstrates unplugging a PDB from a 12.1 DBaaS service, copying the datafiles and XML metadata over scp, and plugging it into a new 12.2 DBaaS service since no in-place upgrade button exists.
- [12cR2: CREATE_FILE_DEST for PDB isolation](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12cr2-create_file_dest-for-pdb-isolation/) - 2016-11-16 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Transactions & Consistency, Internals & Storage, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12cr2-create_file_dest-for-pdb-isolation.json](dbi-services/articles/12cr2-create_file_dest-for-pdb-isolation.json)
  Evidence note: Shows the new 12.2 CREATE_FILE_DEST clause on CREATE PLUGGABLE DATABASE that constrains a PDB's datafiles to a specific directory, fulfilling an OTN enhancement request filed two years earlier.
- [12cR2 Single-Tenant: Multitenant Features for All Editions](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12cr2-single-tenant-multitenant-features-for-all-editions/) - 2016-11-14 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Cloud & Infrastructure, Security, Events & Community; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12cr2-single-tenant-multitenant-features-for-all-editions.json](dbi-services/articles/12cr2-single-tenant-multitenant-features-for-all-editions.json)
  Evidence note: Shares Oracle Open World slides arguing that since non-CDB is deprecated, Single-Tenant still brings dictionary separation, PDB cloning, and metadata links to editions without the option.
- [12cR2 new index usage tracking](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12cr2-new-index-usage-tracking/) - 2016-11-13 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Indexing, Performance & Benchmarking, Operations & Observability, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12cr2-new-index-usage-tracking.json](dbi-services/articles/12cr2-new-index-usage-tracking.json)
  Evidence note: Compares the improved 12.2 DBA_INDEX_USAGE and V$INDEX_USAGE_INFO views against older index monitoring, showing an undocumented parameter switching collection from sampled to all executions.
- [Oracle 12cR2: Pluggable database relocation](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-12cr2-pluggable-database-relocation/) - 2016-11-11 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure, Security; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-12cr2-pluggable-database-relocation.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-12cr2-pluggable-database-relocation.json)
  Evidence note: Demonstrates PDB relocation between two Standard Edition CDBs with no option required, transporting a table's data without exporting metadata logically or setting the source tablespace read-only.
- [Oracle 12cR2: MAX_PDBS](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-12cr2-max_pdbs/) - 2016-11-11 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-12cr2-max_pdbs.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-12cr2-max_pdbs.json)
  Evidence note: Shows that MAX_PDBS, defaulting to 4098, is the only way in plain Enterprise Edition to cap pluggable database count below the option's 4096 limit, since Standard Edition enforces a hard cap.
- [12cR2 has new SQL*Plus features](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12cr2-has-new-sqlplus-features/) - 2016-11-11 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Migration & Compatibility; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12cr2-has-new-sqlplus-features.json](dbi-services/articles/12cr2-has-new-sqlplus-features.json)
  Evidence note: Lists small 12.2 SQL*Plus improvements: the default _EDITOR changes from 'ed' to 'vi', command-line history is added, and row/LOB prefetch plus statement caching become configurable.
- [Oracle 12cR2 multitenant: Local UNDO](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-12cr2-multitenant-local-undo/) - 2016-11-08 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Backup & Recovery, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-12cr2-multitenant-local-undo.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-12cr2-multitenant-local-undo.json)
  Evidence note: Explains why the shared CDB-level UNDO tablespace, despite dictionary separation putting user data in the PDB, is the reason PDBs can't be flashed back without an auxiliary instance in 12.1.
- [Oracle 12cR2 PL/SQL new feature: TNSPING from the database](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-12cr2-plsql-new-feature-tnsping-from-the-database/) - 2016-11-08 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-12cr2-plsql-new-feature-tnsping-from-the-database.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-12cr2-plsql-new-feature-tnsping-from-the-database.json)
  Evidence note: Introduces the 12.2 DBMS_TNS package that resolves a connection string from inside PL/SQL, avoiding the need to run tnsping on the server with matching TNS_ADMIN settings.
- [12cR2 multitenant containers in SQL_TRACE](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-12cr2-multitenant-containers-in-sql_trace/) - 2016-11-08 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure, Development & Drivers; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-12cr2-multitenant-containers-in-sql_trace.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-12cr2-multitenant-containers-in-sql_trace.json)
  Evidence note: Shows that a 12.2 SQL trace now records a CONTAINER ID line at session start and on each implicit container switch, a detail missing from 12.1 traces of the same metadata-link queries.
- [Oracle Public Cloud: create a database from command line](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-public-cloud-create-a-database-from-command-line/) - 2016-11-07 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure, JSON & Documents; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-public-cloud-create-a-database-from-command-line.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-public-cloud-create-a-database-from-command-line.json)
  Evidence note: Shows a JSON request body and curl REST API call against the Oracle Public Cloud service catalog to automate DBaaS provisioning from the command line instead of the web console.
- [Dbvisit replicate when you flashback the target](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/dbvisit-replicate-when-you-flashback-the-target/) - 2016-11-03 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Backup & Recovery, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/dbvisit-replicate-when-you-flashback-the-target.json](dbi-services/articles/dbvisit-replicate-when-you-flashback-the-target.json)
  Evidence note: Explains why flashing back a Dbvisit Replicate target database doesn't break ongoing APPLY, since the MINE and APPLY configurations are stored separately in the source and destination.
- [12c online move datafile in same filesystem.](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12c-online-move-datafile-in-same-filesystem/) - 2016-11-02 | dbi services | Oracle Database | General Database; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12c-online-move-datafile-in-same-filesystem.json](dbi-services/articles/12c-online-move-datafile-in-same-filesystem.json)
  Evidence note: Uses strace to contrast a plain Linux 'mv' within a filesystem, a fast rename() with no copy, against Oracle's 12c online datafile move, which always physically copies even on the same filesystem.
- [12c GTT private statistics and cursor invalidation](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12c-gtt-private-statistics-and-cursor-invalidation/) - 2016-10-27 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12c-gtt-private-statistics-and-cursor-invalidation.json](dbi-services/articles/12c-gtt-private-statistics-and-cursor-invalidation.json)
  Evidence note: Shows that gathering statistics on a global temporary table with session-scoped private statistics triggers immediate, not rolling, cursor invalidation, risking a hard-parse storm.
- [2 x ODA X6-2S + Dbvisit Standby: Easy DR in SE](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/2-x-oda-x6-2s-dbvisit-standby-easy-dr-in-se/) - 2016-10-21 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Indexing, Performance & Benchmarking, High Availability & Replication, Backup & Recovery, Operations & Observability, Development & Drivers; archive: [dbi-services/articles/2-x-oda-x6-2s-dbvisit-standby-easy-dr-in-se.json](dbi-services/articles/2-x-oda-x6-2s-dbvisit-standby-easy-dr-in-se.json)
  Evidence note: Documents pairing two ODA X6-2S appliances running Standard Edition with Dbvisit Standby across two datacenters, noting how odacli logs each provisioning step to a Java DB repository.
- [Datawarehouse ODS load is fast and easy in Enterprise Edition](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/easy-datawarehouse-ods-load-in-enterprise-edition/) - 2016-10-19 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Indexing, High Availability & Replication, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/easy-datawarehouse-ods-load-in-enterprise-edition.json](dbi-services/articles/easy-datawarehouse-ods-load-in-enterprise-edition.json)
  Evidence note: Shows transporting tables into a datawarehouse ODS from a Data Guard snapshot standby using transportable tablespaces, avoiding the need to put the live production tablespace read-only.
- [CDB resource plan: shares and utilization_limit](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/cdb-resource-plan-shares-and-utilization_limit/) - 2016-10-17 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Transactions & Consistency, Cloud & Infrastructure, Security, Events & Community; archive: [dbi-services/articles/cdb-resource-plan-shares-and-utilization_limit.json](dbi-services/articles/cdb-resource-plan-shares-and-utilization_limit.json)
  Evidence note: Explains CDB resource plan shares and utilization_limit as CPU-governing mechanisms in multitenant, demonstrating with 8 CPU-bound sessions how a 50% resource_limit caps one PDB.
- [Oracle 12cR2: DDL deferred invalidation](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-12cr2-ddl-deferred-invalidation/) - 2016-10-02 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-12cr2-ddl-deferred-invalidation.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-12cr2-ddl-deferred-invalidation.json)
  Evidence note: Reproduces a hang caused by ALTER TABLE ADD COMMENT invalidating dependent cursors, then shows 12.2's DDL deferred invalidation applying the rolling-invalidation window used by DBMS_STATS.
- [Oracle 12cR2: IS_ROLLING_INVALID in V$SQL](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-12cr2-is_rolling_invalid-in-vsql/) - 2016-09-25 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-12cr2-is_rolling_invalid-in-vsql.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-12cr2-is_rolling_invalid-in-vsql.json)
  Evidence note: Adds a parse-only DBMS_SQL call to show the new IS_ROLLING_INVALID column in 12.2's V$SQL, distinguishing a cursor marked 'rolling invalid' from one already 'rolling invalid executed'.
- [Modern software architecture – what is a database?](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/modern-software-architecture-what-is-a-database/) - 2016-09-24 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Distributed Databases, Internals & Storage, JSON & Documents, Development & Drivers; archive: [dbi-services/articles/modern-software-architecture-what-is-a-database.json](dbi-services/articles/modern-software-architecture-what-is-a-database.json)
  Evidence note: Argues to a developer audience that a relational database's role goes beyond persistence, tracing the author's path from OO design and RAD tools back to SQL as the technology that persisted.
- [Ravello](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/ravello/) - 2016-09-20 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/ravello.json](dbi-services/articles/ravello.json)
  Evidence note: Describes Ravello, acquired by Oracle earlier that year, as a nested-virtualization layer that imports VMware VM ecosystems and reproduces their private network, including original IP addresses.
- [Oracle 12cR2 Optimizer Adaptive Statistics](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-12cr2-optimizer-adaptive-statistics/) - 2016-09-20 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Migration & Compatibility, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-12cr2-optimizer-adaptive-statistics.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-12cr2-optimizer-adaptive-statistics.json)
  Evidence note: Distinguishes fixing the root cause of 12c adaptive-feature regressions from the common workaround of setting optimizer_features_enable to an older version, previewing 12.2 changes.
- [Oracle 12cR2 Long Identifiers](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-12cr2-long-identifiers/) - 2016-09-20 | dbi services | Oracle Database | General Database; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-12cr2-long-identifiers.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-12cr2-long-identifiers.json)
  Evidence note: Shows that while 12.1's dictionary views already used 128-byte VARCHAR2 columns, only 12.2 actually lifts the 30-character object-identifier limit, since 12.1 still raises ORA-00972.
- [Oracle Database 12.2 – PDBaaS](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-database-12-2-pdbaas/) - 2016-09-19 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Migration & Compatibility, Cloud & Infrastructure, Security, Events & Community; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-database-12-2-pdbaas.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-database-12-2-pdbaas.json)
  Evidence note: Announces Exadata Express Cloud Service as the 'Cloud First' way to access 12.2 features, a fully-managed single-PDB service with no free trial and several system-level operations locked down.
- [Oracle 12cR2 SQL new feature: LISTAGG overflow](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-12cr2-sql-new-feature-listagg-overflow/) - 2016-09-19 | dbi services | Oracle Database | General Database; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-12cr2-sql-new-feature-listagg-overflow.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-12cr2-sql-new-feature-listagg-overflow.json)
  Evidence note: Shows the ORA-01489 string-too-long error LISTAGG raises past 4000 characters, then demonstrates 12.2's new ON OVERFLOW TRUNCATE clause replacing a manual cumulative-length workaround.
- [Result cache side effects on number of calls](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/result-cache-side-effects-on-number-of-calls/) - 2016-09-13 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Performance & Benchmarking; archive: [dbi-services/articles/result-cache-side-effects-on-number-of-calls.json](dbi-services/articles/result-cache-side-effects-on-number-of-calls.json)
  Evidence note: Demonstrates that a RESULT_CACHE-hinted query calls its underlying function twice on a cache miss instead of once, an overhead that matters when the cached function is itself expensive.
- [Multitenant thin provisioning: PDB snapshots on ACFS](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/multitenant-thin-provisioning-pdb-snapshots-on-acfs/) - 2016-09-06 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Internals & Storage, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/multitenant-thin-provisioning-pdb-snapshots-on-acfs.json](dbi-services/articles/multitenant-thin-provisioning-pdb-snapshots-on-acfs.json)
  Evidence note: Traces how ACFS snapshot-based thin cloning works for whole databases on ODA X5 versus PDB-level snapshot cloning in multitenant, and why a CDB itself cannot be created on a snapshot.
- [Adaptive Plans and cost of inactive branches](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/adaptive-plans-and-cost-of-inactive-branches/) - 2016-09-03 | dbi services | Database agnostic | Query Optimization, Indexing, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/adaptive-plans-and-cost-of-inactive-branches.json](dbi-services/articles/adaptive-plans-and-cost-of-inactive-branches.json)
  Evidence note: Investigates a tweeted execution-plan screenshot with mismatched cost numbers, reproducing a hash join with stale statistics to explain how Adaptive Plans cost their inactive branch alternative.
- [Rolling Invalidate Window Exceeded](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/rolling-invalidate-window-exceeded/) - 2016-08-30 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/rolling-invalidate-window-exceeded.json](dbi-services/articles/rolling-invalidate-window-exceeded.json)
  Evidence note: Reproduces a hard-parse-storm postmortem by tracing V$SQL_SHARED_CURSOR.REASON timestamps for a rolling invalidate window exceeded, after a row insert changes a table's online-gathered num_rows.
- [ODA X6 database classes and shapes](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oda-x6-database-classes-and-shapes/) - 2016-08-29 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Internals & Storage, Cloud & Infrastructure, JSON & Documents; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oda-x6-database-classes-and-shapes.json](dbi-services/articles/oda-x6-database-classes-and-shapes.json)
  Evidence note: Documents the odacli create-database command-line options on ODA X6, listing the OLTP/DSS/IMDB database classes and odb1-style shapes available for provisioning.
- [Filenames in AWR reports](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/filenames-in-awr-reports/) - 2016-08-29 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Internals & Storage; archive: [dbi-services/articles/filenames-in-awr-reports.json](dbi-services/articles/filenames-in-awr-reports.json)
  Evidence note: Traces mismatched datafile names in AWR reports back to DBA_HIST_DATAFILE lacking a SNAP_ID, meaning AWR does not track filename history when a datafile moves between ACFS and ASM.
- [ODA X6 command line and Web Console](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oda-x6-command-line-and-web-console/) - 2016-08-27 | dbi services | Oracle Database | General Database; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oda-x6-command-line-and-web-console.json](dbi-services/articles/oda-x6-command-line-and-web-console.json)
  Evidence note: Shows the odacli describe-appliance output and small web console on ODA X6, covering appliance information, disk group redundancy percentages, and provisioning job history.
- [AUDIT_SYS_OPERATIONS and top-level operation](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/audit_sys_operations-and-top-level-operation/) - 2016-08-23 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Security; archive: [dbi-services/articles/audit_sys_operations-and-top-level-operation.json](dbi-services/articles/audit_sys_operations-and-top-level-operation.json)
  Evidence note: Shows that AUDIT_SYS_OPERATIONS, despite documentation limiting it to top-level SYSDBA statements, also logs recursive statements from the automatic statistics job, flooding the audit trail.
- [Unplugged pluggable databases](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/unplugged-pluggable-databases/) - 2016-08-15 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/unplugged-pluggable-databases.json](dbi-services/articles/unplugged-pluggable-databases.json)
  Evidence note: Critiques the Oracle documentation's USB-stick illustration of pluggable databases, arguing CDB$ROOT, PDB$SEED and user PDBs are all containers plugged into the CDB, not into CDB$ROOT.
- [Multitenant internals: object links on fixed tables](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/multitenant-internals-object-links-on-fixed-tables/) - 2016-08-07 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage; archive: [dbi-services/articles/multitenant-internals-object-links-on-fixed-tables.json](dbi-services/articles/multitenant-internals-object-links-on-fixed-tables.json)
  Evidence note: Shows that a query on a shared=object view over a fixed table like V$SESSION switches to CDB$ROOT using a RESULT_CACHE(SYSOBJ=TRUE) hint, explaining an observed refresh delay.
- [Multitenant internals: how object links are parsed/executed](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/multitenant-internals-how-object-links-are-parsedexecuted/) - 2016-08-07 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/multitenant-internals-how-object-links-are-parsedexecuted.json](dbi-services/articles/multitenant-internals-how-object-links-are-parsedexecuted.json)
  Evidence note: Traces the SQL trace of a query on an object-link view, showing it parses locally in the PDB but the recursive query against the CDB$ROOT copy of the table executes in a separate step.
- [Exadata X-5 Bare Metal vs. OVM load testing](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/exadata-x-5-bare-metal-vs-ovm-load-testing/) - 2016-07-31 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Transactions & Consistency, Internals & Storage; archive: [dbi-services/articles/exadata-x-5-bare-metal-vs-ovm-load-testing.json](dbi-services/articles/exadata-x-5-bare-metal-vs-ovm-load-testing.json)

  Evidence note: Uses DBMS_PRIVILEGE_CAPTURE against a DBA-granted role in both CDB$ROOT and a PDB to identify the two separate privileges that must be revoked to fully prevent connecting to a container.
