  Evidence note: Shows that PDB-level spfile parameters are stored in the CDB$ROOT table SYS.PDB_SPFILE$ keyed by PDB_UID, explaining why changing them on a read-only standby needs a different approach.
- [12c Multitenant internals: AWR tables and views](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12c-multitenant-internals-awr-tables-and-views/) - 2016-03-13 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Events & Community; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12c-multitenant-internals-awr-tables-and-views.json](dbi-services/articles/12c-multitenant-internals-awr-tables-and-views.json)
  Evidence note: Shows that AWR's DBA_HIST_ tables and views are not themselves Object Links, but the switch to CDB$ROOT happens through an intermediate INT$ view like INT$DBA_HIST_SYSSTAT.
- [12c multitenant: Cursor sharing in CDB](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12c-multitenant-cursor-sharing-in-cdb/) - 2016-03-12 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Transactions & Consistency, Cloud & Infrastructure, Operations & Observability, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12c-multitenant-cursor-sharing-in-cdb.json](dbi-services/articles/12c-multitenant-cursor-sharing-in-cdb.json)
  Evidence note: Compares library cache contention between 3000-schema consolidation on a 10g database and 50-PDB multitenant consolidation, running the same simulated connection-pool script across 50 sessions.
- [Conversion to Flex ASM with asmca takes 5 minutes](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/conversion-to-flex-asm-with-asmca-takes-5-minutes/) - 2016-03-11 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Migration & Compatibility, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/conversion-to-flex-asm-with-asmca-takes-5-minutes.json](dbi-services/articles/conversion-to-flex-asm-with-asmca-takes-5-minutes.json)
  Evidence note: Shows converting a two-node RAC cluster to Flex ASM using ASMCA's convert button, with the underlying script completing in five minutes and enabling a per-node ASM listener afterward.
- [Resource Manager plan from OEM vs. command line](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/resource-manager-plan-from-oem-vs-command-line/) - 2016-03-10 | dbi services | Oracle Database | General Database; archive: [dbi-services/articles/resource-manager-plan-from-oem-vs-command-line.json](dbi-services/articles/resource-manager-plan-from-oem-vs-command-line.json)
  Evidence note: Maps each OEM Resource Manager plan GUI field, like Utilization limit % and shares, to its corresponding DBMS_RESOURCE_MANAGER.CREATE_PLAN_DIRECTIVE argument, noting deprecated names OEM still uses.
- [Any reason to change the C## common user prefix?](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/any-reason-to-change-the-c-common-user-prefix/) - 2016-03-10 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure, Security; archive: [dbi-services/articles/any-reason-to-change-the-c-common-user-prefix.json](dbi-services/articles/any-reason-to-change-the-c-common-user-prefix.json)
  Evidence note: Shows changing common_user_prefix from C## to OPS$ so an externally-authenticated common user can connect without a password from the matching OS account, via a local bequeath connection.
- [Easy transport of SQL Tuning Sets with OEM](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/easy-transport-of-sql-tuning-sets-with-oem/) - 2016-03-08 | dbi services | Database agnostic | General Database; archive: [dbi-services/articles/easy-transport-of-sql-tuning-sets-with-oem.json](dbi-services/articles/easy-transport-of-sql-tuning-sets-with-oem.json)
  Evidence note: OEM lets you export a SQL Tuning Set to a DataPump dump file via a directory object and import it elsewhere, but importing over an existing STS name is not allowed and requires the Tuning Pack license.
- [GTT in Exadata are eligible to SmartScan](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/gtt-in-exadata-are-eligible-to-smartscan/) - 2016-03-07 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Internals & Storage, Operations & Observability, Events & Community; archive: [dbi-services/articles/gtt-in-exadata-are-eligible-to-smartscan.json](dbi-services/articles/gtt-in-exadata-are-eligible-to-smartscan.json)
  Evidence note: Tests show Exadata SmartScan predicate offloading also works on Global Temporary Tables, verified with the internal filter-predicate library and forced serial direct path reads on a GTT populated from dba_objects.
- [Standard Edition 2 testing the 16 thread limitation](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/standard-edition-2-testing-the-16-thread-limitation/) - 2016-02-26 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/standard-edition-2-testing-the-16-thread-limitation.json](dbi-services/articles/standard-edition-2-testing-the-16-thread-limitation.json)
  Evidence note: On a 32-thread cloud VM running Standard Edition 2 in 12.1.0.2, only 16 of 32 concurrently active sessions could run in CPU at once, confirming the new 16-thread cap introduced for SE2.
- [The taboo of ‘underscore parameters’](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/the-taboo-of-underscore-parameters/) - 2016-02-21 | dbi services | Oracle Database | General Database; archive: [dbi-services/articles/the-taboo-of-underscore-parameters.json](dbi-services/articles/the-taboo-of-underscore-parameters.json)
  Evidence note: Argues hidden underscore parameters are not inherently dangerous since Oracle, ISVs, and appliances like ODA and Exadata rely on them, and shows V$PARAMETER's 381 rows come from a query against X$KSPPI and X$KSPPCV.
- [12c online datafile move and ongoing changes](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12c-online-datafile-move-and-ongoing-changes/) - 2016-02-12 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Transactions & Consistency, Internals & Storage; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12c-online-datafile-move-and-ongoing-changes.json](dbi-services/articles/12c-online-datafile-move-and-ongoing-changes.json)
  Evidence note: Explains that ALTER DATABASE MOVE DATAFILE needs no lock; sessions writing to already-copied blocks just double-write until completion, with progress tracked via V$SESSION_LONGOPS.
- [SQL Plan Directives strikes again](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/sql-plan-directives-strike-again/) - 2016-02-04 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Internals & Storage, Migration & Compatibility; archive: [dbi-services/articles/sql-plan-directives-strike-again.json](dbi-services/articles/sql-plan-directives-strike-again.json)
  Evidence note: Walks through diagnosing a post-12c-migration slowdown via AWR time model and SQL ordered by Parse Calls, tracing excessive hard parsing back to SQL Plan Directives triggering dynamic sampling.
- [awrrpt and spreport in multitenant](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/awrrpt-and-spreport-in-multitenant/) - 2016-01-27 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Operations & Observability, Events & Community; archive: [dbi-services/articles/awrrpt-and-spreport-in-multitenant.json](dbi-services/articles/awrrpt-and-spreport-in-multitenant.json)
  Evidence note: Notes that running awrrpt.sql from inside a PDB under-captures SQL because AWR gathers statistics at CDB level, so per-PDB reports show a lower captured-SQL percentage than the CDB-level report.
- [OFE – Optimizer Features Enable](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/ofe-optimizer-features-enable/) - 2016-01-21 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Migration & Compatibility; archive: [dbi-services/articles/ofe-optimizer-features-enable.json](dbi-services/articles/ofe-optimizer-features-enable.json)
  Evidence note: Compares optimizer_features_enable to runtime feature-flag versioning of the optimizer code, questioning whether setting it to an older release value during a 12c upgrade is safe or reckless.
- [Execution Plan with ASH](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/execution-plan-with-ash/) - 2016-01-21 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/execution-plan-with-ash.json](dbi-services/articles/execution-plan-with-ash.json)
  Evidence note: Shares a query joining V$SQL, V$ACTIVE_SESSION_HISTORY and dbms_xplan.display_cursor to annotate each plan line with its share of ASH samples, useful when Diagnostic Pack is licensed but Tuning Pack is not.
- [DataPump ‘Processing object type’ misleading messages](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/datapump-processing-object-type-misleading-messages/) - 2016-01-18 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Indexing, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/datapump-processing-object-type-misleading-messages.json](dbi-services/articles/datapump-processing-object-type-misleading-messages.json)
  Evidence note: Using Data Pump LOGTIME=ALL timestamps, shows the 'Processing object type TABLE_STATISTICS' message doesn't reflect when the preceding TABLE_DATA export actually finished.
- [log file sync / user commits](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/log-file-sync-user-commits/) - 2016-01-10 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Transactions & Consistency, Internals & Storage, Operations & Observability, Events & Community; archive: [dbi-services/articles/log-file-sync-user-commits.json](dbi-services/articles/log-file-sync-user-commits.json)
  Evidence note: Corrects an earlier presentation slide, explaining that dividing 'log file sync' wait time by 'user commits' is misleading since each commit triggers one wait regardless of how quickly redo was already on disk.
- [PDB Snapshot using dbms_dnfs.clonedb_renamefile](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/pdb-snapshot-using-dbms_dnfs-clonedb_renamefile/) - 2016-01-02 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Backup & Recovery; archive: [dbi-services/articles/pdb-snapshot-using-dbms_dnfs-clonedb_renamefile.json](dbi-services/articles/pdb-snapshot-using-dbms_dnfs-clonedb_renamefile.json)
  Evidence note: Shows getting thin-provisioned PDB snapshot copies on a single non-multitenant-licensed CDB using dbms_dnfs.clonedb_renamefile, cloning before unplugging since plug-in rewrites file headers.
- [Flashback table after multiple drop](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/flashback-table-after-multiple-drop/) - 2016-01-02 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Indexing, Backup & Recovery, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/flashback-table-after-multiple-drop.json](dbi-services/articles/flashback-table-after-multiple-drop.json)
  Evidence note: Demonstrates FLASHBACK TABLE restoring older recycle-bin versions after a table was dropped and recreated six times, using USER_RECYCLEBIN's DROPSCN ordering to pick a specific prior incarnation.
- [PDB snapshot copy for continuous integration testing](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/pdb-snapshot-copy-for-continuous-integration-testing/) - 2016-01-01 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/pdb-snapshot-copy-for-continuous-integration-testing.json](dbi-services/articles/pdb-snapshot-copy-for-continuous-integration-testing.json)
  Evidence note: Argues multitenant lets continuous-integration tests reset state faster than flashback database, because closing and reopening a PDB avoids the instance restart flashback database requires.

### 2015

- [Phantom session environment in multitenant?](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/phantom-session-environment-in-multitenant/) - 2015-12-27 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Transactions & Consistency, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/phantom-session-environment-in-multitenant.json](dbi-services/articles/phantom-session-environment-in-multitenant.json)

- [OCM 12c preparation: RAT in multitenant](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/ocm-12c-preparation-rat-in-multitenant/) - 2015-12-20 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/ocm-12c-preparation-rat-in-multitenant.json](dbi-services/articles/ocm-12c-preparation-rat-in-multitenant.json)

- [OCM 12c preparation: documentation](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/ocm-12c-preparation-documentation/) - 2015-12-18 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Backup & Recovery, Security; archive: [dbi-services/articles/ocm-12c-preparation-documentation.json](dbi-services/articles/ocm-12c-preparation-documentation.json)
  Evidence note: Maps each OCM 12c upgrade exam topic to the exact Oracle documentation book and chapter to search during the exam, since GUI tools may lack sufficient built-in help under exam conditions.
- [OCM 12c preparation: Data Guard with OEM](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/ocm-12c-preparation-data-guard-with-oem/) - 2015-12-18 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Cloud & Infrastructure, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/ocm-12c-preparation-data-guard-with-oem.json](dbi-services/articles/ocm-12c-preparation-data-guard-with-oem.json)

- [OCM 12c preparation: Explain fast refresh](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/ocm-12c-preparation-explain-fast-refresh/) - 2015-12-17 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage; archive: [dbi-services/articles/ocm-12c-preparation-explain-fast-refresh.json](dbi-services/articles/ocm-12c-preparation-explain-fast-refresh.json)

- [OCM 12c preparation: Transportable Tablespaces](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/ocm-12c-preparation-transportable-tablespaces/) - 2015-12-15 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/ocm-12c-preparation-transportable-tablespaces.json](dbi-services/articles/ocm-12c-preparation-transportable-tablespaces.json)
  Evidence note: Traces transportable tablespaces to a 1997 patent for 'plugged-in' tablespaces, noting 12c pluggable databases extend TTS by also transporting SYSTEM, and demonstrates read-only tablespace prep.
- [SQL Plan Directive: disabling usage and column groups](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/sql-plan-directive-disabling-usage-and-column-groups/) - 2015-12-03 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Operations & Observability, Events & Community; archive: [dbi-services/articles/sql-plan-directive-disabling-usage-and-column-groups.json](dbi-services/articles/sql-plan-directive-disabling-usage-and-column-groups.json)
  Evidence note: Clarifies that disabling a SQL Plan Directive's usage stops the dynamic sampling it triggers but not dbms_stats still creating column-group extended statistics from a MISSING_STATS directive.
- [OCM 12c preparation: Manage PDB](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/ocm-12c-preparation-manage-pdb/) - 2015-11-30 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Migration & Compatibility; archive: [dbi-services/articles/ocm-12c-preparation-manage-pdb.json](dbi-services/articles/ocm-12c-preparation-manage-pdb.json)
  Evidence note: Enumerates the ways to create a PDB, from PDB$SEED, another local or remote PDB, an unplugged PDB, or a non-CDB, and shows SQL Developer's object-oriented menus alongside the statement-based documentation.
- [OCM 12c preparation: Create CDB in command line](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/ocm-12c-preparation-create-cdb-in-command-line/) - 2015-11-30 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Migration & Compatibility; archive: [dbi-services/articles/ocm-12c-preparation-create-cdb-in-command-line.json](dbi-services/articles/ocm-12c-preparation-create-cdb-in-command-line.json)

  Evidence note: First trial of AppDynamics' 15-day Linux controller, installed from a 500MB self-extracting shell script with an embedded license file, tested for tracing from user requests down to the database.
- [Real life audience vs. Twitter audience](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/real-life-audience-vs-twitter-audience/) - 2015-11-21 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Events & Community; archive: [dbi-services/articles/real-life-audience-vs-twitter-audience.json](dbi-services/articles/real-life-audience-vs-twitter-audience.json)

- [In-Memory synchronous population with hidden parameters](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/in-memory-synchronous-population-with-hidden-parameters/) - 2015-11-15 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Internals & Storage; archive: [dbi-services/articles/in-memory-synchronous-population-with-hidden-parameters.json](dbi-services/articles/in-memory-synchronous-population-with-hidden-parameters.json)

- [Interested in a deep dive of logical replication?](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/interested-in-a-deep-dive-of-logical-replication/) - 2015-11-11 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/interested-in-a-deep-dive-of-logical-replication.json](dbi-services/articles/interested-in-a-deep-dive-of-logical-replication.json)

- [RMAN channels in RAC](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/rman-channels-in-rac/) - 2015-11-05 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Internals & Storage, Backup & Recovery, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/rman-channels-in-rac.json](dbi-services/articles/rman-channels-in-rac.json)

- [Mapping In-memory Column Store to datafile Row Store extents](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/mapping-in-memory-column-store-to-datafile-row-store-extents/) - 2015-10-11 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage; archive: [dbi-services/articles/mapping-in-memory-column-store-to-datafile-row-store-extents.json](dbi-services/articles/mapping-in-memory-column-store-to-datafile-row-store-extents.json)

- [In-memory trickle repopulation](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/in-memory-trickle-repopulation/) - 2015-10-10 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Indexing, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/in-memory-trickle-repopulation.json](dbi-services/articles/in-memory-trickle-repopulation.json)
  Evidence note: Demonstrates that DML against an In-Memory table invalidates rows in the IMCU asynchronously via SMU logging, with background trickle repopulation refreshing compression units rather than in-place index updates.
- [Dbvisit replicate CDC without old values](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/dbvisit-replicate-cdc-without-old-values/) - 2015-10-07 | dbi services | Database agnostic | Transactions & Consistency, Security, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/dbvisit-replicate-cdc-without-old-values.json](dbi-services/articles/dbvisit-replicate-cdc-without-old-values.json)
  Evidence note: Configures Dbvisit Replicate's change-data-capture wizard to write only new values, not before-images, into an audit table with matching column names plus added operation, timestamp, and SCN metadata columns.
- [Oracle In-Memory Column Store for Business Intelligence at #swissbiday](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-in-memory-column-store-for-business-intelligence-at-swissbiday/) - 2015-10-06 | dbi services | Oracle Database | General Database; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-in-memory-column-store-for-business-intelligence-at-swissbiday.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-in-memory-column-store-for-business-intelligence-at-swissbiday.json)
  Evidence note: Slide deck from a Swiss BI Day talk on using the Oracle In-Memory Column Store to accelerate analytic queries for business intelligence workloads, shared afterward on SlideShare.
- [Result Cache and 12c ‘fetch first n rows’](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/result-cache-and-12c-fetch-first-n-rows/) - 2015-10-04 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Indexing, Internals & Storage, Events & Community; archive: [dbi-services/articles/result-cache-and-12c-fetch-first-n-rows.json](dbi-services/articles/result-cache-and-12c-fetch-first-n-rows.json)
  Evidence note: Shows an execution plan where a result-cache-hinted query with 12c FETCH FIRST 5 ROWS ONLY still materializes and caches the full 100K-row window function result before applying the row limit.
- [Oracle and Analytic Queries before In-Memory option](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-and-analytic-queries-before-in-memory-option/) - 2015-10-04 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Indexing, Internals & Storage; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-and-analytic-queries-before-in-memory-option.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-and-analytic-queries-before-in-memory-option.json)
  Evidence note: Defines analytic queries as needing few columns but many rows versus OLTP's single-row-by-primary-key pattern, surveying the optimization techniques Oracle used before In-Memory Column Store existed.
- [Query V$UNDOSTAT for relevant time window](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/query-vundostat-for-relevant-time-window/) - 2015-09-30 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Transactions & Consistency, Backup & Recovery, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/query-vundostat-for-relevant-time-window.json](dbi-services/articles/query-vundostat-for-relevant-time-window.json)
  Evidence note: Explains that when an ORA-01555 fires, the alert.log gives the query's start SCN and duration, showing how to convert that hex SCN into a timestamp to correlate against V$UNDOSTAT for the relevant window.
- [Nothing in feature usage statistics since 12c?](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/nothing-in-feature-usage-statistics-since-12c/) - 2015-09-29 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Backup & Recovery, Migration & Compatibility, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/nothing-in-feature-usage-statistics-since-12c.json](dbi-services/articles/nothing-in-feature-usage-statistics-since-12c.json)
  Evidence note: Traces a gap in Database Feature Usage tracking after a 12c migration to MMON's feature-usage collection job repeatedly hitting ORA-12751 after 20 minutes of CPU, caused by a dynamic-sampling-heavy internal query.
- [Do you use SQL Plan Baselines?](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/do-you-use-sql-plan-baselines/) - 2015-09-25 | dbi services | Oracle Database, Db2 | Query Optimization, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/do-you-use-sql-plan-baselines.json](dbi-services/articles/do-you-use-sql-plan-baselines.json)
  Evidence note: Argues that OLTP plan instability from adaptive features such as SPD, ADS, cardinality feedback, and bind peeking should be controlled with SQL Plan Baselines rather than disabling optimizer features wholesale.
- [You are in Standard Edition One? Don’t worry.](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/you-are-in-standard-edition-one-dont-worry/) - 2015-09-19 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Migration & Compatibility; archive: [dbi-services/articles/you-are-in-standard-edition-one-dont-worry.json](dbi-services/articles/you-are-in-standard-edition-one-dont-worry.json)
  Evidence note: Confirms that moving from Standard Edition One to Standard Edition Two costs no upgrade fee beyond a 20% higher annual maintenance, while gaining RAC eligibility and current patchsets.
- [Invisible indexes: it’s online in 12c – even in Standard Edition](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/invisible-indexes-its-online-in-12c-even-in-standard-edition/) - 2015-09-14 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Indexing, Performance & Benchmarking, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/invisible-indexes-its-online-in-12c-even-in-standard-edition.json](dbi-services/articles/invisible-indexes-its-online-in-12c-even-in-standard-edition.json)
  Evidence note: Demonstrates that marking an index invisible before dropping it is an online operation available even in Standard Edition in 12c, letting you safely test its removal before a full drop.
- [Waiting for row lock. But which row is locked?](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/waiting-for-row-lock-but-which-row-is-locked/) - 2015-09-13 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Transactions & Consistency, Internals & Storage, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/waiting-for-row-lock-but-which-row-is-locked.json](dbi-services/articles/waiting-for-row-lock-but-which-row-is-locked.json)
  Evidence note: Shows that a session blocked on row lock contention can identify the exact locked row via V$SESSION's row-wait columns, cautioning that ROW_WAIT_OBJ# is actually the DATA_OBJECT_ID, not OBJECT_ID.
- [DataBase Cloud Service performance – Network](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/database-cloud-service-performance-network/) - 2015-09-13 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/database-cloud-service-performance-network.json](dbi-services/articles/database-cloud-service-performance-network.json)
  Evidence note: Sets up SSH port-forwarding of 1521 to reach an Oracle Database Cloud Service instance, measuring roughly 90ms latency from Switzerland to the Amsterdam region versus 240ms to the US region.
- [Windows 10 – I tried and came back](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/windows-10-i-tried-and-came-back/) - 2015-09-07 | dbi services | Db2 | Migration & Compatibility, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/windows-10-i-tried-and-came-back.json](dbi-services/articles/windows-10-i-tried-and-came-back.json)
  Evidence note: Recounts rolling back from Windows 10 after VirtualBox failed to create host-only network interfaces from both the GUI and command line, making the required labs and demo environment unusable.
- [Measuring IOPS on XtremIO](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/measuring-iops-on-xtremio/) - 2015-09-07 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Internals & Storage, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/measuring-iops-on-xtremio.json](dbi-services/articles/measuring-iops-on-xtremio.json)
  Evidence note: Compares EMC XtremIO's own dashboard, Orachrome Lighty, and Enterprise Manager while running dbms_resource_manager.io_calibrate, recording about 120K IOPS and one process sustaining roughly 1.3GB/s.
- [set state=apply-off and datafile consistency](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/set-stateapply-off-and-datafile-consistency/) - 2015-09-06 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Transactions & Consistency, Internals & Storage, Backup & Recovery, Events & Community; archive: [dbi-services/articles/set-stateapply-off-and-datafile-consistency.json](dbi-services/articles/set-stateapply-off-and-datafile-consistency.json)
  Evidence note: Confirms via V$BH block status that stopping Data Guard apply with a broker state change leaves the standby's recovery blocks freed and the datafiles consistent, matching a checkpoint-like alert.log message.
- [Oracle Standard Edition Two](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-standard-edition-two/) - 2015-09-02 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-standard-edition-two.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-standard-edition-two.json)
  Evidence note: Announces Standard Edition 2 in 12.1.0.2, summarizing the new two-socket rule across single instance and RAC node and socket combinations plus the added fee for customers coming from Standard Edition One.
- [DataBase Cloud Service performance – CPU](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/database-cloud-service-performance-cpu/) - 2015-08-30 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Internals & Storage, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/database-cloud-service-performance-cpu.json](dbi-services/articles/database-cloud-service-performance-cpu.json)
  Evidence note: Runs SLOB's cached logical-read workload on a 2-OCPU Oracle Database Cloud Service VM to measure CPU-bound performance, identifying the underlying hardware as an Intel Xeon E5-2690 v2 under Xen and OVM.
- [SLOB in the Cloud: how to check Cloud Services performance](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/slob-in-the-cloud-how-to-check-cloud-services-performance/) - 2015-08-28 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Internals & Storage, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/slob-in-the-cloud-how-to-check-cloud-services-performance.json](dbi-services/articles/slob-in-the-cloud-how-to-check-cloud-services-performance.json)
  Evidence note: Walks through provisioning an Oracle Database Cloud Service VM and a latency-optimized storage volume via the console, then installing SLOB from the command line to benchmark IO performance.
- [DataBase Cloud Service performance – IOPS](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/database-cloud-service-performance-iops/) - 2015-08-28 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Internals & Storage, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/database-cloud-service-performance-iops.json](dbi-services/articles/database-cloud-service-performance-iops.json)
  Evidence note: Runs SLOB against a Cloud Service storage volume provisioned with the latency option, capturing db file sequential read wait histograms in microsecond buckets on freshly opened European cloud capacity.
- [SCN synchronization in distributed transactions](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/scn-synchronization-in-distributed-transactions/) - 2015-08-27 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Distributed Databases, Transactions & Consistency, Internals & Storage, Backup & Recovery; archive: [dbi-services/articles/scn-synchronization-in-distributed-transactions.json](dbi-services/articles/scn-synchronization-in-distributed-transactions.json)
  Evidence note: Explains that Oracle synchronizes SCNs across databases linked in a distributed transaction at each commit, advancing the lowest SCN to match the highest, useful for detecting unexpected db link usage.
- [Oracle Cloud Services DBaaS and dbi services best practices](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-cloud-services-dbaas-and-dbi-services-best-practices/) - 2015-08-27 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-cloud-services-dbaas-and-dbi-services-best-practices.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-cloud-services-dbaas-and-dbi-services-best-practices.json)
  Evidence note: Lists best practices for a freshly provisioned DBaaS instance, including lowering swappiness to avoid swapping cold shared-pool memory and disabling automatic expiration on application account passwords.
- [Oracle CDC for Datawarehouse, Dbvisit replicate as an alternative](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-cdc-for-datawarehouse-dbvisit-replicate-as-an-alternative/) - 2015-08-26 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Internals & Storage, Backup & Recovery; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-cdc-for-datawarehouse-dbvisit-replicate-as-an-alternative.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-cdc-for-datawarehouse-dbvisit-replicate-as-an-alternative.json)
  Evidence note: Notes that Oracle's built-in Change Data Capture and Streams are deprecated in favor of GoldenGate, proposing Dbvisit Replicate's redo-based logical replication as a lower-cost alternative for datawarehouse refresh.
- [Oracle Database Cloud Service – DBaaS](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-database-cloud-service-dbaas/) - 2015-08-25 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Cloud & Infrastructure, Operations & Observability, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-database-cloud-service-dbaas.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-database-cloud-service-dbaas.json)
  Evidence note: Walks through provisioning a full Database as a Service instance with two OCPUs, examining the resulting ext4-mounted storage volumes and the OCPU-to-thread compute shape mapping.
- [OCM 12c preparation: Materialized View explain_rewrite](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/materialized-view-explain_rewrite/) - 2015-08-23 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Migration & Compatibility, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/materialized-view-explain_rewrite.json](dbi-services/articles/materialized-view-explain_rewrite.json)
  Evidence note: Debugs a 12c query-rewrite failure against a materialized view built on SCOTT.DEPT and EMP, showing rewrite is only accepted once foreign key constraints are enforced, not just declared, and the view is not stale.
- [DataGuard broker properties – Part II: log shipping parameters](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/dataguard-broker-properties-part-ii-log-shipping-parameters/) - 2015-08-23 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/dataguard-broker-properties-part-ii-log-shipping-parameters.json](dbi-services/articles/dataguard-broker-properties-part-ii-log-shipping-parameters.json)
  Evidence note: Lists the Data Guard broker log-shipping properties such as LogXptMode, RedoRoutes, and ReopenSecs that are broker-managed but not imported from existing instance parameters when a database joins the configuration.
- [DataGuard broker properties – Part I: imported parameters](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/dataguard-broker-properties-part-i-imported-parameters/) - 2015-08-23 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/dataguard-broker-properties-part-i-imported-parameters.json](dbi-services/articles/dataguard-broker-properties-part-i-imported-parameters.json)
  Evidence note: Lists exactly which instance parameters such as ArchiveLagTarget, DbFileNameConvert, and StandbyFileManagement get imported into Data Guard broker properties when a database is first added to the configuration.
- [Configure the Resource Manager with SQL Developer](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/configure-the-resource-manager-with-sql-developer/) - 2015-08-23 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Migration & Compatibility, Cloud & Infrastructure, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/configure-the-resource-manager-with-sql-developer.json](dbi-services/articles/configure-the-resource-manager-with-sql-developer.json)
  Evidence note: Shows configuring Oracle Resource Manager consumer groups and CPU allocation methods through SQL Developer's DBA view in 12c, since dbconsole no longer exists and EM Express lacks that feature.
- [RESULT_CACHE hint expiration options](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/resultcache-hint-expiration-options/) - 2015-07-05 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization; archive: [dbi-services/articles/resultcache-hint-expiration-options.json](dbi-services/articles/resultcache-hint-expiration-options.json)
  Evidence note: Demonstrates using undocumented RESULT_CACHE hint options to control staleness tolerance, similar to how materialized views can serve slightly outdated results to avoid frequent invalidation on DML.
- [Multitenant vs. schema based consolidation](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/multitenant-vs-schema-based-consolidation/) - 2015-06-30 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Transactions & Consistency, Backup & Recovery, Security, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/multitenant-vs-schema-based-consolidation.json](dbi-services/articles/multitenant-vs-schema-based-consolidation.json)
  Evidence note: Argues most objections to schema-based consolidation such as naming collisions and weak security are overstated, but identifies cursor sharing across schemas as the one genuinely strong reason to prefer PDB isolation.
- [Oracle Log Writer and Write-Ahead-Logging](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-log-writer-and-write-ahead-logging/) - 2015-06-28 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Transactions & Consistency, Internals & Storage, Backup & Recovery; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-log-writer-and-write-ahead-logging.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-log-writer-and-write-ahead-logging.json)
  Evidence note: Revisits a 20-year-old Oracle 7.2 internals document on write-ahead logging to verify, against Jonathan Lewis's Oracle Core, that DBWR must not write a modified block before LGWR writes its redo.
- [Oracle Database Cloud Service – My first trial](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-database-cloud-service-my-first-trial/) - 2015-06-27 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure, Data Modeling & SQL, Development & Drivers; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-database-cloud-service-my-first-trial.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-database-cloud-service-my-first-trial.json)
  Evidence note: Recounts a frustrating first sign-up for Oracle's cloud Database Schema Service trial, including a stray leading space in the emailed username that blocked login until retracing the original activation link.
- [RAC buffer states: XCUR, SCUR, PI, CI](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/rac-buffer-states-xcur-scur-pi-ci/) - 2015-06-25 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage; archive: [dbi-services/articles/rac-buffer-states-xcur-scur-pi-ci.json](dbi-services/articles/rac-buffer-states-xcur-scur-pi-ci.json)
  Evidence note: Demonstrates RAC Cache Fusion buffer states by querying a block from different instances, showing it move from shared current with a single reader to multiple shared copies as more instances read it via GV$BH.
- [Multithreaded 12c and ‘connect / as sysdba’](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/multithreaded-12c-and-connect-as-sysdba/) - 2015-06-24 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Security; archive: [dbi-services/articles/multithreaded-12c-and-connect-as-sysdba.json](dbi-services/articles/multithreaded-12c-and-connect-as-sysdba.json)
  Evidence note: Shows that enabling 12c threaded_execution disables Bequeath OS authentication, so connecting locally as sysdba without a password fails with ORA-01017 unless a password wallet is configured.
- [Does the block size matter?](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/do-the-block-size-matter/) - 2015-06-23 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Indexing, Internals & Storage, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/do-the-block-size-matter.json](dbi-services/articles/do-the-block-size-matter.json)
  Evidence note: Benchmarks full table scan and index access across 8k, 16k, and 32k block sizes using dedicated cache sizes and locally managed tablespaces, informing a recommendation to keep the default 8k for most workloads.
- [Buffer pool advisory in AWR](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/buffer-pool-advisory-in-awr/) - 2015-06-23 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/buffer-pool-advisory-in-awr.json](dbi-services/articles/buffer-pool-advisory-in-awr.json)
  Evidence note: Validates that DBA_HIST_DB_CACHE_ADVICE's estimated reads at the current buffer size closely track actual physical-reads-cache values across AWR snapshots, confirming advisors reset only at startup.
- [2 ways to move archivelogs – both need RMAN](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/2-ways-to-move-archivelogs-both-need-rman/) - 2015-06-23 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Backup & Recovery; archive: [dbi-services/articles/2-ways-to-move-archivelogs-both-need-rman.json](dbi-services/articles/2-ways-to-move-archivelogs-both-need-rman.json)
  Evidence note: Explains RMAN's CATALOG command can register archivelogs already moved at OS level, while a separate command can move and re-catalog them together, since the controlfile must always reflect the actual file location.
- [Show the Top 10 events from latest AWR snapshot](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/show-the-top-10-events-from-latest-awr-snapshot/) - 2015-06-12 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/show-the-top-10-events-from-latest-awr-snapshot.json](dbi-services/articles/show-the-top-10-events-from-latest-awr-snapshot.json)
  Evidence note: Shares a script that pulls a specific named section, such as Top 10 Foreground Events, from the latest AWR snapshot via DBMS_WORKLOAD_REPOSITORY.AWR_REPORT_TEXT, filtered with a regexp parameter.
- [Parallel DML in 12c](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/parallel-dml-in-12c/) - 2015-06-12 | dbi services | Oracle Database | General Database; archive: [dbi-services/articles/parallel-dml-in-12c.json](dbi-services/articles/parallel-dml-in-12c.json)
  Evidence note: Reproduces a colleague's question about enabling parallel DML at the statement level in 12.1.0.2, testing an insert-select between parallel-defined tables with statistics_level=all to confirm the behavior.
- [Oracle memory advisors: how relevant ?](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-memory-advisors-how-relevant/) - 2015-06-12 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Internals & Storage, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-memory-advisors-how-relevant.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-memory-advisors-how-relevant.json)
  Evidence note: Queries V$DB_CACHE_ADVISOR's estimated physical reads at different buffer sizes and finds the advisor is based on statistics accumulated since instance startup, potentially spanning months of unrepresentative activity.
- [How to import SQL Plan Directives](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/how-to-import-sql-plan-directives/) - 2015-06-12 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Migration & Compatibility; archive: [dbi-services/articles/how-to-import-sql-plan-directives.json](dbi-services/articles/how-to-import-sql-plan-directives.json)
  Evidence note: Shows packing SQL Plan Directives into a staging table with dbms_spd, then exporting and importing via Data Pump so directives disabled after QA testing can be carried into production in the same state.
- [How to disable a SQL Plan Directive permanently](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/how-to-disable-a-sql-plan-directive-permanently/) - 2015-06-12 | dbi services | Oracle Database | General Database; archive: [dbi-services/articles/how-to-disable-a-sql-plan-directive-permanently.json](dbi-services/articles/how-to-disable-a-sql-plan-directive-permanently.json)
  Evidence note: Shows that a SQL Plan Directive disabled a year earlier stays superseded and inactive, but the same predicates recreate a new directive since the disabled one no longer suppresses detection of the misestimate.
- [Flame Graph for quick identification of Oracle bug](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/flame-graph-for-quick-identification-of-oracle-bug/) - 2015-06-12 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking; archive: [dbi-services/articles/flame-graph-for-quick-identification-of-oracle-bug.json](dbi-services/articles/flame-graph-for-quick-identification-of-oracle-bug.json)
  Evidence note: Uses ASH to spot that a 2-hour parse phase precedes execution of a slow query, then captures perf cpu-cycles samples and renders them as a flame graph to visually pinpoint where the parse time is spent.
- [DBA_SQL_PLAN_DIRECTIVE.LAST_USED](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/dba_sql_plan_directive-last_used/) - 2015-06-12 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/dba_sql_plan_directive-last_used.json](dbi-services/articles/dba_sql_plan_directive-last_used.json)
  Evidence note: Demonstrates how the plus-metrics dbms_xplan format reveals which specific SQL Plan Directive ID was used for dynamic sampling in a query, then shows that directive's LAST_USED timestamp updating afterward.
- [Convert a snapshot standby to a new database](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/convert-a-snapshot-standby-to-a-new-database/) - 2015-06-12 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Backup & Recovery, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/convert-a-snapshot-standby-to-a-new-database.json](dbi-services/articles/convert-a-snapshot-standby-to-a-new-database.json)
  Evidence note: Documents converting a Data Guard snapshot standby into a permanent independent database with a new name and DBID, useful when the ideal recovery point isn't known in advance and must be found first.
- [Can you have pending system statistics?](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/can-you-have-pending-system-statistics/) - 2015-06-12 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/can-you-have-pending-system-statistics.json](dbi-services/articles/can-you-have-pending-system-statistics.json)
  Evidence note: Tests whether system statistics, unlike object statistics, support a pending and publish workflow in 11g, comparing full table scan cost estimates before and after altering SYS.AUX_STATS$ values directly.
- [An alternative to DBA_EXTENTS optimized for LMT](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/an-alternative-to-dba_extents-optimized-for-lmt/) - 2015-06-12 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage; archive: [dbi-services/articles/an-alternative-to-dba_extents-optimized-for-lmt.json](dbi-services/articles/an-alternative-to-dba_extents-optimized-for-lmt.json)
  Evidence note: Shares a datafile-map view built on the underlying LMT extent fixed tables instead of DBA_EXTENTS, avoiding costly joins so mapping a file_id and block_id to its segment stays fast with thousands of datafiles.
- [12c Dynamic Sampling and Standard Edition](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12c-dynamic-sampling-and-standard-edition/) - 2015-06-12 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12c-dynamic-sampling-and-standard-edition.json](dbi-services/articles/12c-dynamic-sampling-and-standard-edition.json)
  Evidence note: Explains 12c Adaptive Dynamic Sampling runs its sampling queries with a result-cache snapshot hint so repeated dynamic sampling for the same statement is cached for an hour instead of re-run each time.
- [List all RMAN backups that are needed to recover](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/list-all-rman-backups-that-are-needed-to-recover/) - 2015-05-20 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Backup & Recovery, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/list-all-rman-backups-that-are-needed-to-recover.json](dbi-services/articles/list-all-rman-backups-that-are-needed-to-recover.json)
  Evidence note: Argues against relying on backup-job timestamps to identify required restore files when concurrent archivelog backups are allowed, proposing a generated list of exactly the RMAN pieces needed for a point-in-time restore.
- [Variations on 1M rows insert(2): commit write](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/variations-on-1m-rows-insert2-commit-write/) - 2015-05-18 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Indexing; archive: [dbi-services/articles/variations-on-1m-rows-insert2-commit-write.json](dbi-services/articles/variations-on-1m-rows-insert2-commit-write.json)
  Evidence note: Adds a commit after every row in the 1M-row PL/SQL insert benchmark, measuring the added durability cost of one log file sync wait per row versus committing only once at the end.
- [Variations on 1M rows insert (5): using ‘perf’](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/variations-on-1m-rows-insert-5-using-perf/) - 2015-05-18 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking; archive: [dbi-services/articles/variations-on-1m-rows-insert-5-using-perf.json](dbi-services/articles/variations-on-1m-rows-insert-5-using-perf.json)
  Evidence note: Switches from VirtualBox to VMware with virtualized CPU performance counters to get valid perf cpu-cycles samples, confirming a 1M-row insert test case consistently takes about twice as long on 11.2.0.4 and 12c.
- [Variations on 1M rows insert (4): IN MEMORY](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/variations-on-1m-rows-insert-4-in-memory/) - 2015-05-18 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Indexing, Internals & Storage; archive: [dbi-services/articles/variations-on-1m-rows-insert-4-in-memory.json](dbi-services/articles/variations-on-1m-rows-insert-4-in-memory.json)
  Evidence note: Tests the 1M-row PL/SQL insert benchmark against a 12c IN MEMORY table, finding it still takes roughly twice as long as the 11.2.0.3 baseline, consistent with a CPU regression rather than an In-Memory-specific effect.
- [Variations on 1M rows insert (4): APPEND_VALUES](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/variations-on-1m-rows-insert-4-append_values/) - 2015-05-18 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Indexing, Transactions & Consistency, Internals & Storage; archive: [dbi-services/articles/variations-on-1m-rows-insert-4-append_values.json](dbi-services/articles/variations-on-1m-rows-insert-4-append_values.json)
  Evidence note: Adds the APPEND_VALUES hint to a bulk PL/SQL FORALL insert of 1M rows, cutting elapsed time further by performing direct-path inserts straight into the datafile and bypassing the buffer cache.
- [Variations on 1M rows insert (3): TimesTen](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/variations-on-1m-rows-insert-3-timesten/) - 2015-05-18 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Development & Drivers; archive: [dbi-services/articles/variations-on-1m-rows-insert-3-timesten.json](dbi-services/articles/variations-on-1m-rows-insert-3-timesten.json)
  Evidence note: Runs the 1M-row insert benchmark against Oracle TimesTen configured with 400MB permanent and 320MB temporary memory via ttIsql, comparing its in-memory OLTP performance against the same test on Oracle Database.
- [Variations on 1M rows insert (1): bulk insert](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/variations-on-1m-rows-insert-1-bulk-insert/) - 2015-05-18 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking; archive: [dbi-services/articles/variations-on-1m-rows-insert-1-bulk-insert.json](dbi-services/articles/variations-on-1m-rows-insert-1-bulk-insert.json)
  Evidence note: Kicks off a benchmark series by timing a single-CPU VM inserting 1 million rows via row-by-row PL/SQL with one primary key index and a single final commit, as the baseline for later comparisons.
- [Variations on 1M insert (6): CPU Flame Graph](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/variations-on-1m-insert-6-cpu-flame-graph/) - 2015-05-18 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Indexing, Performance & Benchmarking; archive: [dbi-services/articles/variations-on-1m-insert-6-cpu-flame-graph.json](dbi-services/articles/variations-on-1m-insert-6-cpu-flame-graph.json)
  Evidence note: Uses perf-captured CPU samples turned into flame graphs to investigate why a 1M-row PL/SQL insert test case takes roughly twice as long in 11.2.0.4 as in 11.2.0.3, zooming into the underlying call path.
- [SQL Monitor report little improvement in 12c](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/sql-monitor-report-little-improvement-in-12c/) - 2015-05-18 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Performance & Benchmarking, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/sql-monitor-report-little-improvement-in-12c.json](dbi-services/articles/sql-monitor-report-little-improvement-in-12c.json)
  Evidence note: Notes that 11g SQL Monitoring active reports split CPU and wait-event percentages into separate columns, misleadingly making wait events look dominant, while 12c merges them into one activity-percent column.
- [Rebuilt index on increasing values after deletes?](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/rebuilt-index-on-increasing-values-after-deletes/) - 2015-05-18 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Indexing, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/rebuilt-index-on-increasing-values-after-deletes.json](dbi-services/articles/rebuilt-index-on-increasing-values-after-deletes.json)
  Evidence note: Investigates whether indexes on sequence-generated increasing values need periodic rebuilding after purge-style deletes, using dbms_space and index_stats to check whether freed leaf space is actually reused.
- [Matching SQL Plan Directives and queries using it](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/matching-sql-plan-directives-and-queries-using-it/) - 2015-05-18 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Internals & Storage, Operations & Observability, Events & Community; archive: [dbi-services/articles/matching-sql-plan-directives-and-queries-using-it.json](dbi-services/articles/matching-sql-plan-directives-and-queries-using-it.json)
  Evidence note: Shows that matching a query to the specific SQL Plan Directive it used requires an EXPLAIN PLAN and reading PLAN_TABLE.OTHER_XML, since V$SQL_PLAN and the plan note alone don't expose the directive ID.
- [Matching SQL Plan Directives and extended stats](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/matching-sql-plan-directives-and-extended-stats/) - 2015-05-18 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Migration & Compatibility, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/matching-sql-plan-directives-and-extended-stats.json](dbi-services/articles/matching-sql-plan-directives-and-extended-stats.json)
  Evidence note: Shows there is no direct link between DBA_SQL_PLAN_DIRECTIVES and DBA_STATS_EXTENSIONS, so matching a directive to the extended statistics it triggered requires parsing each one's differently formatted column list.
- [List listeners and services from the instance](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/list-listeners-and-services-from-the-instance/) - 2015-05-18 | dbi services | Oracle Database | General Database; archive: [dbi-services/articles/list-listeners-and-services-from-the-instance.json](dbi-services/articles/list-listeners-and-services-from-the-instance.json)
  Evidence note: Shows querying the undocumented GV$LISTENER_NETWORK view, available since 11.2, to list every listener including SCAN listeners and their services directly from the instance rather than listener.ora.
- [C15LV: Nothing to BLOG About – Think Again](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/c15lv-nothing-to-blog-about-think-again/) - 2015-05-18 | dbi services | Database agnostic | Internals & Storage, Events & Community; archive: [dbi-services/articles/c15lv-nothing-to-blog-about-think-again.json](dbi-services/articles/c15lv-nothing-to-blog-about-think-again.json)
  Evidence note: Recaps an IOUG Collaborate panel encouraging reluctant bloggers, then recounts starting on forums like dba-village and developpez.com before Jonathan Lewis's translated-post model inspired an independent blog.
- [ALTER TABLE INMEMORY](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/alter-table-inmemory/) - 2015-05-18 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Indexing, Performance & Benchmarking; archive: [dbi-services/articles/alter-table-inmemory.json](dbi-services/articles/alter-table-inmemory.json)
  Evidence note: Warns that flipping a table to INMEMORY immediately changes the optimizer's chosen full-table-scan plan to expect the column store even before the table has actually been populated in memory.
- [A migration pitfall with ALL COLUMN SIZE AUTO](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/a-migration-pitfall-with-all-column-size-auto/) - 2015-05-18 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Indexing, Performance & Benchmarking, Migration & Compatibility, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/a-migration-pitfall-with-all-column-size-auto.json](dbi-services/articles/a-migration-pitfall-with-all-column-size-auto.json)
  Evidence note: Reproduces a post-migration performance regression where several histograms went missing despite identical automatic stats-gathering jobs, using a skewed status column and index to demonstrate the pitfall.
- [Yesterday’s AWR Straight to the Goal](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/yesterdays-awr-straight-to-the-goal/) - 2015-04-14 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Data Modeling & SQL, Events & Community; archive: [dbi-services/articles/yesterdays-awr-straight-to-the-goal.json](dbi-services/articles/yesterdays-awr-straight-to-the-goal.json)
  Evidence note: Recaps a Collaborate15 AWR-reading talk, noting about half the room lacked Diagnostic Pack, and explains setting Statspack to level 7 so those users can follow the same time-model-to-SQL methodology.
- [Cloning a PDB from a standby database](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/cloning-a-pdb-from-a-standby-database/) - 2015-04-13 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Internals & Storage; archive: [dbi-services/articles/cloning-a-pdb-from-a-standby-database.json](dbi-services/articles/cloning-a-pdb-from-a-standby-database.json)
  Evidence note: Confirms that a single-tenant physical standby opened read-only can serve as the source for PDB cloning, since PDB clone like transportable tablespaces only requires the source to be read-only, not the primary.
- [RAC Attack! was another great success at C15LV](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/rac-attack-was-another-great-success-at-c15lv/) - 2015-04-12 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Security, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/rac-attack-was-another-great-success-at-c15lv.json](dbi-services/articles/rac-attack-was-another-great-success-at-c15lv.json)
  Evidence note: Recaps a hands-on RAC Attack workshop at Collaborate15 where attendees install a RAC cluster on their own laptop from a wikibook cookbook, with VirtualBox and networking misconfiguration as the main exercise.
- [Dbvisit replicate REDO_READ_METHOD](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/dbvisit-replicate-redoreadmethod/) - 2015-04-09 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Internals & Storage, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/dbvisit-replicate-redoreadmethod.json](dbi-services/articles/dbvisit-replicate-redoreadmethod.json)
  Evidence note: Configures Dbvisit Replicate's fetcher process to read only archived logs instead of the online redo logs, reducing the read overhead placed on a production source during a proof of concept.
- [DataGuard wait events have changed in 12c](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/dataguard-wait-events-have-changed-in-12c/) - 2015-04-08 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Transactions & Consistency, Internals & Storage, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/dataguard-wait-events-have-changed-in-12c.json](dbi-services/articles/dataguard-wait-events-have-changed-in-12c.json)
  Evidence note: Compares an 11g synchronous Data Guard AWR report, where sessions wait behind LNS and LGWR-LNS channel waits, against the renamed and restructured wait events introduced by 12c's new Data Guard network processes.
- [Oracle tuning silver bullet: add an order by to make your query faster](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-tuning-silver-bullet-add-an-order-by-to-make-your-query-faster/) - 2015-04-01 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Performance & Benchmarking, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-tuning-silver-bullet-add-an-order-by-to-make-your-query-faster.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-tuning-silver-bullet-add-an-order-by-to-make-your-query-faster.json)
  Evidence note: Demonstrates a full table scan of a padded 10000-row table running over 20 seconds under autotrace, then shows adding an order by makes SQL*Plus appear faster by changing how rows are fetched and displayed.
- [Index on SUBSTR(string,1,n) – do you still need old index?](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/index-on-truncdate-do-you-still-need-old-index-1/) - 2015-03-27 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Indexing; archive: [dbi-services/articles/index-on-truncdate-do-you-still-need-old-index-1.json](dbi-services/articles/index-on-truncdate-do-you-still-need-old-index-1.json)
  Evidence note: Extends an earlier trunc-date finding to substring predicates, showing Oracle can use an index on the full string for range predicates on a substring prefix from 12.1.0.2 onward, without a separate index.
- [Standard Edition on Oracle Database Appliance](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/standard-edition-on-oracle-database-appliance/) - 2015-03-25 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, High Availability & Replication, Internals & Storage; archive: [dbi-services/articles/standard-edition-on-oracle-database-appliance.json](dbi-services/articles/standard-edition-on-oracle-database-appliance.json)
  Evidence note: Argues ODA remains a good fit for small companies despite bundling Enterprise Edition by default, since it avoids requiring in-house storage, network, and RAC expertise while offering flash performance and easy HA.
- [12c: shutdown abort a PDB?](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12c-shutdown-abort-a-pdb/) - 2015-03-16 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Backup & Recovery, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12c-shutdown-abort-a-pdb.json](dbi-services/articles/12c-shutdown-abort-a-pdb.json)
  Evidence note: Shows that issuing shutdown abort from within a PDB session actually performs a clean close-abort with a full checkpoint, leaving datafiles non-fuzzy, unlike a true instance-crashing shutdown abort at CDB level.
- [Index on trunc(date) – do you still need old index?](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/index-on-truncdate-do-you-still-need-old-index/) - 2015-03-15 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Indexing; archive: [dbi-services/articles/index-on-truncdate-do-you-still-need-old-index.json](dbi-services/articles/index-on-truncdate-do-you-still-need-old-index.json)
  Evidence note: Demonstrates that from 11.2.0.2 the optimizer can use a plain index on a date column for a trunc-date equality predicate without a function-based index, shown by resetting optimizer_features_enable to an older release.
- [Oracle compression, availability and licensing](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-compression-availability-and-licensing/) - 2015-03-06 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Transactions & Consistency, Internals & Storage; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-compression-availability-and-licensing.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-compression-availability-and-licensing.json)
  Evidence note: Untangles the renamed and overlapping compression features, clarifying which require the Advanced Compression Option, which need Exadata, and which are usable in plain nocompress-only Standard Edition.
- [RAC Attack! next month 12c in Las Vegas](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/rac-attack-next-month-12c-in-las-vegas/) - 2015-03-03 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Internals & Storage, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/rac-attack-next-month-12c-in-las-vegas.json](dbi-services/articles/rac-attack-next-month-12c-in-las-vegas.json)
  Evidence note: Previews the RAC Attack workshop at IOUG Collaborate15, describing RAC as spanning storage, network, and application-level failover skills, inviting laptop-equipped attendees to build a 12c cluster hands-on.
- [Generic query for multicriteria search – part II: BIND_AWARE (Adaptive Cursor Sharing)](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/generic-query-for-multicriteria-search-part-ii-bindaware-adaptive-cursor-sharing/) - 2015-03-03 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking; archive: [dbi-services/articles/generic-query-for-multicriteria-search-part-ii-bindaware-adaptive-cursor-sharing.json](dbi-services/articles/generic-query-for-multicriteria-search-part-ii-bindaware-adaptive-cursor-sharing.json)
  Evidence note: Adds the BIND_AWARE hint to an earlier generic multicriteria search query, showing Adaptive Cursor Sharing then generates plan variants keyed to actual bind selectivity instead of sharing one plan for all combinations.
- [Generic query for multicriteria search – part I: USE_CONCAT (OR Expansion)](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/generic-query-for-multicriteria-search-part-i-useconcat-or-expansion/) - 2015-03-03 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Indexing, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/generic-query-for-multicriteria-search-part-i-useconcat-or-expansion.json](dbi-services/articles/generic-query-for-multicriteria-search-part-i-useconcat-or-expansion.json)
  Evidence note: Shows a generic multi-column optional-filter search query on the HR schema shares one plan across all bind combinations, convenient for maintainability but suboptimal versus dynamic SQL or OR expansion.
- [Query the Enterprise Manager collected metrics](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/query-the-enterprise-manager-collected-metrics/) - 2015-03-02 | dbi services | Oracle Database, Microsoft SQL Server | High Availability & Replication, Internals & Storage, Cloud & Infrastructure, Operations & Observability, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/query-the-enterprise-manager-collected-metrics.json](dbi-services/articles/query-the-enterprise-manager-collected-metrics.json)
  Evidence note: Shows querying Enterprise Manager's SYSMAN repository directly for ASM disk group free-space history, joining target and raw metric tables that keep 7 days of detail rather than the coarser hourly and daily aggregates.
- [How to set NLS for SQL Developer](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/how-to-set-nls-for-sql-developer/) - 2015-02-21 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Migration & Compatibility, Development & Drivers; archive: [dbi-services/articles/how-to-set-nls-for-sql-developer.json](dbi-services/articles/how-to-set-nls-for-sql-developer.json)
  Evidence note: Shows setting Java user.language and file.encoding options when launching SQL Developer's beta command line, needed because its thin JDBC driver ignores NLS_LANG and defaults to the OS locale.
- [12c online statistics gathering and startup restrict](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12c-online-statistics-gathering-and-startup-restrict/) - 2015-02-21 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Operations & Observability, Events & Community; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12c-online-statistics-gathering-and-startup-restrict.json](dbi-services/articles/12c-online-statistics-gathering-and-startup-restrict.json)
  Evidence note: Traces 12c online statistics gathering by enabling a dbms_stats trace flag during an append insert, documenting cases including startup restrict scenarios where online gathering silently doesn't fire.
- [Is CDB stable after one patchset and two PSU?](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/is-cdb-stable-now-after-one-patchset-and-two-psu/) - 2015-02-16 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Backup & Recovery, Operations & Observability, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/is-cdb-stable-now-after-one-patchset-and-two-psu.json](dbi-services/articles/is-cdb-stable-now-after-one-patchset-and-two-psu.json)
  Evidence note: Retests a known 12.1.0.1 bug where losing a PDB's SYSTEM datafile crashed the whole CDB, confirming via RMAN recovery of a dropped non-system tablespace that it is fixed by the 12.1.0.2 patchset with the latest PSU.
- [OracleText: inserts and fragmentation](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracletext-inserts-and-fragmentation/) - 2015-02-15 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Indexing, Internals & Storage, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracletext-inserts-and-fragmentation.json](dbi-services/articles/oracletext-inserts-and-fragmentation.json)
  Evidence note: Starts an OracleText internals series showing that inserting a document into a CONTEXT-indexed CLOB column doesn't immediately populate the index tables, since token synchronization is deferred until an explicit sync.
- [OracleText: deletes and garbage](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracletext-deletes-and-garbage/) - 2015-02-15 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Indexing, Internals & Storage; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracletext-deletes-and-garbage.json](dbi-services/articles/oracletext-deletes-and-garbage.json)
  Evidence note: Continues an OracleText internals series, showing how the token, key, and reverse-lookup index tables are updated when documents are removed, treating an update as a delete plus insert rather than an in-place change.
- [Never gather WORKLOAD stats on Exadata…](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/never-gather-workload-stats-on-exadata/) - 2015-02-09 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/never-gather-workload-stats-on-exadata.json](dbi-services/articles/never-gather-workload-stats-on-exadata.json)
  Evidence note: Demonstrates that gathering WORKLOAD statistics on Exadata during direct-path reads undercounts MBRC, since those reads aren't counted as multiblock reads, so use the dedicated EXADATA statistics mode instead.
- [Oracle multitenant dictionary: rowcache](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-multitenant-dictionary-rowcache/) - 2015-02-08 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-multitenant-dictionary-rowcache.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-multitenant-dictionary-rowcache.json)
  Evidence note: Creates a sharing=metadata function in both CDB$ROOT and a PDB, then flushes the shared pool to show how the row cache dictionary component repopulates separately per container for that metadata-linked object.
- [IOUG Collaborate #C15LV](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/ioug-collaborate-c15lv/) - 2015-02-03 | dbi services | Oracle Database | General Database; archive: [dbi-services/articles/ioug-collaborate-c15lv.json](dbi-services/articles/ioug-collaborate-c15lv.json)
  Evidence note: Previews the author's AWR-reading session and RAC Attack ninja participation at IOUG Collaborate 2015 in Las Vegas, linking session details and hands-on cluster-building registration.
- [Flush one SQL statement to hard parse it again](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/flush-one-sql-statement-to-hard-parse-it-again/) - 2015-01-29 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Indexing, Performance & Benchmarking; archive: [dbi-services/articles/flush-one-sql-statement-to-hard-parse-it-again.json](dbi-services/articles/flush-one-sql-statement-to-hard-parse-it-again.json)
  Evidence note: Shows how to force one specific cursor to hard-parse again without flushing the whole shared pool, illustrated through a bind-variable-peeking demo on a skewed year-of-birth histogram column.
- [Stay with non-CDB or go to CDB?](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/stay-with-non-cdb-or-go-to-cdb/) - 2015-01-28 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Migration & Compatibility, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/stay-with-non-cdb-or-go-to-cdb.json](dbi-services/articles/stay-with-non-cdb-or-go-to-cdb.json)
  Evidence note: Responds to Oracle's deprecation of the non-CDB architecture by explaining the CDB and PDB dictionary split, user metadata per PDB versus system metadata shared via root links, and what each migration path implies.
- [When to gather workload system statistics?](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/when-to-gather-workload-system-statistics/) - 2015-01-23 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/when-to-gather-workload-system-statistics.json](dbi-services/articles/when-to-gather-workload-system-statistics.json)
  Evidence note: Shares a query against AWR history to chart how irregular WORKLOAD system statistics can be across an 8-day OLTP production period, arguing for either multi-day gathering or manually chosen representative values.
- [NLS_LANG on Windows in Europe: WE8MSWIN1252 or WE8PC850?](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/nlslang-on-windows-in-europe-we8mswin1252-or-we8pc850/) - 2015-01-15 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Development & Drivers; archive: [dbi-services/articles/nlslang-on-windows-in-europe-we8mswin1252-or-we8pc850.json](dbi-services/articles/nlslang-on-windows-in-europe-we8mswin1252-or-we8pc850.json)
  Evidence note: Explains that Windows GUI apps default to WE8MSWIN1252 in the registry, but the DOS console SQL*Plus runs under codepage 850, so NLS_LANG must be set to WE8PC850 to display French accented characters correctly.
- [Insert into GTT: bulk with APPEND_VALUES](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/insert-into-gtt-bulk-with-appendvalues/) - 2015-01-09 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/insert-into-gtt-bulk-with-appendvalues.json](dbi-services/articles/insert-into-gtt-bulk-with-appendvalues.json)
  Evidence note: Investigates a GTT accumulating excessive undo from single-row inserts used to pass variable-length value lists, showing bulk inserts with the APPEND_VALUES hint reduce the redo and undo overhead of that pattern.
- [Flashback logging overhead: ‘db file sequential read’ on UNDO tablespace](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/flashback-logging-overhead-db-file-sequential-read-on-undo-tablespace/) - 2015-01-09 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Backup & Recovery; archive: [dbi-services/articles/flashback-logging-overhead-db-file-sequential-read-on-undo-tablespace.json](dbi-services/articles/flashback-logging-overhead-db-file-sequential-read-on-undo-tablespace.json)
  Evidence note: Compares tkprof statistics with and without flashback logging enabled, showing flashback logging forces overwritten UNDO blocks to be read from disk before being logged, adding physical reads not seen otherwise.

- [Oracle multitenant dictionary: metadata links](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-multitenant-dictionary-metadata-links/) - 2015-01-05 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Backup & Recovery, Migration & Compatibility, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-multitenant-dictionary-metadata-links.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-multitenant-dictionary-metadata-links.json)

### 2014
