- [Partial Join Evaluation in Oracle 12c](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/partial-join-evaluation-in-oracle-12c/) - 2014-07-14 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/partial-join-evaluation-in-oracle-12c.json](dbi-services/articles/partial-join-evaluation-in-oracle-12c.json)
  Evidence note: Demonstrates 12c's Partial Join Evaluation transformation improving semi-join queries written with IN by evaluating only enough of the join to confirm existence, compared against the equivalent 11g execution plan.
- [Oracle Parallel Query: Did you use MapReduce for years without knowing it?](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-parallel-query-are-you-doing-mapreduce-for-years-without-knowing-it/) - 2014-06-26 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Distributed Databases; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-parallel-query-are-you-doing-mapreduce-for-years-without-knowing-it.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-parallel-query-are-you-doing-mapreduce-for-years-without-knowing-it.json)
  Evidence note: Argues Oracle Parallel Query has delivered MapReduce-like distributed filter, aggregation, and join scalability for 20 years via a simple ALTER TABLE PARALLEL, illustrated with a basic parallel join execution plan.
- [Linux: how to monitor the nofile limit](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/linux-how-to-monitor-the-nofiles-limit/) - 2014-06-18 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/linux-how-to-monitor-the-nofiles-limit.json](dbi-services/articles/linux-how-to-monitor-the-nofiles-limit.json)
  Evidence note: Shows counting genuine file-descriptor usage per process against the Linux nofile ulimit by filtering lsof output, since naively counting all lsof lines overcounts entries that aren't actual open descriptors.
- [Linux: how to monitor the nproc limit](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/linux-how-to-monitor-the-nproc-limit-1/) - 2014-06-10 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Operations & Observability, Development & Drivers; archive: [dbi-services/articles/linux-how-to-monitor-the-nproc-limit-1.json](dbi-services/articles/linux-how-to-monitor-the-nproc-limit-1.json)
  Evidence note: Shows that counting processes per user undercounts because Linux implements threads as light-weight processes needing a special ps flag, revealing a system with 1370 processes and threads approaching the nproc limit.
- [How to list all Oracle system schemas](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-system-schemas-vs-created-users/) - 2014-06-05 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-system-schemas-vs-created-users.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-system-schemas-vs-created-users.json)
  Evidence note: Surveys ways to distinguish Oracle-maintained schemas from user-created ones, including 12c's oracle-maintained and common-user columns in DBA_USERS, the default password table, and sysaux occupant descriptions.
- [Oracle Exadata – poor optimization for FIRST_ROWS](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/poor-optimization-for-firstrows-in-exadata/) - 2014-05-28 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Indexing, Internals & Storage, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/poor-optimization-for-firstrows-in-exadata.json](dbi-services/articles/poor-optimization-for-firstrows-in-exadata.json)
  Evidence note: Shows a highly selective query on Exadata performing worse with a full SmartScan table scan than with a forced bad index access, because a first-rows-style stop-key plan still fully sorts and scans before limiting rows.
- [Oracle 12c extended datatypes better than CLOB?](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/12c-extended-datatypes-better-than-clob/) - 2014-05-23 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Performance & Benchmarking, Internals & Storage, Migration & Compatibility; archive: [dbi-services/articles/12c-extended-datatypes-better-than-clob.json](dbi-services/articles/12c-extended-datatypes-better-than-clob.json)
  Evidence note: Weighs 12c's extended VARCHAR2, CHAR, and RAW datatypes against CLOB, noting the new types store as chained rows unless created fresh, at which point they behave like LOBs including the same slow row-by-row fetch.
- [Oracle SQL Monitoring reports in flash, html, text](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-sql-monitoring-reports-in-flash-html-text/) - 2014-05-15 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-sql-monitoring-reports-in-flash-html-text.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-sql-monitoring-reports-in-flash-html-text.json)
  Evidence note: Shows extracting the plain-text execution plan from a 12c SQL Monitoring active report by base64-decoding and zlib-decompressing the embedded XML, since 12c compresses that data unlike the readable 11g report format.
- [Exploring Oracle SE & EE performance statistics with Orachrome Lighty](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/exploring-oracle-se-a-ee-performance-statistics-with-orachrome-lighty/) - 2014-05-12 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Performance & Benchmarking, Operations & Observability, Development & Drivers; archive: [dbi-services/articles/exploring-oracle-se-a-ee-performance-statistics-with-orachrome-lighty.json](dbi-services/articles/exploring-oracle-se-a-ee-performance-statistics-with-orachrome-lighty.json)
  Evidence note: Walks through installing Orachrome Lighty against a Standard Edition database using Statspack instead of AWR, showing its graphical Top Events, Top Segments, and Top SQL views built from a simple JDBC connection.
- [ROWNUM vs ROW_NUMBER() and 12c fetch first](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-rownum-vs-rownumber-and-12c-fetch-first/) - 2014-05-05 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Indexing, Internals & Storage; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-rownum-vs-rownumber-and-12c-fetch-first.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-rownum-vs-rownumber-and-12c-fetch-first.json)
  Evidence note: Contrasts ROWNUM and ROW_NUMBER top-n pagination plans, showing ROWNUM's stop-key lets an index range scan stop early while ROW_NUMBER requires evaluating the analytic function first, ahead of 12c's fetch-first syntax.
- [Oracle 12c CDB – metadata & object links internals](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-12c-cdb-metadata-a-object-links-internals/) - 2014-04-30 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Internals & Storage, Cloud & Infrastructure; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-12c-cdb-metadata-a-object-links-internals.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-12c-cdb-metadata-a-object-links-internals.json)
  Evidence note: Explains that 12c CDB dictionary sharing relies on metadata links for objects like DBMS packages stored only in CDB$ROOT, and object links for shared reference data like AUDIT_ACTIONS and AWR's history repositories.
- [Oracle 12c Adaptive Plan & inflection point](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-12c-adaptive-plan-inflexion-point/) - 2014-04-11 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-12c-adaptive-plan-inflexion-point.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-12c-adaptive-plan-inflexion-point.json)
  Evidence note: Clarifies that a 12c Adaptive Plan doesn't restart a join method mid-execution but defers the parse-time join-method decision until a statistics collector reads the inflection-point row count from the driving table.
- [Best practice for the sending of an Oracle execution plan](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/best-practice-to-send-an-oracle-execution-plan/) - 2014-04-08 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Query Optimization, Performance & Benchmarking, Operations & Observability, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/best-practice-to-send-an-oracle-execution-plan.json](dbi-services/articles/best-practice-to-send-an-oracle-execution-plan.json)
  Evidence note: Recommends capturing execution plans with statistics_level=all and dbms_xplan.display_cursor's allstats and peeked-binds format rather than autotrace, which doesn't reflect actual bind values or row counts.
- [The consequences of NOLOGGING in Oracle](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/the-consequences-of-nologging-in-oracle/) - 2014-03-23 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Indexing, Internals & Storage, Backup & Recovery; archive: [dbi-services/articles/the-consequences-of-nologging-in-oracle.json](dbi-services/articles/the-consequences-of-nologging-in-oracle.json)
  Evidence note: Walks through a full RMAN test case showing that restoring a backup taken before a NOLOGGING load leaves that data unrecoverable, requiring manual steps such as truncating the affected table to reach a usable state.
- [Investigating Oracle lock issues with event 10704](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/investigating-oracle-lock-issues-with-event-10704/) - 2014-03-14 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Indexing, Transactions & Consistency, Operations & Observability, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/investigating-oracle-lock-issues-with-event-10704.json](dbi-services/articles/investigating-oracle-lock-issues-with-event-10704.json)
  Evidence note: Shows tracing a delete against a parent table with event 10704 alongside 10046 to reveal locks acquired for unindexed-foreign-key referential integrity checks, explaining which trace lines matter.
- [Oracle 12c In-Memory option: waiting for 12.1.0.2](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-12c-in-memory-option-waiting-for-12102/) - 2014-03-04 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Indexing, Performance & Benchmarking, Internals & Storage, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-12c-in-memory-option-waiting-for-12102.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-12c-in-memory-option-waiting-for-12102.json)
  Evidence note: Previews the pre-release In-Memory option ahead of the 12.1.0.2 patchset, distinguishing it from prior redundant-storage features like TimesTen by its columnar design aimed at reducing CPU rather than I/O.
- [Oracle Partitioned Sequences – a future new feature in 12c?](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-partitioned-sequences-a-future-new-feature-in-12c/) - 2014-02-20 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Indexing, Performance & Benchmarking, Internals & Storage, Data Modeling & SQL; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-partitioned-sequences-a-future-new-feature-in-12c.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-partitioned-sequences-a-future-new-feature-in-12c.json)
  Evidence note: Documents an undocumented 12c sequence-partitioning syntax, discoverable via a partition-count column in DBA_SEQUENCES, aimed at easing buffer-busy-wait contention from sequence-driven primary keys.
- [Oracle et Dbvisit Replicate pour migrer sans arrêt de service… et sans stress](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-et-dbvisit-replicate-pour-migrer-sans-arret-de-service-et-sans-stress/) - 2014-02-13 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Migration & Compatibility, Events & Community; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-et-dbvisit-replicate-pour-migrer-sans-arret-de-service-et-sans-stress.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-et-dbvisit-replicate-pour-migrer-sans-arret-de-service-et-sans-stress.json)
  Evidence note: French recap of a Dbvisit webinar arguing replication-based migration reduces risk versus a classic upgrade, since it avoids needing full advance knowledge of schemas, synonyms, and triggers.
- [Oracle is hanging? Don’t forget hanganalyze and systemstate!](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/oracle-is-hanging-dont-forget-hanganalyze-and-systemstate/) - 2014-02-07 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Backup & Recovery; archive: [dbi-services/articles/oracle-is-hanging-dont-forget-hanganalyze-and-systemstate.json](dbi-services/articles/oracle-is-hanging-dont-forget-hanganalyze-and-systemstate.json)
  Evidence note: Recommends capturing oradebug hanganalyze and systemstate dumps immediately when a database appears fully hung, since a forced shutdown abort destroys the session state and ASH data needed for later root-cause analysis.
- [Archivelog deletion policy for Standby Database in Oracle Data Guard](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/archivelog-deletion-policy-for-standby-database-in-oracle-data-guard/) - 2014-01-27 | dbi services | Oracle Database | High Availability & Replication, Backup & Recovery, Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/archivelog-deletion-policy-for-standby-database-in-oracle-data-guard.json](dbi-services/articles/archivelog-deletion-policy-for-standby-database-in-oracle-data-guard.json)
  Evidence note: Shows RMAN's applied-on-all-standby deletion policy doesn't automatically make applied archivelogs reclaimable in the FRA before 12c, needing a custom query to check per-archivelog status instead of the summary view.
- [Sockets, Cores, Virtual CPU, Logical CPU, Hyper-Threading: What is a CPU nowadays?](https://www.dbi-services.com/blog/sockets-cores-virtual-cpu-logical-cpu-hyper-threading-what-is-a-cpu-nowadays-1/) - 2014-01-13 | dbi services | Oracle Database | Operations & Observability; archive: [dbi-services/articles/sockets-cores-virtual-cpu-logical-cpu-hyper-threading-what-is-a-cpu-nowadays-1.json](dbi-services/articles/sockets-cores-virtual-cpu-logical-cpu-hyper-threading-what-is-a-cpu-nowadays-1.json)
  Evidence note: Diagnoses an AIX LPAR AWR report showing only 30% CPU busy despite complaints of 4x slower response, explaining how virtual CPU and hyper-threading hide real CPU starvation behind low utilization.

## Coverage statement

- Unique publications represented: 1,361
- Archived snapshots inventoried: 1,364
- Practical playbooks: 30
- Field-manual procedures: 121
- Publication years: 2014-2026
- Deduplication rule: when a non-LinkedIn record and LinkedIn record share a canonical URL, retain the LinkedIn publication in this knowledge base.
- Classification rule: database, category, and version labels are regex-assisted discovery metadata, not claims that every paragraph applies to every listed product.
